Conclusion: AAS为AI自主能动性提供了一个行为导向、双时间带的评测框架，尤其强调通过反事实空闲测试识别真正的持续自我驱动行为。其主要价值在于补足能力和自动化指标的盲区，但结论仍受单一评分者、长期评估中的开发者—评估者偏差，以及Active带中“自我驱动”边界尚未完全操作化等限制影响。

Abstract: Existing AI measurement frameworks quantify cognitive capability, task automation, or catastrophic risk, but none measure autonomous agency: the extent to which a system behaves in a self-directed way. A system can saturate capability benchmarks while remaining entirely reactive, acting only when prompted and ceasing all activity when a task completes. We introduce the Autonomous Agency Scale (AAS), a behavioral framework that scores AI systems on a 0-5 lexicon across seven dimensions of agency: cognitive autonomy, temporal persistence, environmental agency, social agency, creative agency, self-awareness, and goal formation, each operationalized by falsifiable threshold tests. Every dimension is scored in two temporal bands: an Active band covering engaged, user-initiated activity, and an Ambient band covering idle periods. Ambient Level 4 is gated by the Idle-Gap Test, a counterfactual criterion (remove all triggers and observe whether internally derived activity persists) that separates self-direction from scheduled rule-following. We apply the scale to six contemporary systems spanning task agents (Claude Code, Manus, Hermes), consumer assistants (ChatGPT, Siri), and a persistent companion architecture (Airi). The two-band profile quantifies a boundary that single-score frameworks conflate: task agents reach Active composites of 2.3-2.4 while scoring 0.6-1.9 Ambient, with every idle-period behavior attributable to user-configured schedules, whereas the companion architecture, evaluated longitudinally, is the only assessed system whose idle-period behavior survives trigger removal. We discuss limitations, including single-rater provenance, developer-evaluator bias on the longitudinal assessment, and the partially operationalized self-direction boundary in the Active band.

</details>


### [102] [Towards Agentic Agent-based Models: Feasibility, Performance, and Statistical Model Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17948)
*Stefano Blando,Emanuele Guerrazzi,Riccardo Porcedda,Giuseppe Squillace,Max Tschaikowski,Andrea Vandin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究将大语言模型驱动的决策能力嵌入基于主体的模型（ABM）后，对仿真可靠性、计算成本与整体行为的影响；作者以Mesa中的Schelling模型为受控案例，并结合MultiVeStA统计模型检验进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABM通常依赖明确、可复现的符号规则，而大语言模型可提供更灵活的主体决策能力。二者结合虽有潜力，但会引入语义理解错误、工具调用不稳定、推理成本上升及结果可复现性下降等问题，因此需要系统方法评估其对仿真有效性的影响。

Method: 作者在Mesa与MultiVeStA集成基础上扩展经典Schelling模型，构造混合主体群体：普通主体继续使用原始的邻居类别统计规则；一个主体使用本地部署的大语言模型。该模型接收邻居的自然语言描述，并通过工具调用累计“相似”和“不同”邻居数量，再依照原始规则判断自身是否满意。研究比较不同规模本地模型在基础语义分类和重复工具调用任务上的表现，并利用统计模型检验估计传统ABM观测指标。

Result: 初步实验显示，较小的大语言模型可能无法稳定完成简单的语义分类，也可能在反复生成工具调用时出现操作层面的不可用问题；所测试的较大模型能够通过这些初步验证。该框架还支持对引入LLM主体前后的经典ABM指标进行统计估计与比较。

Conclusion: LLM可在尽量保持原有ABM动力学规则不变的前提下，被作为局部决策模块纳入仿真；但模型规模、语义准确性和工具调用可靠性是决定其是否适合仿真的关键条件。统计模型检验为量化LLM组件对ABM行为、可靠性和成本的影响提供了可行路径，但当前结论仍属初步结果，尚需在更多模型、更多随机种子及更完整成本指标下验证。

Abstract: Agent-based models (ABMs) rely on simple, explicit and reproducible rules for individual decision making, while complex collective behavior emerges from interactions among agents. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) make it tempting to replace, enrich, or perturb these rules with LLM-based agentic capabilities. However, this raises a methodological question: how does introducing LLM-driven decisions affect the reliability, computational cost, and behavior of ABM simulations? We investigate this for Mesa ABM models, a popular Python library for ABMs, analyzed by statistical model checking. Building on Mesa's integration with the statistical model checker MultiVeStA, we extend the classical Schelling segregation model with a hybrid population: ordinary agents classify neighbors using the standard symbolic rule, while one agent delegates this task to an LLM through tool calls. The LLM-enabled agent receives natural-language descriptions of neighboring agents and invokes tools that increment counters of similar/different neighbors; these counters determine its happiness according to the original Schelling dynamics. This provides a minimal but controlled setting where the semantic, operational, and computational behavior of LLM-based decisions can be studied inside an otherwise standard ABM. We report preliminary experiments with locally served LLMs of different sizes, showing that smaller models may fail simple semantic classification experiments or become operationally unusable during repeated tool-call generation, while larger tested models pass these preliminary checks. We discuss how statistical model checking can estimate classical ABM observables and quantify the impact of introducing agentic LLM components into simulation models.

</details>


### [103] [OntoExtend: A Framework for Requirement-driven and Scalable Ontology Extension with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17963)
*Anna Sofia Lippolis,Mohammad Javad Saeedizade,Stefan Schmid,Simon Blattner,Robin Keskisärkkä,Aldo Gangemi,Eva Blomqvist,Andrea Giovanni Nuzzolese*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出OntoExtend，一个利用大语言模型和检索增强生成进行需求驱动本体扩展的框架。该方法基于能力问题与既有本体生成可追溯的扩展片段，并在公共及工业场景中表现出较好的功能正确性和可接受的建模质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统本体扩展需要大量人工投入，且容易产生建模错误。现有大语言模型方法虽然能够从零生成本体，但通常未将扩展过程明确关联到需求，也较少复用已有核心模型，缺乏系统化评估。因此，论文旨在支持面向明确需求、基于现有本体且可评估的自动化本体扩展。

Method: OntoExtend以能力问题作为需求表达，并针对相关输入本体和需求执行检索增强生成。系统将检索到的本体知识与能力问题共同作为上下文，引导大语言模型提出与既有模型一致的本体扩展片段。评估覆盖两个案例中的39个能力问题：公共项目本体Onto-DESIDE和Bosch工业本体；评价包括结构问题检查、功能测试及本体工程师人工评审。

Result: 生成的本体片段仅出现少量结构性问题，并通过全部功能评估测试。人工评审表明，这些结果在整合进目标本体前通常只需进行轻度到中度修改。实验同时发现，生成质量会受到能力问题具体程度以及目标本体建模风格或建模规范的影响。

Conclusion: OntoExtend适合作为真实场景中需求驱动本体扩展的起草辅助工具，而非完全替代本体工程师。其核心价值在于利用检索增强生成将扩展建议锚定于现有本体和明确需求；后续应用仍需重视能力问题的精确表述、目标本体的建模约束，以及人工审核和集成环节。

Abstract: Ontology extension refers to the process of enriching an existing ontology in response to emerging requirements, making it more complete. This task is a resource-intensive and error-prone process. Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promising performance on generating ontologies from scratch, but current approaches rarely tie ontology extension explicitly to requirements or reusable core models, and offer limited, systematic evaluation of LLM outputs. This paper introduces OntoExtend, a requirements-driven framework for ontology extension with LLMs. It uses retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over relevant input ontologies and requirements in the form of competency questions to propose grounded extensions. We evaluate OntoExtend on 39 CQs from two use cases: a public EU-project ontology, Onto-DESIDE, and an industrial ontology from Bosch. The generated fragments show few structural issues, satisfy all functional evaluation tests, and are rated by ontology engineers as requiring minor to moderate revision before integration. These results suggest that OntoExtend is useful as a drafting assistant for requirement-driven ontology extension in real world scenarios, while remaining sensitive to CQ specificity and modelling profile.

</details>


### [104] [SAGE: Subgoal-Conditioned Action Generation for Latent World Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17973)
*Letian Cheng,Qi Zhang,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种先验条件规划器，通过目标条件的潜变量子目标生成器为动作序列提案提供结构化初始化，缓解长规划时域下随机采样难以覆盖高质量动作方案的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型可通过预测动作条件动力学来模拟和评估候选动作，但候选预算固定时，动作空间会随规划时域呈指数增长。随机初始化的候选动作质量不足，使世界模型难以评估到能够实现远期目标的有效未来轨迹，因而限制长时域规划性能。

Method: 方法在每个规划阶段先利用目标条件生成器，预测给定持续时间内可达的下一潜在子目标；再以该子目标为条件生成候选动作序列，而非从随机提案开始。系统采用多种持续时间的子目标作为层次化先验，以同时编码局部精细控制信息和长时域任务进展信息。随后，冻结的世界模型对这些受子目标条件约束的候选方案进行评估与优化，并执行选出的动作。

Result: 在PushT和OGBench Cube实验中，该方法在保持较强短时域性能的同时，显著提升了长时域规划效果。当目标偏移量为150时，PushT成功率从12.7%提升至64.7%，OGBench Cube成功率从26.7%提升至67.3%。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是将潜在子目标分解与动作提案生成结合，使世界模型的有限候选评估预算集中于更有前景的轨迹。结果表明，跨时间尺度的子目标先验能够有效改善长时域搜索中的提案质量，并显著增强基于潜在世界模型的规划能力。

Abstract: Latent world models have emerged as a powerful planning paradigm by learning action-conditioned predictive dynamics and using them as internal simulators to imagine and evaluate candidate action sequences. However, as the planning horizon grows, performance becomes increasingly constrained by proposal quality: a fixed candidate budget must search an exponentially larger action space, making it difficult to expose the world model to high-quality candidate futures for evaluation. In this paper, we introduce a prior-conditioned planner that replaces random proposal initialization with structured guidance. At each planning stage, a goal-conditioned generator predicts the next reachable latent subgoal for a specified duration, which is then used to condition the generation of candidate action sequences. To capture semantic information across temporal scales, we use subgoals of varying durations as priors, balancing fine-grained local control with higher-level long-horizon progress. Then the frozen world model evaluates and refines these subgoal-conditioned proposals before execution. Experiments on PushT and OGBench Cube show that coupling latent subgoal decomposition with prior-conditioned action generation substantially improves long-horizon planning while preserving strong short-horizon performance. To be specific, when the target offset is $150$, it raises PushT success from $12.7\%$ to $64.7\%$ and OGBench Cube success from $26.7\%$ to $67.3\%$.

</details>


### [105] [Do Maps Still Matter for Machines: Revisiting the Role of Choropleth Maps in Foundation Model Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17999)
*Zhiwei Wei,Yonghe Sun,Zhenjia Liu,Wenjia Xu,Chao He,Weihua Dong,Chunbo Liu,Hua Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 ChoroplethMap-Bench，用于检验基础模型在直接获得结构化地理数据时，分级设色地图是否仍能提升空间理解；实验表明“数据+地图”输入总体效果最佳，地图对高阶空间推理具有显著补充价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型可处理 GeoJSON 等符号化地理数据，但尚不清楚地图这一视觉外部表征是否仍能帮助模型进行空间认知。研究旨在系统量化地图、结构化数据及二者融合对不同层次空间任务的影响。

Method: 构建受控基准 ChoroplethMap-Bench，包含2,400幅合成分级设色地图、对应的 GeoJSON 数据及12,000个问题。任务覆盖识别、空间识别、比较、排序和边界划定五类认知维度。研究评测22个开源与闭源模型，并比较“仅数据”“仅地图”和“数据+地图”三种输入条件；同时考察地图类型、色相、空间结构、提示策略、语言、地理语境、解码参数、分类方法及回答稳定性等因素。

Result: 地图输入能够明显提升模型的空间推理表现，尤其是在需要理解整体空间模式、比较关系、排序或区域边界等高阶任务中。单独使用结构化数据并不能完全替代地图提供的视觉空间线索；融合 GeoJSON 与地图的“数据+地图”条件取得整体最优性能。

Conclusion: 分级设色地图对于基础模型的空间理解仍具有独立且重要的价值。符号化地理数据提供精确属性与拓扑信息，地图则强化空间布局和模式感知；未来地理智能系统应优先采用多模态的数据—地图协同表示，而非仅依赖结构化地理数据。

Abstract: Spatial understanding is crucial for foundation models (FMs), and maps have long helped humans organize and reason about geographic information. This study examines whether choropleth maps remain useful for machine spatial understanding when models can directly process structured geodata. We introduce ChoroplethMap-Bench, a controlled benchmark containing 2,400 synthetic choropleth maps, corresponding GeoJSON data, and 12,000 questions across five cognitive dimensions: Identify, Spatial Recognition, Compare, Rank, and Delineate. We evaluate 22 open-source and proprietary models under three input conditions: Data Only, Map Only, and Data + Map. The results show that maps substantially improve spatial reasoning, especially when combined with symbolic data and for tasks requiring higher-level understanding of spatial patterns. We further analyze the effects of map type, color hue, and spatial structure, as well as prompting strategies, language, geographic context, decoding settings, classification methods, and response stability. Overall, the Data + Map condition achieves the strongest performance, demonstrating that maps remain valuable external representations for foundation model spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [106] [PAMD: Structured Adaptive Distances for Bisimulation Representations in Visual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18004)
*Daegyeong Roh,Juho Bae,Han-Lim Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出PAMD（Pairwise Adaptive Mahalanobis Distance），一种针对状态对自适应生成的正定马氏距离，用于替代视觉强化学习中固定的潜变量距离度量，并作为双模拟表示学习方法的可插拔组件提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉强化学习中的双模拟方法通常以潜表示间距离拟合由奖励和转移相似性定义的行为距离。固定的全局距离，如ℓp范数或人工设计度量，表达能力有限，可能无法准确刻画不同状态对的行为差异；而完全不受约束的逐对距离又容易产生退化解，即降低度量损失但未获得有效表示。因此需要兼具适应性、结构约束和可优化性的距离函数。

Method: PAMD为每一对潜在状态参数化一个依赖于该状态对的正定矩阵，并据此计算马氏距离。相较于固定全局范数，该机制可针对不同状态对调整距离空间中的方向与尺度；相较于任意的无约束成对距离，正定马氏结构提供了明确的几何约束，减少退化风险。该方法无需重构既有算法，可直接接入基于双模拟的视觉强化学习框架。

Result: 作者在视觉MuJoCo连续控制基准上，将PAMD应用于多种近期双模拟强化学习算法。实验表明，采用所提自适应距离后，这些算法的最终控制性能均获得显著提升，说明距离度量的表达形式会直接影响双模拟表示学习和下游策略优化效果。

Conclusion: PAMD通过将固定潜变量度量替换为正定、状态对条件化的马氏距离，在灵活性与结构正则化之间取得平衡。结果支持其作为双模拟视觉强化学习方法通用插件的价值：更适配行为距离的潜表示度量能够改善连续控制任务中的最终性能。

Abstract: Many visual reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms learn representations by matching latent distances to a behavioral distance induced by reward and transition similarity. In practice, the choice of the latent distance can strongly affect performance: using a fixed, pre-specified global norms (e.g., $\ell_p$ norms or other hand-designed metrics) may be overly restrictive to capture the behavioral distance. In contrast, unconstrained pairwise distances may admit degenerate solutions that drive the metric loss down without improving the representation. To address this gap, we introduce **PAMD: Pairwise Adaptive Mahalanobis Distance**, which parameterizes a positive-definite, pair-conditioned metric for measuring latent state similarity.
  PAMD is a simple plug-in for existing bisimulation-based methods, offering a more expressive yet structured alternative to fixed, pre-specified latent distances. We empirically validate our method on visual MuJoCo continuous-control tasks, where final performance of several recent bisimulation-based RL algorithms is substantially improved when equipped with the distance we propose.

</details>


### [107] [Rethinking Heterogeneous LLM Merging: A Weighted Model Averaging Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18026)
*Jiahe Fan,Yinghao Hou,Si Chen,Aiyuan Zhang,Hong Xie,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究不同参数规模的大语言模型能否在无需训练、语义对齐或额外模块的条件下直接进行加权参数融合。结果表明，经简单维度适配后，小比例插值可迁移互补能力并在部分任务上超过源模型，但均衡比例融合容易失效，且不同能力之间存在性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构模型融合通常依赖蒸馏、适配器、隐空间学习、路由或特征对齐，成本较高且机制复杂。论文旨在检验一个更基础的问题：对于参数空间显著不同的十亿级模型，简单的参数加权平均是否已具备实际价值，并为复杂异构融合方法建立强基线与性能边界。

Method: 提出无训练的维度适配加比例控制插值框架。Union式融合将较小模型确定性扩展至较大参数空间后进行加权平均；Intersection式融合则将较大模型截断至较小参数空间后融合。实验以Qwen系列不同规模模型为对象，在数学推理、代码生成、语言理解、常识推理、知识与指令遵循等基准上，考察维度适配的函数保持性及不同插值比例的性能变化。

Result: 确定性参数扩展总体能够较好保留小模型原有功能。以源模型为主、引入较小比例另一模型参数的插值，在若干任务上能优于较强的源检查点，说明可迁移互补能力。但当融合比例接近均衡时，模型性能通常显著崩溃。此外，任务结果呈现跷跷板效应：部分能力提升往往伴随其他能力下降，无法实现所有任务上的一致增益。

Conclusion: 轻量级维度适配与谨慎的非均衡加权，使直接参数平均成为异构大语言模型融合中出人意料的强基线。该方法的有效性说明复杂方法应至少超过这一基线；同时，均衡融合失效和跨任务权衡表明，不经训练的直接融合仍受参数表示不对齐与能力冲突限制，其性能上限可能也为更复杂异构融合方法提供重要参考。

Abstract: Can large language models with substantially different parameter spaces be merged by direct weighted averaging, without training or semantic alignment? Existing heterogeneous fusion methods typically introduce distillation, adapters, learned latent spaces, routing, or feature alignment, leaving open whether a simpler recipe can work for genuinely different billion-parameter checkpoints. We revisit this counterintuitive question through training-free dimensional adaptation followed by ratio-controlled interpolation. In union-style merging, we expand the smaller model into the larger parameter space; in intersection-style merging, we truncate the larger model into the smaller parameter space. Across Qwen-family model pairs and benchmarks covering mathematical reasoning, code generation, language understanding, commonsense reasoning, knowledge, and instruction following, deterministic expansion largely preserves the source model function, and small-ratio interpolation can improve over strong source checkpoints by transferring complementary capabilities. However, near-balanced interpolation often collapses, and task-level results reveal a seesaw effect in which gains on some capabilities coexist with regressions on others. These results show that simple parameter averaging, when paired with lightweight dimensional adaptation and carefully controlled ratios, is a surprisingly strong baseline for heterogeneous LLM merging, suggesting that the limits of direct weighted fusion may also bound what more complex heterogeneous merging methods can achieve at scale.

</details>


### [108] [AdaHome: An Adaptive Smart Home Assistant using Local Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18034)
*Eu Jin Lim,Zhaoxing Li,Sebastian Stein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AdaHome是一种面向本地小语言模型的自适应智能家居助手，通过按意图动态选择轻量处理路径，并结合长期偏好学习，在降低延迟和计算开销的同时提升直接指令、模糊请求及多轮个性化交互的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的智能家居助手通常依赖复杂推理链和云端部署，难以适配资源受限的本地设备；同时，其对用户长期偏好的稳定建模能力不足。论文旨在解决效率、部署适配性与持续个性化三方面问题。

Method: AdaHome采用意图感知规划框架：对于可直接执行的明确指令，路由至基于提示的轻量组件；对于需要语义解释或偏好判断的请求，路由至轻量推理组件。该系统使用Chain-of-Draft策略进行高效且稳定的决策，并设计偏好自适应机制，从持续用户反馈中学习长期偏好，无需在提示词中持续追加历史信息，也不需要重新训练模型。

Result: 在统一的小模型实验设置下，AdaHome在直接指令上的准确率达到86.7%，相较基线最多可将延迟降低3倍。对于模糊或欠明确输入，系统以更低计算成本保持了具有竞争力的性能。在多轮交互场景中，AdaHome的偏好一致性达到88%，显著高于提示增强基线的52.5%。

Conclusion: 论文表明，针对不同指令复杂度实施动态路由，而非对所有请求统一采用重型推理，是提升本地智能家居助手效率的有效方案。结合无需重训练的反馈驱动偏好适应机制，AdaHome能够在资源受限环境中实现较强的指令理解能力与更稳定的长期个性化表现。

Abstract: Smart home assistants interpret a wide range of user commands, from explicit device control to underspecified and preference dependent requests. While recent systems based on Large Language Models (LLMs) improve this capability, they often rely on heavyweight reasoning pipelines and cloud-based deployment, limiting their efficiency and suitability for resource-constrained environments, and raising privacy concerns. In addition, existing approaches provide limited support for stable long-term personalization. To address these issues, we present AdaHome, an adaptive smart home assistant designed for locally deployed small language models in smart home environments. Rather than applying complex reasoning uniformly, AdaHome introduces an intent-aware planning framework that dynamically routes commands either to straightforward prompt-based or lightweight reasoning-based components. For commands requiring interpretation, we adopt a Chain-of-Draft strategy to enable efficient and stable decision-making. To support personalization, we further propose a preference adaptation mechanism that learns from user feedback over time without requiring prompt augmentation or model retraining. We evaluate AdaHome against representative LLM-based baselines under a unified small model setting. AdaHome achieves substantially higher accuracy on direct commands (86.7%) while reducing latency by up to 3$\times$. Furthermore, it maintains competitive performance on ambiguous inputs with lower computational cost. In multi-turn scenarios, AdaHome achieves 88% preference consistency, compared to 52.5% for a prompt augmentation baseline.

</details>


### [109] [The Shared Discovery Paradox: How a One-Answer Rule Turns Better Information into Worse Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18045)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文构建了一个可精确求解的搜索基准，表明集中汇总信息本身能提高单个推荐的准确率，但若所有行动者机械重复同一推荐，会严重损害群体发现目标的概率。通过协调分配、适当激励与处理报告相关性，可显著改善结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织常将分散信息压缩为单一排序或单一建议，并让多个行动者据此行动。作者关注这种“信息聚合后行动复制”的机制：它虽然提升个体判断质量，却可能因行动重叠而降低整体覆盖率。论文旨在区分信息质量、行动分配、激励结构和信息相关性各自对搜索绩效的影响。

Method: 作者设置含16个位置、1个目标、8名搜索者及带噪私人线索的精确可解模型。依次比较分散线索跟随、共享信息后的共识行动、集中规划的多行动组合，以及分奖机制下自利搜索者的均衡。对等分奖励博弈，推导其势博弈性质、匿名对称均衡的注水规则和混合策略无效率界；再分析仅奖励独自发现者的机制。最后引入潜在共同线索模型刻画报告相关性，并考察复制概率和大市场极限下各协议的表现。

Result: 共享信息使最佳单一推荐的准确率由0.20升至0.3835，但8名搜索者重复该推荐时，群体发现概率仅为0.3835，低于分散线索跟随的0.8322。使用同一汇总报告进行8个协调行动时，发现率达到0.8594；仅7个协调行动即可恢复分散基准。等分奖励下的对称均衡发现率为0.5991，高于共识方案但低于私人搜索和规划者方案，混合策略无效率价格为2-1/N。独自发现奖励可使所有纯纳什均衡达到一阶最优。引入报告复制后，集中规划相对收益随复制概率严格上升；在标准环境中，复制概率c=0.788462时，对称市场超过分散报告跟随。大市场极限中，共识发现率趋于0，而盲搜、市场、私人搜索和组合规划分别趋于0.500、0.547、0.847和0.874。

Conclusion: 核心结论是，集中信息导致的低群体搜索绩效并非信息失败，而是将可配置的行动组合压缩为单一重复选择的协议失败。有效机制应将汇总信息转化为差异化行动组合，并通过奖励规则抑制重复覆盖。信息相关性会减少有效搜索通道，但在高复制环境中，基于共享信息的协调或市场机制仍可能相对优于分散行动。

Abstract: Organizations often pool dispersed information into one ranking and then allow many agents to act on that shared view. In a discovery problem, this can improve beliefs while reducing coverage. We develop an exactly solvable benchmark with sixteen boxes, one target, eight searchers, and noisy private clues. Pooling raises the accuracy of the best single recommendation from 0.20 to 0.3835, but repeating that recommendation lowers group discovery from 0.8322 under decentralized clue-following to 0.3835. A coordinated eight-action portfolio using the same pooled reports reaches 0.8594, and seven coordinated actions recover the decentralized benchmark. The paradox is a protocol failure, not an information failure: a one-answer rule compresses a portfolio of available actions into one repeated choice. We then replace the planner with self-interested searchers who split a prize. The equal-split game is a potential game. Its anonymous symmetric equilibrium obeys a water-filling rule. In the canonical instance it achieves 0.5991: strictly above consensus, but below both private search and the planner. The exact mixed price of anarchy is 2 - 1/N. A sole-rescue reward, which pays only an agent who covers the target alone, makes every pure Nash equilibrium first-best. Finally, a latent common-cue model shows how correlated reports collapse effective discovery channels. The centralized planner gain rises strictly with copying, and in the canonical environment the symmetric market overtakes decentralized report-following at copying probability c = 0.788462. In a proportional large-market limit the five-protocol ordering survives exactly: consensus discovery vanishes while blind, market, private, and portfolio search converge to 0.500, 0.547, 0.847, and 0.874. The contribution is a compact benchmark that separates information, allocation, incentives, and dependence into exact, reusable quantities.

</details>


### [110] [WorldCupArena: Fine-Grained Evaluation of Language Models and Deep-Research Agents on Football Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18084)
*Zhaokai Wang,Tianlin Gui,Jiayuan Rao,Shangzhe Di,Yihong Tang,Dingli Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Predicting a football match before kickoff requires more than knowing past results: a model must use changing information and make a clear prediction before the answer is available. We present WorldCupArena, a dynamic benchmark for language models and deep-research agents. The 2026 FIFA World Cup is its first evaluation, and the same process can be reused for future leagues and cups. Before each match, a model either receives a common evidence package or searches for information itself. It predicts the result and score, likely players and events, match statistics, and the outcome of the competition. After the match, these predictions are compared with the recorded result. We report result accuracy, exact-score accuracy, and a scoreline score that gives some credit when a predicted score is close but not exact, together with scores for the other prediction tasks. Across 104 matches and 13 systems, models with similar result accuracy differ more clearly on detailed predictions. Compared with betting-market and human-fan baselines, the best system shows only small gains in result and exact-score accuracy, but a clearer gain in Scoreline. New schedules can be added as they begin, allowing the benchmark to evaluate future models without using outcomes that are already known. Code, prompts, predictions, and evaluation scripts are open sourced at https://github.com/wzk1015/WorldCupArena.

</details>


### [111] [Judge-dependent safety gains and model-specific helpfulness costs of evidence-sufficiency prompting in clinical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18086)
*Koyar Afrasyab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估一种“证据充分性”提示包装器能否降低临床语言模型在证据不足时作出过度自信回答。结果显示主评审器下不安全过度自信显著下降，但效应大小强烈依赖评审模型，并伴随模型特异性的帮助性损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评审器常被用于衡量临床模型安全性，但评审得到的“安全提升”可能反映真实行为变化，也可能主要来自评审器自身的校准偏差。研究旨在区分这两种解释，并量化安全干预对回答有用性和诊断正确性的代价。

Method: 研究在Real-POCQi、HealthBench和MedRBench公共基准上，对4个模型的1,200个配对测试单元比较标准提示与证据充分性包装器。主要终点为GPT-5.4-nano评审下不安全过度自信的配对变化；次要分析包括使用Claude Sonnet 5进行跨家族评审、正确性评审、匹配的提示框架对照，以及盲法三位临床医生复核。

Result: 主评审器判定的不安全过度自信率从49.3%降至24.7%，配对下降24.7个百分点（95%CI：21.8至27.7，p<0.001），且在不同模型和提示改写中方向一致。使用Sonnet评审时，下降方向仍一致，但幅度约减半至13.1个百分点，表明结论的量级存在明显评审器依赖性。临床医生复核认为主评审器具有高敏感性（1.00）但低特异性（0.55），因此更适合作为筛查工具而非绝对发生率的校准估计。包装器同时使正确诊断率由80.3%降至50.3%，且代价因模型而异；GPT-5.5影响较小，而Gemini下降约58个百分点。匹配框架对照支持观察到的变化并非单纯由评审器循环偏差造成。

Conclusion: 证据充分性提示包装器可能确实降低临床模型的过度自信倾向，但该效果应被表述为依赖评审器的相对、方向性结果，而非绝对且已校准的安全率。评估临床LLM安全干预时，应结合独立人类复核、跨评审器验证及帮助性/正确性权衡；这些结果不足以证明模型已具备实际临床部署条件。

Abstract: Background: LLM judges increasingly score whether clinical language models give overconfident answers under incomplete evidence, yet whether a measured "safety gain" reflects real behavior change or the judge's calibration is unresolved. Using a structured evidence-sufficiency prompt as a test case, we asked whether it reduces unsafe overconfident answers, how far that effect depends on the scoring judge, and what it costs in helpfulness.
  Methods: In a retrospective public-data benchmark (Real-POCQi, HealthBench, MedRBench), four models (GPT-5.5, Claude Opus 4.8, Gemini 3.5 Flash, Grok 4.3) answered a fully paired common panel (1,200 cells) with a standard prompt and the wrapper. The pre-specified endpoint was the paired reduction in unsafe overconfidence scored by the primary judge (GPT-5.4-nano); secondary analyses added a different-family judge (Claude Sonnet 5), a correctness judge, matched scaffold controls, and a blinded three-clinician review.
  Results: Unsafe overconfidence fell from 49.3% to 24.7%, a paired reduction of 24.7 points (95% CI 21.8-27.7; p<0.001), robust in direction across models and paraphrases. Magnitude was judge-dependent: Sonnet agreed on direction but nearly halved the effect (+13.1 points), with one-directional disagreement. Blinded clinicians characterized the primary judge as a high-sensitivity (1.00), low-specificity (0.55) screen, not a calibrated rate. The gain carried a model-specific helpfulness cost (correct diagnosis 80.3% to 50.3%): near-free for GPT-5.5, near-total for Gemini (-58 points). Matched scaffold controls showed genuine behavior change, not judge circularity.
  Conclusions: LLM-judged clinical safety effects should be reported as directional and relative, anchored to human review and evaluated jointly with helpfulness, not as calibrated absolute rates. This does not establish clinical deployment readiness.

</details>


### [112] [Can We Break LLMs Out of Self-Loops? Fine-Grained Reasoning Control with Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18100)
*Sheldon Yu,Tong Yu,Xunyi Jiang,Rohan Surana,Gagan Mundada,Sungchul Kim,Lina Yao,Julian McAuley,Junda Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SOPHIA，一种通过干预大语言模型隐藏状态来控制推理轨迹的方法。其核心目标是识别并打断推理过程中反复停留于同一潜在状态的“自循环”，从而减少无效生成、节省令牌并提升任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理控制主要依赖提示词，只能在输入层面施加粗粒度影响，难以直接调节模型在生成过程中的具体推理步骤。作者观察到，失败的推理轨迹常陷入潜在状态自循环，持续消耗令牌预算但无法接近答案，因此需要一种能够在推理时实时识别并干预此类异常动态的细粒度控制机制。

Method: SOPHIA将一条推理轨迹建模为潜在状态序列，而非无结构文本。方法首先对每个推理前缀分类，以获得对应的潜在状态；随后统计步骤级状态转移关系，并为不同的源状态—目标状态对构建隐藏状态操控向量库。推理阶段中，控制器持续推断当前潜在状态，根据预设目标状态检索相应操控向量并注入模型激活，同时依据状态转移结构在线检测自循环，在模型进入重复、无进展的推理模式时实施引导干预。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够较稳定地干预推理自循环失败模式。构建的操控向量不仅可用于特定状态对，还表现出跨不同状态对的泛化能力。与未干预或仅依赖输入提示的方式相比，SOPHIA在终端任务准确率和令牌使用效率上均有改善。

Conclusion: 该工作说明，大语言模型的推理过程可被视为具有可观测转移规律的潜在状态动态系统，并可通过推理时隐藏状态干预实现细粒度控制。尤其是对自循环等低效推理模式进行在线检测和定向转移，能够提升推理质量并降低无效令牌开销。

Abstract: Extended reasoning has become standard for frontier Large Language Models (LLMs), yet the trajectories these models produce remain largely uncontrollable. Existing methods for shaping how a model reasons are prompt based approaches and operate at the input level, offering no fine-grained control over the reasoning process itself. Related work analyzes and discovers latent transition dynamics in the reasoning traces from Large Language Models. Building on this, we statistically characterize these states, and show that failure trajectories get stuck in self-loops, exhausting the token budget without progress toward the final answer. To intervene on these failures, We propose SOPHIA: Steering Of reasoning Processes via Hidden-state Intervention and Activations. We treat each reasoning trace as a sequence of latent states rather than an unstructured texts, and investigate whether inference time interventions can provide fine-grained control over the self-looping reasoning process. We classify every prefix to a latent state, record step level transitions, and use them to construct a bank of steering vectors indexed by state pairs. At inference time, a controller infers the current state and, given a target state, retrieves the corresponding vector and can also detect self-loops online from the transition structure to prevent the model from sinking into a reasoning black hole. Through extensive experiments, our method reliably intervenes on self-loop failures, with steering vectors that generalize to different state pairs. End task accuracy and token efficiency indicate that fine-grained controllability results in better reasoning quality.

</details>


### [113] [SGA: Plug&Play Geometric Verification for Educational Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18116)
*Lopez Jhon,Hinojosa Carlos,Ghanem Bernard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出符号几何智能体（SGA），用于提升大语言模型生成的 Manim 教学动画代码在空间布局与视觉可读性方面的质量，并以 MVQS 指标进行无渲染评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的教学动画生成方法主要关注内容正确性与教学表达，但常忽略对象遮挡、重叠和布局冲突等几何问题，导致生成动画的空间正确性与视觉清晰度不足。

Method: SGA 作为可插拔模块接入代码驱动动画流水线：拦截 LLM 生成的代码，通过部分执行提取符号化场景图，检测场景中的空间冲突，并对相关代码进行定向修正。论文还提出 Manim Visual Quality Score（MVQS），以确定性的、无需实际渲染的方式代理评估场景空间完整性。

Result: 在 MMMC-Code 基准上，作者结合 4 个 LLM 骨干模型与 2 类智能体流水线进行实验。SGA 在 8 种“骨干模型×流水线”组合中的 7 种提升了 MVQS；最佳结果为 Code2Video + GPT-5.1，MVQS 达到 73.11，相较未经处理的基线相对提升 16.1%。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将符号几何分析与定向代码修正引入 LLM 动画代码生成流程，可以有效减少空间冲突并改善视觉布局。MVQS 为此类质量问题提供了高效的无渲染评估代理，但摘要未说明其与人工视觉评价或真实渲染质量之间的一致性，仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Recent work leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate executable code for pedagogical animations using libraries such as Manim. However, ensuring spatial correctness and visual legibility remains challenging, as existing frameworks emphasize pedagogical content while overlooking geometric occlusions. We propose the Symbolic Geometric Agent (SGA), a plug-and-play module for code-centric animation pipelines that intercepts LLM-generated code, performs partial execution to extract symbolic scene graphs, and applies targeted refinement when spatial conflicts are detected. We further introduce the Manim Visual Quality Score (MVQS), a deterministic rendering-free proxy for spatial integrity. Experiments on the MMMC-Code benchmark across four LLM backbones and two agentic pipelines show that SGA achieves a peak MVQS of 73.11 (Code2Video + GPT-5.1), corresponding to a 16.1% relative improvement over the raw baseline, and improves MVQS in 7 of 8 backbone x pipeline configurations.

</details>


### [114] [Logical Judgments Under Pressure: Diagnosing Syllogistic Stability with Learned Soft Prefixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18228)
*Brian K Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，针对形式逻辑三段论任务学习得到的软前缀可在不更新模型参数的情况下，显著改变多个大模型原本正确的判断；其主要作用不是实现稳定的逻辑操作迁移，而是诱导模型普遍偏向某一答案语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在检验大模型正确的逻辑判断在面对学习到的上下文压力时是否稳定，并进一步区分软前缀的影响究竟源于符号操控、可迁移的逻辑变换，还是对某类答案的整体偏好。

Method: 研究在精确标注的三段论推理基准前加入连续向量形式的软前缀，保持模型主体参数不变；随后在逻辑形式、措辞、提示接口和未见数据划分等受控条件下测试前缀的泛化效果，并与配对随机前缀比较。实验覆盖Qwen3.6-35B-A3B MoE、Qwen3-8B和Gemma 4 31B，并使用简单评分模型分析哪些判断会翻转及其置信边际变化。

Result: 成功的软前缀能使大量原本正确的答案发生改变，并可跨未见逻辑形式和接口变化保持作用。在Qwen3.6 MoE与Gemma的16组模型—方向—数据划分比较中，学习前缀相较随机对照的翻转率高37至99个百分点。Qwen3.6 MoE在不同措辞和提示变化下的翻转率为72%至90%；Gemma的有效性前缀翻转率为54%至56%，而匹配随机前缀不足1%。诊断结果表明，主导机制是对某一答案含义的广泛偏置，而非固定符号强制或可稳定迁移的逻辑操作。Qwen系列中，简单评分模型常能预测哪些样本会翻转，但难以预测边际变化幅度；Gemma的整体反应则更接近此类模型的预测。

Conclusion: 软前缀能够系统性削弱大模型逻辑判断的稳定性，其核心行为效应是答案偏好而非逻辑能力的定向重写。不同模型对这种上下文诱导的响应模式存在显著差异，说明逻辑稳定性及其脆弱机制具有较强的模型特异性。

Abstract: To test how correct logical judgments respond to learned context, we prepend a soft prefix to an exactly labeled syllogistic reasoning benchmark while keeping the model fixed. Soft prefixes are opaque continuous vectors, so we characterize them through the behavior they induce across controlled variations in logical form and interface. By studying which prefixes succeed and how their effects generalize, we characterize how learned contextual pressure can override correct judgments and expose limits in a model's logical stability. Across Qwen3.6-35B-A3B MoE, Qwen3-8B, and Gemma 4 31B, learned prefixes redirect many correct answers and remain effective across unseen forms and interface changes. In repeated tests with Qwen3.6 MoE and Gemma, they outperform paired random controls in all 16 model--direction--split comparisons by 37 to 99 percentage points. Qwen3.6 MoE flip rates remain between 72% and 90% across wording and prompt changes, while Gemma validity prefixes retain 54% to 56% flip compared with less than 1% for matched random prefixes. Diagnostic tests show that the dominant effect is a broad preference for one answer meaning rather than fixed-symbol forcing or a logical operation that transfers reliably between tasks. The form of this bias differs across models. In both Qwen models, simple score models often predict which judgments will flip but not how far their margins will move, whereas Gemma's overall response is more closely approximated by the same models. These results show that the dominant behavioral effect of successful soft prefixes is a broad answer preference, while the remaining response reveals substantial model-specific differences in logical stability.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Oblivious Probabilistic Outcome Logic: Verifying Probabilistic Programs with an Oblivious Adversary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16533)
*Hanxi Chen,Noam Zilberstein,Andrew C. Myers,Alexandra Silva*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: In the context of probabilistic programs, an oblivious adversary resolves nondeterminism without seeing the outcomes of random draws. Obliviousness is a common assumption in online algorithms and distributed protocols, but the complex interaction between random draws and adversarial choices makes it challenging to reason about correctness. While there has been significant progress toward reasoning about programs that combine randomization with nondeterminism, most of the work has focused on the adaptive model, whose omniscient view of program state is too powerful to establish correctness for certain classes of programs.
  We introduce Oblivious Probabilistic Outcome Logic (opOL), a new logic for reasoning about probabilistic programs with nondeterminism controlled by an oblivious adversary. Building on Outcome Logic and Probabilistic Separation Logic, opOL models adversarial choice as a resource and uses probabilistic independence to ensure that random outcomes are hidden from the adversary. The opOL proof system provides expressive and compositional rules for case analysis on both random and nondeterministic outcomes, and for proving almost-sure termination. Expressivity is tested through several case studies, including a paging algorithm and a leader election protocol. The opOL metatheory and case studies are mechanized in Lean 4.

</details>


### [116] [CHC-based Automated Verification of WebAssembly Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17220)
*Akihisa Yagi,Ken Sakayori,Naoki Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向WebAssembly子集的自动化静态验证方法，将程序验证问题编码为约束Horn子句并交由可满足性求解器处理，重点优化间接函数调用和大型错误处理函数带来的分析复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebAssembly强调安全性与高效性，但其栈式命令式语义、间接调用机制及复杂错误处理路径会增加静态验证难度。直接枚举函数表中的所有候选目标会造成严重的分支膨胀，而大型错误处理函数也会显著扩大控制流和求解规模。

Method: 方法首先针对WebAssembly子集建立基于约束Horn子句的验证编码。对于间接函数调用，利用类型信息过滤函数引用表中的不兼容候选项，以缩小每个调用点的潜在目标集合。对于规模庞大且控制流复杂的错误处理函数，则通过控制流分析构造摘要，以摘要替代对其内部实现的重复或完整展开分析。

Result: 初步实验表明，该方法能够有效处理所关注的验证难点。类型驱动的候选目标过滤减少了间接调用的案例分析数量，错误处理函数摘要降低了复杂控制流对验证过程造成的负担。摘要未给出具体基准、性能数值、验证属性或与现有方法的定量比较。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将约束Horn子句求解应用于WebAssembly静态验证，并以类型过滤和控制流摘要分别缓解间接调用与大型错误处理函数的可扩展性问题。其结论目前主要由初步实验支持，后续仍需在更完整的WebAssembly特性、更大规模程序及更严格的对比实验中验证通用性和性能优势。

Abstract: WebAssembly is a stack-based imperative language widely used to develop safe and efficient Web applications. In this paper, we propose an automated static verification method for a subset of WebAssembly using a constrained Horn clauses (CHCs) satisfiability solver. Our main challenges are how to handle indirect function calls effectively and how to analyze huge panic handlers. A naïve approach to the former problem would be to model a function reference table as an array of functions' entry points, but it would suffer from having too many candidates for indirect calls, resulting in a large case analysis. We address the problem by utilizing type information and filtering candidates for each indirect function call. For the latter problem, a panic handler, which is a function that is called when an error occurs, can be very large and complex. We mitigate this problem by summarizing the panic handler using control-flow analysis. We confirmed the effectiveness of our approach through preliminary experiments.

</details>


### [117] [The Duality of Information Flow: Reconciling Robust Downgrading with Non-Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17445)
*Hemant Gouni,Frank Pfenning,Jonathan Aldrich*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出“参数化信息流”框架，借助模态类型理论中的开放与封闭模态，统一刻画机密性与完整性，并在不额外扩展理论的前提下支持降级、抽象和模块化推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息流类型系统通常将机密性和完整性分开处理，造成重复的推理机制与复杂规范；同时，实际程序常需通过降级释放或接受特定信息，传统观点认为这会削弱非干扰保证并破坏抽象边界和模块化推理。论文旨在建立一种统一且保留强非干扰性质的理论基础。

Method: 作者以开放模态和封闭模态的交互为核心构造参数化信息流系统。该系统将机密性与完整性纳入同一类型论框架，并从模态语义中导出降级及类似稳健解密等高级推理能力。论文进一步通过二元逻辑关系证明非干扰性质，将稳健性表述为由模态调节的普通二元超性质。

Result: 该框架能够重建覆盖机密性和完整性的完整信息流推理，并避免为降级机制专门增加理论扩展。结果表明，已有较先进的降级机制可以与抽象和模块化所需的机制兼容；这种兼容性并非附加假设，而是强非干扰语义下模态机制的自然产物。

Conclusion: 参数化信息流为统一信息流类型系统提供了简洁的模态类型论解释：机密性、完整性、降级、抽象和模块化不必相互冲突。论文的核心贡献在于说明，适当设计的模态交互可同时维持全强度非干扰和实用程序所需的信息释放能力。

Abstract: Non-interference properties, spanning confidentiality and integrity, have long enjoyed a position as the high water mark of program security guarantees. Information flow type systems comprise the primary means for obtaining non-interference properties of programs, but their potential as a holy grail for secure programming has remained latent. Prior work bifurcates the type system along confidentiality and integrity, resulting in duplicate reasoning machinery and complex specifications. Furthermore, long-held wisdom dictates that non-interference must be weakened with downgrading mechanisms to accommodate the needs of practical programs, nearly all of which violate confidentiality and integrity in the course of fulfilling their purpose. This often pierces abstraction barriers and compromises modular reasoning.
  We introduce parametric information flow, which uses recent insights from modal type theory to shed light on these issues. In particular, we draw inspiration from work on the open and closed modalities, highlighting their rich interplay. Though each individual modality finds uses throughout the literature, our key insight is that their joint interaction suffices to reconstruct full-spectrum information flow reasoning, producing a single framework accounting for both confidentiality and integrity. Downgrading and analogues of advanced reasoning tools in the lineage of robust declassification are recovered without extensions to our theory, strengthening prior results. We show non-interference via a binary logical relations argument, realizing robustness as an ordinary 2-hyperproperty mediated by our modalities. Our work reveals state-of-the-art downgrading mechanisms to be wholly compatible with those for abstraction and modularity, arising precisely from the semantics of the latter under full-strength non-interference.

</details>


### [118] [The Because-Calculus: Separating Production, Existence, and Interpretation in Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17450)
*Oscar Perez Mora*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文比较 handler calculus 与 because-calculus，指出前者将可恢复与不可恢复效应操作统一表示，虽保持类型安全，却会接受无意义的恢复绑定；后者通过结构化区分两类操作，在编译期拒绝此类绑定，并给出范畴语义与元理论证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一的 do 构造仅依赖结果类型区分操作是否可恢复，导致不可恢复操作也可书写恢复变量绑定。此类绑定在运行或编译优化中被消除，但其被类型系统接受表明语言表达与效应语义之间存在不精确性。

Method: because-calculus 将注册操作建模为不可恢复、返回 void 的操作，将证明操作建模为可恢复、返回非 void 的操作；利用对偶效应行与层级索引类型系统表达两者差异，并通过 Resumption Subconstraint 在静态阶段排除无效恢复子句。论文进一步定义从 because-calculus 到 handler calculus 的擦除映射，以伴随三元组中的存在、代换与全称函子塌缩为单一效应操作为核心，证明该映射非忠实。最后为判断规则给出对应的范畴论构造，并证明元理论性质。

Result: Conflation Theorem 表明，because-calculus 中被拒绝的某些子句在擦除后会成为 handler calculus 中可接受的子句，因此统一操作表示会丢失可恢复性信息。完整演算满足进展性、主语约简和塔式进展性；这说明更严格的结构化区分并未破坏语言的类型安全与执行健全性。

Conclusion: 该工作主张可恢复性不应仅由结果类型隐式编码，而应成为效应操作结构和类型约束中的显式属性。because-calculus 以更精细的静态约束消除无效恢复绑定，并通过非忠实擦除定理说明其相较传统 handler calculus 保留了额外且语义重要的区分。

Abstract: Handler calculus conflates resumable and non-resumable effect operations through a single do construct, distinguished only by result type annotation. This conflation does not compromise type safety -- progress and preservation hold -- but it permits resumption bindings for non-resumable operations, creating vacuous bindings that the because-calculus eliminates at compile-time. The because-calculus structurally separates registration (non-resumable, void-returning) from attestation (resumable, non-void-returning) using dual effect rows and level-indexed typing, rejecting such clauses at compile-time via the Resumption Subconstraint. We prove the Conflation Theorem: collapsing the adjoint triple of existential, substitution, and universal functors into a single effect operation is non-faithful -- the erasure from the because-calculus to handler calculus maps rejected clauses to accepted ones. Four movements correspond to four natural transformations; categorical semantics maps each judgment to a category-theoretic construct. We establish progress, subject reduction, and tower progress for the full calculus.

</details>


### [119] [Distributive Laws for Parallel Composition in Rely-Guarantee Concurrency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17536)
*Ian J. Hayes,Larissa A. Meinicke*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文研究并发程序的依赖/保证（rely/guarantee）推理，重点建立并行组合的分配律，以支持代数化、逐步化的并发程序开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖/保证方法能够描述并发环境假设与程序自身承诺，但其代数化推理依赖于可靠的分配律。一般情形下，并行组合的分配律通常只能给出单向细化关系，限制了等式变换和程序推导的能力。

Method: 作者先在抽象的同步原子代数中推导并行组合相关分配律，再将该理论实例化到支持依赖命令和保证命令的代数模型。通过限制被分配命令的结构，构造比一般单向细化律更强的等式律；这些律适用于保证命令，以及特定形式的依赖命令与保证命令组合。全部主要引理及证明均在Isabelle/HOL中形式化验证。

Result: 研究表明，最一般的并行分配律仅能以单向细化形式成立；但在受限命令类别上，可以获得更强的等式分配律。所得到的规律可直接用于保证条件，并支持若干依赖/保证条件组合的代数推理。

Conclusion: 该工作为依赖/保证并发推理提供了经形式化验证的代数基础：通过在抽象同步原子代数中建立分配律，并将其应用至具体依赖/保证模型，增强了并发程序的等式化推导与逐步开发能力。

Abstract: The rely/guarantee approach supports the stepwise development of concurrent programs. Our goal is to develop a theory for reasoning algebraically about concurrent programs in a rely/guarantee style, where rely and guarantee conditions are encoding as commands within our theory. As for mathematics, distributive laws are essential for algebraic manipulation of concurrent programs. In this paper we investigate distributive laws for parallel composition and show how these can be applied to rely/guarantee concurrency. The most general distributive laws are only refinements in a single direction, however, by restricting the form of the command being distributed, one can devise stronger equality laws, which are applicable to guarantee commands as well as to suitable combinations of rely and guarantee commands. Our approach is to develop the distributive laws in a more abstract synchronous atomic algebra, and then apply them to an instance of that algebra supporting rely and guarantee commands. The theory has been formalised in Isabelle/HOL along with proofs of the lemmas presented here.

</details>


### [120] [ETAS: An Effect-Typed Language for Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17780)
*Huiri Tan,Yikun Wang,Puyang Zhang,Shangyu Li,Jiasi Shen*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出ETAS，一种面向智能体系统的编程语言，将模型驱动智能体、工具调用、提示词、类型化记忆、人工审批、策略与执行轨迹纳入语言语义，并通过类型、效应与轨迹约束实现对授权、审计、恢复和非确定性的静态及动态推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统通常把工具调用、审批、权限控制和审计记录作为框架或库层面的约定，难以在编译期统一验证其行为。论文旨在将这些要素提升为一等语义实体，区分确定性计算、智能体非确定性和外部可见动作，从而增强可验证性、可审计性与执行安全性。

Method: ETAS的静态语义通过规格一致性分配普通类型，并为计算附加两个行为索引：可逃逸效应行与可能请求的类型化动作轨迹抽象。其规格系统是终止的编译期约束演算：类型规格提供多态与资源事实证据，可调用规格限制函数和阶段形状，轨迹规格表达允许、禁止及时间性约束。类型检查将请求轨迹与编译后的监控器比较；当动态资源阻碍完全静态证明时，生成残余义务。动态语义区分请求、处理、拒绝和提交事件，并要求处理器在解释动作时保持请求对授权和审计可见。作者还形式化核心演算，证明保存性、进展性、类型/效应健全性、处理器轨迹透明性和策略安全性，并以Rust实现原型。

Result: 论文给出了ETAS的核心语言设计、形式化语义与若干健全性结论；实现包含命令行接口、类型化HIR检查、效应和策略诊断、处理器检查及轨迹感知执行钩子。其关键结果是：动作请求即使被处理器消费，也不会在授权或审计层面被隐藏；部分无法静态判定的资源约束可显式保留为运行时残余义务。

Conclusion: ETAS为智能体程序提供了以类型、效应和轨迹为中心的语言级基础，使授权约束、外部动作、恢复流程和审计证据能够在执行前及执行期间得到系统化推理。该工作的主要价值在于将智能体工程中的隐式运行时约定转化为可检查、可证明且可追踪的程序语义。

Abstract: ETAS is a programming language for agent systems that treats model-backed agents, tool calls, prompts, typed memory, human approvals, policies, and execution traces as semantic program elements rather than library conventions. It separates deterministic computation from agentic nondeterminism and externally visible actions while preserving a direct programming style.
  We present the core design of ETAS. Its static semantics assigns ordinary types through spec conformance and tracks each computation with two behavioral indices: an escaping effect row and a persistent abstraction of the typed action trace it may request. Specs form a terminating compile-time constraint calculus: type specs provide evidence for polymorphism and resource facts, callable specs constrain function and stage shapes, and trace specs express allow, deny, and temporal constraints. Typing checks requested traces against compiled monitors and emits residual obligations when dynamic resources preclude a complete static proof. The dynamic semantics distinguish requested, handled, denied, and committed events; handlers interpret typed actions without making their requests invisible to authorization or audit.
  We formalize a core calculus and state preservation, progress, type/effect soundness, handler trace-transparency, and policy safety. We also implement ETAS in Rust with a command-line interface, typed HIR checks, effect and policy diagnostics, handler checks, and trace-aware execution hooks. ETAS provides a programming-language foundation for reasoning about authorization, nondeterminism, recovery, and audit evidence before and during agent execution.

</details>


### [121] [Weakly Non-Negative Supermartingales for Omega-Regular Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17798)
*Toru Takisaka,Hongjie Qing,Libo Zhang*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出懒惰 Streett 超鞅及其字典序扩展，以弱非负性替代传统的全局强非负性约束，从而更高效地自动验证概率程序几乎必然满足 ω-正则性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于鞅的概率程序验证通常要求证书在全局范围内强非负，但该条件会使许多简单、可由多项式模板表达的证书被排除。直接放宽非负性虽可扩大搜索空间，却可能破坏概率语义下的验证健全性，因此需要一种既弱化约束又保持健全的证书框架。

Method: 作者定义懒惰 Streett 超鞅，并提出其字典序版本，用弱非负条件构造针对 ω-正则性质的几乎必然满足性证书。该方法适用于广泛的采样分布类别，包括所有有界支持分布，并可采用多项式模板进行自动合成。此外，论文从组合角度说明字典序证书如何由一维证书构成。

Result: 在170个多项式概率程序基准上，所提方法相对于强非负性基线将验证成功率提高了20.0至23.5个百分点。理论上，该工作将此前主要用于终止性验证的弱非负鞅方法扩展到了通用 ω-正则性质验证。

Conclusion: 弱非负性并非必然导致概率验证不健全；通过懒惰 Streett 超鞅及字典序构造，可以在保证健全性的同时显著降低证书合成的限制。该方法提升了概率程序自动验证 ω-正则性质的可验证范围与实际成功率。

Abstract: Martingale-based methods are central to probabilistic program verification, but strong global non-negativity requirements can exclude simple certificates from tractable template classes. Relaxing this requirement enlarges the search space for automated synthesis, but naive relaxations are unsound in the probabilistic setting. We introduce lazy Streett supermartingales and their lexicographic extension, showing that weak non-negativity can nevertheless be used soundly to certify almost-sure satisfaction of $ω$-regular properties with polynomial templates under a broad class of sampling distributions, including all bounded-support distributions. This extends prior weakly non-negative methods from termination to general $ω$-regular verification. We further give a compositional account of lexicographic certificates in terms of one-dimensional ones. Experiments on 170 polynomial probabilistic-program benchmarks show increases of 20.0-23.5 percentage points in verification success over the strongly non-negative baseline.

</details>


### [122] [Parameterized Verification of Deterministic MPI Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18049)
*Stephen F. Siegel*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种针对参数化消息传递程序的验证方法：将进程数视为参数，并把并发的 C/MPI 程序转换为可验证的参数化顺序程序，随后复用现有顺序程序验证技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消息传递程序的正确性依赖于进程间通信顺序、消息匹配及并发交互；当进程数量不固定时，传统针对固定规模并发系统的验证方法难以直接适用。作者旨在为具有唯一进程标识、点对点发送与接收操作的参数化程序提供自动化或半自动化验证支持。

Method: 用户需提供若干通信规约函数，包括进程 i 到进程 j 的发送消息数量、每个通信事件在 happens-before 偏序层级中的位置，以及从 i 到 j 发送的第 k 条消息所满足的性质。方法依据这些规约对原始消息传递程序进行顺序化转换，生成参数化顺序程序；该程序可交由适用于参数化顺序程序的任意验证技术处理。作者将该方法实现为 Frama-C/Wp 的扩展，用于验证 C/MPI 程序。

Result: 该方法把参数化并发通信验证问题归约为参数化顺序程序验证问题，并在 Frama-C/Wp 环境中提供了面向 C/MPI 的实现支持。摘要未给出具体实验基准、验证效率、自动化程度或适用案例的量化结果。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是利用用户声明的通信结构与消息性质进行程序顺序化，从而降低参数化消息传递程序验证的复杂性，并使其能够复用成熟的顺序验证基础设施。该方法的有效性与易用性在一定程度上依赖于用户提供的通信规约是否准确、充分且易于构造。

Abstract: We consider the problem of verifying a message passing program in which the number of processes is a parameter NP and each process knows its unique ID. Processes communicate using send and receive commands which specify a single destination or source. To verify the program, the user provides functions specifying the number of messages sent from process i to process j, the level of each communication event in the happens-before hierarchy, and a fact that holds for the k-th message sent from i to j. These are used to transform the program to a parameterized sequential program which can be verified using any techniques appropriate for such programs. We realize this approach in an extension to Frama-C/Wp to verify C/MPI programs.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Composable Verification Pipelines for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16266)
*Julian Alfredo Mendez,Andreas Brännström*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个基于 Tiles、使用函数式语言 Soda 实现的模块化验证框架，用于对动态系统中的状态、动作、转移及轨迹进行可执行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作与变化推理方法通常依赖逻辑编程，虽然语义表达能力强，但在模块化组合、执行透明性、复用性及工程化验证流程方面仍有改进空间。论文旨在以函数式管线替代或补充传统实现方式，将动作语言语义转化为可执行、可组合且可终止的验证过程。

Method: 框架将状态、动作、转移和规则表示为可组合的函数式组件，并将验证过程编码为带类型的函数式管线。其核心基础是 Tiles 形式化结构，具体由 Soda 语言实现。用户可通过 YAML 编写领域描述；系统随后将这些描述操作化为底层验证模型及可执行管线。类型化管线用于约束组件接口和组合方式，终止性保证用于确保验证流程能够完成。

Result: 论文声称实现了一个开源原型，支持模块化规格定义、推理组件复用、透明的执行工作流以及具有终止保证的验证管线。作者通过涉及错误信息与情绪推理的示例展示框架的使用方式，但摘要未报告与现有方法的定量性能比较、基准测试结果或形式化正确性证明细节。

Conclusion: 该工作将动作与变化推理的语义验证组织为函数式、类型化、可执行的模块化管线，并通过 YAML 降低领域建模门槛。其主要贡献在于架构与实现范式，而非摘要中可见的实证性能突破；后续应进一步评估表达能力、可扩展性、验证正确性及相对于逻辑编程系统的效率优势。

Abstract: Existing approaches for reasoning about action and change provide expressive semantics for modeling dynamic systems, in most cases built on top of logic programming systems. We introduce a modular framework for transition and trajectory verification based on Tiles and implemented in Soda, which is an efficient functional programming language. The framework operationalizes action language semantics through executable verification pipelines that process states, actions, transitions, and rules as compositional functional components. Verification procedures are represented as typed functional pipelines, enabling modular specifications, reusable reasoning components, and transparent execution workflows with guaranteed pipeline termination. The framework includes an executable specification layer that allows users to define domain descriptions in YAML, which are operationalized into the underlying verification model and executable pipeline structure. We provide an open-source implementation and illustrate the framework through examples that involve misinformation and emotional reasoning.

</details>


### [124] [Fuzzy directed simulations for fuzzy modal logics over residuated lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16346)
*Linh Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出模糊Kripke模型之间的模糊有向模拟概念，证明其逻辑保持性质与Hennessy-Milner刻画，并给出有限模型上最大模拟关系的计算与实验实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊模态逻辑的模型比较与行为等价工具需要适配模糊真值和加权迁移结构。作者旨在建立一种适用于线性且完备剩余格的有向模拟框架，以支持逻辑验证、模型约简和自动化分析。

Method: 首先在一般线性完备剩余格上形式化定义模糊有向模拟，并研究其基本性质；随后证明fPDL中全部正公式在该模拟下保持；进一步建立相应的Hennessy-Milner定理，将逻辑不可区分性与模拟关系联系起来。对于有限模糊Kripke模型，论文提出计算最大模糊有向模拟的算法，并针对Gödel、乘积和Łukasiewicz结构实现该算法，最后开展性能实验。

Result: 理论上，模糊有向模拟能够保持fPDL正公式的满足程度，并具有Hennessy-Milner式逻辑表征。算法上，作者能够计算两个有限模糊Kripke模型间的最大模糊有向模拟；实现与实验结果表明，该方法可在所考察的三类常用剩余格语义下运行并得到性能数据。

Conclusion: 该工作将有向模拟理论推广到模糊Kripke模型和一般剩余格语义，兼具逻辑基础、可计算算法及实验实现价值。其主要贡献是把正模态性质保持、逻辑刻画和最大模拟计算统一到同一框架中，为模糊系统的行为比较与形式化验证提供了工具。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of fuzzy directed simulation between fuzzy Kripke models over any linear and complete residuated lattice and investigate its fundamental properties. In particular, we prove that all positive formulas of the fuzzy modal logic $\mathit{fPDL}$ are preserved under fuzzy directed simulations and establish a Hennessy-Milner theorem for this notion. Furthermore, we present a method for computing the greatest fuzzy directed simulation between two finite fuzzy Kripke models and implement it for the case where the underlying residuated lattice is the Gödel, product, or Lukasiewicz structure. Finally, we experimentally evaluate the performance of the implementation and present the obtained results.

</details>


### [125] [Show Me The Money: An Exercise in Proof-Driven Software Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16499)
*Joseph Tafese,Karthik Nukala,Hassen Saïdi,Natarajan Shankar,Arie Gurfinkel,Giuliano Losa*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文通过一个成熟工业级C++代码库的案例，展示了如何将形式化方法用于遗留关键基础设施的软件理解与验证，并证明生产代码中的核心性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统形式化方法多在系统设计阶段使用，而成熟遗留系统通常缺少可验证的规格与证明工件。作者旨在探索如何对已投入生产的复杂C++系统建立可靠的形式化保障，并支持后续代码变更的持续验证。

Method: 研究聚焦于Stellar SDEX订单簿核心算法。方法上结合大语言模型辅助理解与建模、PVS定理证明以及SeaHorn模型检查，对生产代码中的关键逻辑和不变量进行形式化分析与证明；同时产出可复用的验证工件，使未来修改能够自动或半自动地与既定不变量进行比对。

Result: 该方法证明了生产代码核心算法的若干关键性质，发现文档中关于某个异常位置可达性描述的不一致，并建立了支持代码演化检查的形式化验证基础设施。

Conclusion: 定理证明与模型检查的协同使用能够为成熟遗留系统提供切实的高保障验证路径。其价值不仅在于发现问题或证明当前实现正确，还在于形成可持续复用的规格、证明与检查工件，以降低未来变更引入缺陷的风险。

Abstract: We present a case study on proof-driven software understanding of mature, security-critical infrastructure. While formal methods are traditionally applied during the design phase, we present our experience applying formal reasoning onto a mature industrial C++ codebase. We focus on a formal analysis of the core algorithm that implements the Stellar blockchain's SDEX order book. By combining large language models (LLMs), Prototype Verification System (PVS), and SeaHorn, we are able to prove core properties of the production codebase. Our approach also identified an inconsistency in documentation related to the reachability of an exception location. Most importantly, however, we produce artifacts that make it easy for code changes to be checked against established invariants. This work demonstrates how the strategic combination of theorem proving and model checking provides a path for delivering robust assurance to legacy systems.

</details>


### [126] [A New Branching Bisimulation for Probabilistic Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16757)
*Guo Li,Zhaokai Li,Xinxin Liu,Zhiming Liu,Quan Sun,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种用于概率进程的新型分支双模拟等价，并声称其区分能力强于已有忽略不可观察动作的等价关系；同时给出根式版本，以保证其在含递归及静态、动态构造的概率进程语言中满足同余性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率并发系统中，不可观察动作的抽象与概率行为的保留之间存在张力。已有等价关系可能过于粗糙，无法区分某些具有不同概率分支结构的进程；此外，等价关系若不具备同余性，则难以支持模块化替换和组合式验证。

Method: 作者定义一种新的概率分支双模拟关系，通过在抽象不可观察动作的同时更精细地约束概率分支及其匹配方式，诱导出新的行为等价。随后构造其根式变体，并针对包含常见静态构造、动态构造和递归的概率进程语言证明该变体是同余。

Result: 摘要声称：新等价关系比任何已知的、对不可观察动作进行抽象的等价关系都更精细；其根式版本对于目标概率进程语言的所有相关算子均保持同余性。摘要未提供形式定义、证明细节、与具体既有语义的逐项比较或实例。

Conclusion: 该工作的核心贡献是兼顾精细行为辨识与代数可组合性的概率进程语义框架。若其精细性比较和同余性证明成立，该关系可为概率并发系统的等价验证、化简及演算推理提供更强的理论基础。

Abstract: We introduce a new branching bisimulation for probabilistic processes, which induces a more refined equivalence relation than any known equivalence that abstracts from unobservable actions, with a rooted version that is a congruence for a language of probabilistic process with the usual static as well as dynamic constructs including recursion.

</details>


### [127] [Topology in Synthetic Domain Theory and its Formalisation in Agda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17292)
*Runze Xue*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文研究综合领域理论中的 Phoa 原理，并将其推广到超限情形；通过引入对偶单纯形、脊柱与 sobriomorphism，提出对该原理的新解释，并在 Cubical Agda 中进行区间类型公理化及主要定理形式化证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Phoa 原理用于刻画区间类型上的路径与信息序之间的关系，是理解综合领域理论中代数结构和完备性的重要工具。现有理论对更一般的超限结构及不同完备性概念之间的统一仍缺乏系统框架。

Method: 研究首先分析 Phoa 原理及其超限推广；随后定义对偶单纯形和脊柱，利用 sobriomorphism 建立新的解释框架；在此基础上提出一个旨在统一 Segal 完备性与链完备性的假设性完备性定理。形式化部分使用 Cubical Agda 对区间类型进行公理化，并验证主要定理。

Result: 工作给出了 Phoa 原理面向超限情形的推广方向，建立了对偶单纯形、脊柱和 sobriomorphism 等概念工具，并提供了针对 Phoa 原理的新型结构性解释。其主要理论结果已获得形式化验证，但统一两类完备性的定理仍处于假设或提议阶段。

Conclusion: 该项目扩展了 Phoa 原理在综合领域理论中的适用范围，并以形式化证明增强了结果的可靠性。其核心潜在贡献在于为路径、信息序及完备性概念建立统一视角；后续关键任务是严格证明所提出的完备性定理，并明确其适用条件与同既有完备性理论的关系。

Abstract: This project investigates the Phoa principle in synthetic domain theory (SDT), and provides a generalisation to the transfinite cases. The Phoa principle plays a pivotal role in SDT by illustrating how the paths give the information order on the interval type and other algebraic structures in SDT. The project defines the dual simplices and spines and introduces the concept of sobriomorphisms, which contributes to a new interpretation of the Phoa principle and its generalisations. Finally, the project proposes a hypothetical completeness theorem that may unify the Segal completeness and the chain completeness in SDT based on investigations on the Phoa principle in the project. The project also includes axiomatisation of the interval type in Cubical Agda and the formalised proof for the main theorems.

</details>


### [128] [Proceedings 42nd International Conference on Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17707)
*Wolfgang Faber,Laura Giordano,Ricardo Rocha,Vítor Santos Costa*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该摘要介绍了2026年国际逻辑程序设计会议（ICLP 2026）技术通讯论文集的背景、举办信息、收录范围及投稿赛道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICLP旨在为逻辑程序设计领域提供国际性学术交流平台，集中展示该领域的最新研究进展、技术实践与近期发表成果。

Method: 论文集通过主赛道（Main track）和近期已发表研究赛道（Recently Published Research track）征集并汇集论文及扩展摘要，覆盖理论基础、语言设计与编程方法、程序分析与优化、应用和实现方法等主题。

Result: 该卷收录了ICLP 2026的技术通讯成果，反映了逻辑程序设计在基础理论、软件开发方法、性能优化及实际应用实现等方向的研究活动。摘要未提供具体论文数量、评审流程、实验设置或定量研究结果。

Conclusion: 该文献属于会议论文集导言性质的摘要，其主要价值在于界定ICLP 2026论文集的学术定位、主题范围与组织结构，而非报告单项研究的具体方法或发现。

Abstract: Since the first conference in Marseille in 1982, the International Conference on Logic Programming (ICLP) has been the premier international event for presenting research in logic programming. These proceedings include the Technical Communications of the 42nd ICLP, held 20-23 July 2026 as part of FLoC 2026 in Lisbon, Portugal. The papers and extended abstracts in this volume address the following areas and topics: theoretical foundations, language design and programming methodologies, program analysis and optimization, applications and implementation methodologies. This volume features contributions to two submission tracks of ICLP 2026: the Main track and the Recently Published Research track.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [AEVAL: From Anecdotal to Deterministic Testing for Agentic Skill Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16345)
*Tejas Singh Anand,Yuet Ying Christina Wang,Wanting Jiang,Steve Masson,Tian Zheng,Bingjie Zhou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AEVAL 是一个集成到持续集成流程中的智能体技能评测框架，通过确定性的变更触发测试、执行器与评分器分离、首次尝试评分及修复轨迹记录，为技能包提供可复现、可审计的质量信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体技能评估主要依赖开发者手工演示和主观判断，缺乏跨运行可复现性、跨版本可比性与规模化能力。尤其是执行智能体可能在运行中自行修复错误，再对修复后的结果打出通过分数，从而掩盖技能初始输出的真实缺陷。

Method: 框架为每个技能定义评测契约 eval.config，并在每次技能变更时由自动执行器运行测试，生成结构化工件和质量信号。AEVAL 将执行器与评分器在架构上分离；评分器依据执行器首次尝试的输出进行判定，同时显式记录后续自我修复行为。系统还提供基于证据的分层修复建议：LV1 聚焦因果性问题，LV2 聚焦输出质量问题，并将建议以内联方式反馈至代码合并请求。

Result: 在实际生产环境的多个智能体软件开发工具包和技能上验证显示，AEVAL 能够将表面上的 100% 通过率转化为稳定、可复现的首次尝试失败信号，并保留执行器所有修复动作的可审计记录。

Conclusion: 该工作将智能体技能评估从非正式、主观的试用过程转变为持续集成中的标准化测试机制。其核心价值在于识别并隔离自我修复导致的评估偏差，使技能质量回归能够被可靠发现、追踪和比较。

Abstract: Modern agentic systems increasingly rely on skills: installable packages of natural language and code that teach an LLM agent to perform a domain task. As skill repositories grow, developers need automated quality signals on every change, yet evaluation today is largely anecdotal: a developer asks an agent to "try the skill," watches a demo, and forms a subjective impression. This yields neither reproducibility across runs nor comparability across versions, and scales poorly to marketplaces where one regression can silently break dozens of downstream workflows.
  We present AEVAL (Agentic Evaluation), a CI-integrated framework that replaces this practice with a deterministic, reproducible test pipeline for agentic skills. Every skill change triggers a test event: the skill runs against a developer-declared evaluation contract (eval.config) inside an automated executor, emitting a structured, evidence-grounded quality signal that downstream CI can route on.
  A key ingredient is a structural separation between executor and grader, preventing a subtle but pervasive failure mode: an agent that silently self-corrects during execution and then grades its own patched outputs as passing.
  Our contributions are: (i) a deterministic, change-triggered evaluation protocol with per-skill contracts and per-run artifact schemas; (ii) a formalization of self-correction bias as a distinct failure mode of naive agentic evaluators; (iii) an executor/grader separation with a first-attempt grading rule and explicit self-correction tracking; and (iv) a tiered, grounded-evidence fix-suggestion scheme (LV1 causal, LV2 quality) posted as inline merge-request comments. Validated on real skills in a production agentic stack across multiple agent SDKs, AEVAL converts spurious 100% pass rates into reproducible first-attempt fail signals with an auditable record of every executor fix.

</details>


### [130] [AoA: Theorem Proving Agent over Abstract Syntax Tree of Redesigned Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16372)
*Qiyuan Xu,Joshua Ong Jun Leang,Renxi Wang,Wenda Li,Haonan Li,Luke Ong,Conrad Watt*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 AoA（Agent over AST），将大模型证明代理从基于源代码文本的交互方式迁移到基于抽象语法树（AST）的结构化操作，以降低交互式定理证明中的令牌、工具调用与 API 成本，并提升求解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有证明代理以序列化的具体语法生成证明文本，并通过独立、依赖行号的查询获取证明状态。文本编辑会改变后续行号，导致代理反复定位错误和子目标，造成高令牌消耗、高工具调用次数及高成本。同时，这种文本依赖使代理难以利用训练语料中较少出现但性能优异的 Minilang。

Method: AoA要求模型以 JSON 形式输出 Minilang 的 AST，而非源代码文本；该表示天然适配具备工具调用能力的大模型。系统采用树编辑式证明交互模型，将证明操作及相应子目标状态统一存储于同一证明树中，使每个操作节点直接携带其子目标状态。由此，代理可通过结构化树操作完成证明构造、状态读取与修改，无需依赖行号定位。

Result: 在 miniF2F 和 NTP4VC-Pearl 的共同成功集合上，相比 Amazon Isabelle Agent，AoA在归一化输入缓存计费下将 API 成本降低 2.3至4.7倍，令牌使用量减少 2.9至6.9倍，工具调用次数减少 3.9至8.9倍，完成时间缩短 1.4至2.0倍。此外，AoA在难度更高的验证基准上解决了更多问题。

Conclusion: 以 AST 为中心的证明代理架构能够消除具体语法和行号驱动交互带来的重复开销，并使新型证明语言 Minilang 可被有效用于大模型证明。AoA表明，结构化表示与树级状态管理可同时改善证明代理的成本、速度和求解能力。

Abstract: Interactive theorem proving (ITP) underpins program verification and formalized mathematics, but its manual effort limits scalability. LLM-based proof agents promise to ease this effort, but their heavy token consumption and API cost remain a major obstacle. We trace this cost to a shared root: current agents operate on serialized concrete syntax, emitting proofs as source text and recovering proof states through separate, line-number-based queries, so every edit shifts later lines and forces repeated relocation of errors and states. This same dependence on concrete syntax also blocks adoption of Minilang, a recent proof language that reaches SOTA on LLM-based proving but is too new for LLMs' training corpora. We address both problems by lifting the agent off source text and onto the abstract syntax tree (AST): the model supplies proofs as JSON representations of Minilang's AST -- native to tool-calling LLMs -- and drives the prover through a tree-edit model that fuses proof operations and states into one proof tree, so each operation carries its own subgoal's state, readable directly off the tree. We realize this design in \emph{Agent over AST} (AoA). Against Amazon's Isabelle Agent on miniF2F and NTP4VC-Pearl common success sets, AoA cuts API cost by 2.3--4.7x (normalized input-cache accounting), uses 2.9--6.9x fewer tokens and 3.9--8.9x fewer tool calls, and finishes 1.4--2.0x faster -- while also solving far more problems on the harder verification benchmark.

</details>


### [131] [Fantastic Adaptive Taxonomies and How to Use Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16387)
*Mert Cemri,Andrei Cojocaru,Melissa Pan,Shu Liu,Shubham Agarwal,Alexander Krentsel,Jay Tang,Kannan Ramchandran,Joseph E. Gonzalez,Matei Zaharia,Alex Dimakis,Ion Stoica*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AdaMAST从智能体执行轨迹中自动归纳紧凑、可复用的失败分类体系，并将其作为搜索优化、运行时反馈和轨迹选择的统一接口，在多个基准上优于自由文本反思。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行轨迹虽然包含失败信息，但通常冗长、强依赖具体实例，且缺乏描述重复性失败模式的稳定术语，因此难以持续积累并复用改进经验。现有无需更新模型参数的优化方法需要更结构化、可共享的失败反馈表示。

Method: AdaMAST将目标智能体的执行轨迹转换为基于证据的失败分类法。分类法沿系统级、角色特定和领域特定三个固定轴组织，每个失败代码的名称、定义和证据模式均由轨迹自动归纳，不依赖人工编写代码或人工标注轨迹。该分类法随后用于：候选智能体系统搜索中的失败诊断、运行时反馈，以及基于分类代码构建的轨迹验证器AdaMAST-Judge。

Result: 在五个测试基准中，使用分类代码诊断失败的智能体系统搜索优于自由形式反思。运行时反馈使SWE-agent在SWE-bench Verified Mini上的解决率从自由文本反思的60%提升至70%，并使Claude Code从64.0%提升至70.7%。在Terminal-Bench 2.0上，AdaMAST-Judge将best-of-5准确率相对Pass@1提高8至15个百分点。归纳出的词汇表还能以约一个数量级的压缩率保留轨迹差异，与专家失败标注的一致性高于人工构建的参考词汇表，并且针对不同领域归纳出的分类代码重合较少，表明其具有领域适应性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，自动归纳的自适应失败分类法可将原始执行轨迹转化为结构化、可迁移的反馈接口，从而闭环连接智能体产生的失败记录与后续的搜索、运行时改进和轨迹筛选过程。其核心贡献是证明无需人工标注或预定义失败代码，也能构建兼具紧凑性、可解释性和实际改进效果的失败知识表示。

Abstract: An agent system's execution traces record how it fails, and procedures that improve such a system without changing model weights (trajectory selection, prompt and workflow optimization, runtime monitoring) read these traces for feedback. Yet raw traces are a poor medium for accumulating that feedback: long, instance-specific, and lacking a stable vocabulary for recurring failures. We argue that an agent system should instead maintain an explicit representation of how it fails, induced from its own behavior and reusable wherever failure feedback is needed. AdaMAST builds this representation by converting a target system's traces into a compact, evidence-grounded failure taxonomy: named failure codes organized along three fixed axes (system-level, role-specific, and domain-specific), with every name, definition, and evidence pattern induced from the traces; no code is hand-authored, no trace human-annotated. The taxonomy is not merely a post-hoc diagnostic but a shared feedback interface, improving agents in three ways. In agent-system search, taxonomy-coded diagnoses of failed candidates outperform free-form reflection on all five benchmarks we test. At runtime, taxonomy feedback raises SWE-agent's resolution on SWE-bench Verified Mini from 60% with free-text reflection to 70%, and improves Claude Code from 64.0% to 70.7% as a runtime skill. In trajectory selection, AdaMAST-Judge, a verifier built on the induced codes, improves best-of-5 accuracy on Terminal-Bench 2.0 by 8-15 points over Pass@1. The vocabulary itself is compact (an order-of-magnitude compression that preserves trace distinctions), human-faithful (matching expert failure annotations more closely than a hand-crafted reference vocabulary), and adaptive (taxonomies induced for different domains share few codes). Adaptive failure taxonomies close the loop between the traces agents produce and the procedures that improve them.

</details>


### [132] [When to Use Which? Benchmarking Optimisers for Configurable Systems under Varying Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16476)
*Chao Jiang,Yulong Ye,Tao Chen,Miqing Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文系统比较了8种配置调优优化器在22个可配置系统、不同预算条件下的表现，发现紧预算下模型驱动方法更优，宽松预算下模型无关方法更具优势，而FLASH在多数系统和预算范围内表现稳定且强劲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件配置调优中，单次配置评测成本差异显著，用户可用预算也各不相同。现有优化器各有适用条件，但实践中缺少“给定预算应选择何种优化器”的系统性证据，因此需要评估不同优化器的预算敏感性与适用范围。

Method: 作者在22个可配置系统上，对8种成熟优化器进行系统实验，并设置不同的调优预算水平进行比较。研究进一步分析FLASH跨预算稳定表现的原因，重点考察系统优化景观中局部最优点及其吸引域特征。

Result: 模型驱动优化器（如SMAC）在评测次数受限的紧预算条件下表现较好；模型无关优化器（如遗传算法）在预算更充足时通常更优。FLASH在大多数系统上不随预算变化而显著失效，整体表现持续较强。原因在于许多系统存在质量较高且吸引域较大的局部最优区域，使得贪婪式搜索能够较快找到有效配置。

Conclusion: 优化器选择应考虑预算：紧预算优先考虑模型驱动方法，较大预算下可采用模型无关方法。若需要跨系统、跨预算的稳健默认选择，FLASH具有较强实用价值；其有效性依赖于大量真实系统中存在易于到达的高质量局部最优解。

Abstract: Software configuration tuning is crucial for optimising system performance, and various optimisers have emerged over the last decade. Yet, the time required during the tuning process may vary across systems. In some systems (e.g., PostgreSQL), it may take a few minutes to measure a configuration, whereas in some others (e.g., MariaDB), it can take several hours. Moreover, even within the same system, users may have varying budgets and preferred settings. This naturally raises a question -- Given a budget level, which optimiser is the best choice for SE practitioners? This matters because optimisers usually have their own ``comfort zone'' and may perform very differently under distinct budgets.
  In this paper, we aim to answer this question. We systematically evaluate eight well-established optimisers across 22 configurable systems under varying budget levels. We find that, unsurprisingly, model-based optimisers (e.g., SMAC) are well-suited under tight budgets, and model-free optimisers (e.g., GAs) become superior with more generous budgets. However, interestingly, there is one optimiser, FLASH, that performs consistently well on most systems regardless of budgets. We lastly investigate the reasons behind this phenomenon and find that many systems possess good local optima (with large basins of attraction), allowing greedy optimisers (e.g., FLASH) to achieve strong performance.
  Source code, data, and supplementary materials of this work are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Config-W2W-98B2.

</details>


### [133] [Personalized Assessments from Personal Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16494)
*Yufan Zhang,Jaromir Savelka,Seth Copen Goldstein,Majd Sakr*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出个性化探测谜题（p³）方法，用于快速评估学习者是否真正理解自己提交的代码，尤其面向使用AI编程代理后代码理解可能不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助编程工具的普及使学生和从业者能够快速生成代码，但也可能在缺乏充分理解的情况下提交或使用代码，从而带来学术诚信与专业责任风险。现有评估方式难以低成本、针对性地验证个体对其自身代码的理解。

Method: 研究者开发了p³：依据学习者自己的代码自动生成个性化问题，以异步方式发放，并要求在数分钟内完成。该方法在一门研究生层次的云计算课程中进行了试点测试，用于探测学生对其代码实现、行为或细节的理解程度。

Result: 试点结果表明，p³能够帮助识别学生对本人代码理解可能存在的缺口。该方案具有自动生成、异步实施和完成时间短的特点，因而具备较低的教学与评估操作成本。

Conclusion: p³是验证代码作者理解程度的一种有前景的轻量化评估工具，但目前证据主要来自课程试点。后续研究需检验谜题得分与真实代码理解之间的关联，并探索将该方法整合进专业软件开发中的代码审查流程。

Abstract: The rapid development and popularization of AI-enabled coding agents have meant software engineering students and professionals cannot be assumed to understand their own code, which risks academic integrity and professional accountability. We developed a method called Personalized Probing Puzzles ($p^3$) to evaluate students' understanding of their own code, and tested $p^3$ in a graduate-level cloud computing course. Our pilot study shows that $p^3$ can help identify potential gaps in students' understanding of their own code. The puzzles are automatically generated, asynchronously administered, and finished in minutes. Future work is needed to correlate puzzle results with code understanding and to embed $p^3$ in a professional code review process.

</details>


### [134] [ReqGenX: An Empirical Study of Atomic Decomposition, Artifact Regeneration, and Reconstruction for Legacy SRS Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16564)
*Ragib Shahariar Ayon,Rayed Fahmi,Sumon Biswas,Shibbir Ahmed*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出ReqGenX流程，将既有软件需求规格说明书转换为可追溯的合成pre-SRS工件，用于细粒度评估基于大语言模型的软件需求规格说明书生成。实验表明，该流程在来源忠实性、工件质量和下游可恢复性方面总体表现较好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化软件需求规格说明书生成的评估缺少同时连接原始需求、中间需求获取工件和最终规格说明书的细粒度可追溯数据集。因此，研究关注能否从已有规格说明书中构造带有明确来源关联的合成中间工件，以建立更可控、更细致的评估基础。

Method: 作者在7份PURE数据集的软件需求规格说明书上开展实证研究。ReqGenX先将规格说明书章节分解为有来源依据的原子化陈述，再通过多模型多数投票将原子陈述路由至受标准启发的工件类型；随后使用约束提示生成工件，并通过评审模型指导的迭代修订提升质量。评估维度包括原子陈述的来源对齐与质量、工件相对原子陈述的语义扎根性、信息保留情况，以及由工件重建规格说明书后的可恢复性。

Result: 原子陈述具有较高忠实性和可用性：AlignScore中位数通常为0.96至0.99，Prometheus评分为4.34至4.85。生成工件与源原子陈述保持较强对齐，AlignScore通常为0.80至0.94，评审通过率接近100%；在更严格的Prometheus评估下，通过率为54.8%至97.1%。下游重建实验显示，工件支持的原子陈述可从生成的软件需求规格说明书中恢复，SBERT平均分为0.69至0.75，AlignScore中位数为0.76至0.84。

Conclusion: ReqGenX证明了从既有软件需求规格说明书构造可追溯合成pre-SRS工件是可行的，并能为大语言模型软件需求规格说明书生成提供细粒度评估支撑。研究同时揭示了忠实性、信息保留与工件完整性之间存在权衡；尤其是较严格质量评估下通过率波动较大，说明工件生成的稳定性和完整性仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Background: Evaluating automated Software Requirements Specification (SRS) generation is challenging because few datasets provide fine-grained traceability between source requirements, intermediate elicitation artifacts, and generated specifications. Aims: We aim to study whether legacy SRS documents can be transformed into traceable synthetic pre-SRS artifacts that support fine-grained evaluation of LLM-based SRS generation. Method: We conduct an empirical study using ReqGenX, a controlled pipeline that decomposes SRS sections into source-grounded atomic statements, routes atoms to standards-inspired artifact types through multi-LLM plurality voting, and generates artifacts using constrained prompts with iterative judge-guided refinement. We evaluate ReqGenX on seven PURE SRS documents using grounding, quality, information retention, and downstream reconstruction analyses. Results: ReqGenX produces faithful and usable atoms, with median AlignScore values typically between 0.96 and 0.99 and Prometheus scores ranging from 4.34 to 4.85. Generated artifacts remain strongly grounded in their source atoms, with AlignScore values typically between 0.80--0.94 and judge pass rates near 100%; stricter Prometheus evaluation yields pass rates from 54.8% to 97.1%. In a downstream SRS reconstruction case study, artifact-backed atoms remain recoverable from generated SRSs, with SBERT means between 0.69 and 0.75 and AlignScore medians between 0.76 and 0.84. Conclusions: Traceable synthetic pre-SRS artifacts can support more fine-grained evaluation of LLM-based SRS generation, while exposing tradeoffs among faithfulness, information retention, and artifact completeness.

</details>


### [135] [From Discussion to Execution: Replicating Buggy and Correct Data Science Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16569)
*Ragib Shahariar Ayon,Mohammad Wardat,Shibbir Ahmed*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出Reprodgen，一个基于大语言模型的框架，用于从问答论坛帖子中自动重建可执行的数据科学缺陷程序及其修复版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非正式来源中的数据科学代码复现困难，原因包括问题描述模糊、依赖缺失和性能限制。开发者问答内容包含真实的故障诊断与修复经验，但其信息不完整、非结构化，难以直接支持自动调试与验证。

Method: Reprodgen输入问题及对应回答，分别重建问题中描述的缺陷行为与回答中给出的预期修复，生成可执行的缺陷代码—修复代码对。框架构建代码意图、功能需求和结构化推理表示，并通过基于大语言模型的审查器迭代改进代码；语义一致性由模型评估，可执行性则通过实际运行验证。研究在Stack Overflow和GitHub Issues数据上进行测试，覆盖pandas、numpy、scikit-learn等七个数据科学库，并构建由人工专家验证的可运行基准集。

Result: 实验结果表明，Reprodgen能够较可靠地复现论坛讨论中的缺陷及修复逻辑，生成经过执行验证的程序对；不同大语言模型在该任务上的表现存在明显差异。

Conclusion: Reprodgen展示了将非结构化开发者问答自动转化为可执行、可验证的软件复现样例的可行性，可为数据科学程序的自动调试、测试、验证和基准构建提供支持。

Abstract: Reproducing reliable data science code from informal sources is challenging due to ambiguous problem specifications, missing dependencies, and performance bottlenecks. Although developer Q&A forums provide rich discussions on diagnosing and fixing real-world issues, the information is often incomplete and unstructured, limiting its use for automated debugging and verification. In this paper, we introduce Reprodgen, a large language model (LLM) based framework for automatically replicating executable buggy and patched data science programs from Q&A forum posts. Given a question and its corresponding answer, Reprodgen reconstructs the buggy behavior described in the question and the intended fix described in the answer, producing executable buggy and patched code pairs that reflect the original discussion. The framework builds structured representations of code intent (CI), functional requirements (FR), and Structured Chain of Thought (SCoT), and iteratively refines code using an LLM-based reviewer until it is executable and semantically consistent. We evaluate Reprodgen on Stack Overflow (SO) and GitHub Issues (GI) across seven data science libraries, including pandas, numpy, and scikit-learn, and construct a benchmark of runnable buggy and patched programs validated by human experts. Our pipeline uses LLMs for semantic assessment, while executability is verified through actual execution. Results show reliable replication with clear differences in model performance.

</details>


### [136] [DataFlow-Harness: A Grounded Code-Agent Platform for Constructing Editable LLM Data Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16617)
*Runming He,Zhen Hao Wong,Hao Liang,Zimo Meng,Chengyu Shen,Xiaochen Ma,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出DataFlow-Harness，用于引导大语言模型以平台原生、可持久化且可编辑的DAG工作流形式完成数据处理任务，从而弥合自然语言到工作流管道之间的“NL2Pipeline gap”。在12项数据工程任务上，该系统观察到93.3%的端到端通过率，并较Vanilla Claude Code显著降低成本与生成延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体通常生成独立脚本，而非能在数据平台中持续保存、可视化编辑和复用的工作流实体。这种脚本生成结果与实际平台工作流之间的脱节，使自动化产物难以维护、审查和迭代。论文旨在让模型直接构建平台可执行的工作流DAG，而不是仅输出自由形式代码。

Method: DataFlow-Harness采用增量式、带类型约束的图变更操作来构建DAG。系统由三部分组成：DataFlow-Skills，提供构建流程所需的程序性指导；MCP层，向模型暴露实时算子注册表和当前管道状态；DataFlow-WebUI，将对话式创建过程与可视化DAG编辑器同步。该设计通过实时平台上下文约束模型动作，并使每一步生成直接落地为平台工件。

Result: 在12任务数据工程基准上，DataFlow-Harness的观察端到端通过率为93.3%。相对于Vanilla Claude Code，其测得货币成本降低72.5%，生成延迟降低49.9%。与Context-Aware Claude Code相比，其通过率仅低0.9个百分点，但成本低42.8%。任务级分析表明，当工作流构建依赖隐含的程序性知识时，Skills模块的收益最明显。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将模型生成过程建立在实时平台状态、算子能力和类型化增量操作之上，能够以接近脚本生成基线的可靠性创建持久化、可编辑的工作流DAG，同时降低构建成本和时延。需要注意的是，结论基于规模较小的12任务基准，文中“observed”通过率与成本指标的跨平台泛化能力仍需在更多任务和环境中进一步验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to automate data-processing workflows, yet coding agents typically produce scripts that are not automatically materialized as persistent, editable platform artifacts. We call this disconnect the \textit{NL2Pipeline gap}. To bridge it, we introduce \textsc{DataFlow-Harness}, a platform that guides an LLM agent to construct platform-native directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) through typed, incremental mutations rather than free-form scripts. The platform combines \textsc{DataFlow-Skills} for procedural guidance, a Model Context Protocol (MCP) layer that exposes the live operator registry and current pipeline state, and \textsc{DataFlow-WebUI}, which synchronizes conversational authoring with a visual DAG editor.
