On a 12-task data-engineering benchmark, \textsc{DataFlow-Harness} achieves a 93.3\% observed end-to-end pass rate. Relative to Vanilla Claude Code, it reduces measured monetary cost by 72.5\% and generation latency by 49.9\%; its observed pass rate is within 0.9 percentage points of the Context-Aware Claude Code baseline while its cost is 42.8\% lower. Per-task analysis indicates that Skills are most useful when construction depends on implicit procedural knowledge. These results show that live platform grounding can produce persistent, editable workflow artifacts with an observed reliability close to script-generation baselines and with lower measured construction cost and latency.

</details>


### [137] [CLOSER-Bench: Evaluating Budgeted Cross-Stage Design Closure for Hardware Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16632)
*Peilong Zhou,Zhirong Chen,Cangyuan Li,Haoyu Gao,Kaiyan Chang,Ziming Qu,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CLOSER-Bench提出一种用于评估硬件编码智能体跨阶段设计收敛能力的受控基准，强调在有限预算下从规格、RTL到GDS的连续决策与恢复能力，而非孤立的代码生成成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件智能体基准分别考察RTL生成、代码修复、验证、PPA优化或物理实现，任务设计与评测预言机彼此割裂，难以定位智能体在抽象层级切换时的真实能力。硬件设计中的工具反馈具有延迟、异构和连续优化特征，后端失败还可能要求回溯修改RTL，因此pass@k等离散指标不足以描述设计收敛过程。

Method: 该工作构建CLOSER-Bench评测协议：针对同一设计和隐藏目标，配对设置规格到RTL、RTL到GDS、规格到GDS三类任务；完整记录仿真、综合、静态时序分析和布局布线工具调用；评估最终质量、任意时刻进展、工具成本及跨阶段恢复能力。基准基于Verilator、Yosys、OpenROAD、KLayout、Sky130和Harbor智能体框架实现，并通过包含RTL修复、变异验证、覆盖率、PPA优化、设计空间探索、跨模型调试等在内的十项试点任务验证执行链路。

Result: 试点结果显示存在显著的“局部完成—整体收敛”差距：三个智能体均可完成局部AXI修复任务，但在对应的验证收敛任务中，仅前沿智能体表现出明显优势，另外两个原本成功的基线智能体未能达到相同水平。作者还验证了完整RTL到GDS流程，并构建了基于宏模块的AXI/DMA流式加速器，用于阶段配对评估。

Conclusion: 该论文的核心贡献是将硬件设计收敛形式化为受预算约束的长时序决策问题，并提供可追踪全过程工具交互、比较跨阶段恢复能力的统一评测框架。其结果表明，仅衡量局部任务完成率会高估智能体的硬件工程能力；未来评估应同时考察端到端质量、过程效率以及面对后端反馈时的跨抽象层回溯与修正能力。

Abstract: Hardware engineering exposes coding agents to a form of long-horizon work that is difficult to capture with pass-at-k: progress is continuous, tool feedback is delayed and heterogeneous, and a backend failure may require revising RTL rather than tuning another physical-design parameter. Existing benchmarks measure RTL generation, repository repair, verification, PPA evolution, or physical implementation, but their different designs and oracles make it hard to determine where an agent succeeds or fails across abstraction boundaries. We introduce CLOSER-Bench, a controlled evaluation protocol for budgeted cross-stage design closure. For one design and one hidden objective, it pairs spec-to-RTL, RTL-to-GDS, and spec-to-GDS tasks, records every simulator, synthesis, STA, and place-and-route invocation, and measures final quality, anytime progress, tool cost, and cross-stage recovery. The benchmark is built on open-source Verilator, Yosys, OpenROAD, KLayout, Sky130, and the Harbor agent harness. A ten-task pilot spanning RTL repair, mutation-based verification, coverage, PPA optimization, design-space exploration, cross-model debugging, and security establishes the executable harness and exposes a sharp completion--closure gap: three agents solve a localized AXI repair task, while the matched verification-closure task separates a frontier agent from two otherwise successful baselines. We further validate a full RTL-to-GDS flow and construct a macro-based AXI/DMA streaming accelerator for the stage-paired evaluation. These results motivate treating hardware closure as a budgeted sequential decision problem rather than a collection of independent code generation tasks.

</details>


### [138] [Falsification-Based Verification of LLM-Generated Optimization Models: Sound Test Batteries and Their Detection Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16646)
*Haifeng Li,Mo Hai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向大语言模型生成优化模型的“基于证伪的验证”框架：不依赖参考模型或人工标签，仅通过对输入数值槽位进行受控变换并调用求解器，检测模型是否违反必然成立的优化性质。该方法以零误报为设计目标，并在实验中有效发现了大量“可运行但语义错误”的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言到优化模型的转换存在隐蔽风险：生成模型即使能够被求解器正常执行，也可能对应了错误的决策问题。传统执行成功率或与答案的简单对比难以识别这类错误，尤其在缺少标准参考模型时。因此，需要一种无需标签、可提供可靠错误证据的验证机制。

Method: 作者将问题描述中的每个数值视为带类型的槽位，并对这些槽位施加结构化变换，再比较候选模型在变换前后的求解结果。测试规则由对偶理论、比较静态分析和多面体极限论证导出，涵盖方向性、曲率、压缩探针、禁止性极限、湮灭和交换等类别。所有测试均满足健全性：一旦观测到违反，即可确定候选模型不忠实于原问题。论文还分析该框架不可观测的错误，给出典型错误类别必然被检出的条件，并证明固定阈值扰动测试器无法同时做到健全且非平凡。

Result: 在NL4OPT的326个真实模型及四类基准模型上，测试电池的误报率为0.0%，而阈值测试器为54.9%。该方法检出70.0%的已认证条件类别变异模型，并识别出40.4%无法通过执行准确率评分发现的变异错误。实验结果还复现了理论预测的可检测性分布，包括理论上应当无法检测的零值区域。

Conclusion: 该工作将优化模型验证从“判断输出是否可执行或近似正确”转向“利用优化结构进行可证伪检验”。其核心贡献是构建了无需参考答案、零误报的求解器调用验证框架，并明确刻画其检验能力与理论边界。该方法尤其适合作为大语言模型自动建模系统的可靠性筛查层，但其本质是证伪工具：未发现违规并不等同于模型已被完全证明正确。

Abstract: Large language models now translate natural-language descriptions of decision problems into solver-ready optimization models, but they fail silently. A generated model often runs and still formulates the wrong problem. This paper develops a theory of falsification-based verification for this setting. Every numeric quantity in the description is a typed slot, and a candidate model is tested only through solver calls on slot-transformed instances; no reference model or label is consulted. From duality, comparative statics, and polyhedral limit arguments we derive a battery of test classes covering directions, curvature, crush probes, prohibitive limits, annihilation, and exchange. Every test is sound, so a violation certifies unfaithfulness and the false-positive rate is zero by design. We characterize what such verification can never see, give conditions under which the canonical error classes are detected with certainty, and prove that no fixed-threshold perturbation tester is simultaneously sound and nontrivial. Experiments on 326 ground-truth models from NL4OPT and four benchmark families confirm the theory. The battery attains a 0.0% false-positive rate against 54.9% for a threshold tester, detects 70.0% of certified conditional-class mutants, convicts 40.4% of the mutants invisible to execution-accuracy scoring, and reproduces the predicted detectability pattern including its zeros.

</details>


### [139] [How Do You Choose Your AI Component? An Interview Study of Secure AI Integration in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16660)
*Mahzabin Tamanna,Elizabeth Lin,Sparsha Gowda,Laurie Williams,Dominik Wermke*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过访谈22名软件开发者、架构师与AI从业者，考察其选择和集成大语言模型等AI组件时的决策过程。结果显示，实践者主要依据性能、准确率、成本及工具调用、多模态等功能特性进行选择，较少系统评估安全性、来源可信度和持续风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI组件正快速进入现代软件系统，但传统软件供应链中关于依赖来源、组件可信度、数据保护和生命周期治理的经验，尚未被充分迁移到AI组件采用实践。研究旨在理解从业者当前的实际决策逻辑及其安全考量缺口，为降低AI软件供应链风险提供依据。

Method: 研究采用探索性半结构化访谈方法，对来自不同组织的22名开发者、软件架构师和AI实践者进行访谈，了解其在AI组件选择、集成和使用过程中的评价标准、安全实践与相关认知。随后对访谈材料进行分析，归纳共性模式和问题。

Result: 模型选择通常由功能和业务因素主导，包括模型能力、输出质量、性能、成本、可用性，以及工具调用和多模态支持等特征。安全性很少被作为独立且关键的筛选标准；在组件引入、配置和后续维护等阶段，也普遍缺乏持续性的安全评估。研究认为，行业正重演早期软件依赖管理中重视快速复用、忽视安全与来源治理的问题。

Conclusion: AI组件的采用应转向安全内建的治理模式：采用方应将安全评价纳入选型、集成、部署和维护全过程；模型提供方应提升组件来源、能力边界、数据处理与风险信息的透明度；研究者应形成适用于AI供应链的评估方法和实践指导。核心结论是，安全不应在功能选型完成后再补充，而应成为AI组件生命周期中的持续性要求。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) as AI components in modern software systems introduces distinct security risks to the software supply chain. While many considerations and safety mechanisms are in place for components of the traditional software supply chain, the recent rapid adoption of AI components and platforms has overlooked these hard learned lessons. Selecting and integrating AI models without clear guidance on how these choices affect system security may leave applications vulnerable to threats, such as malicious components, data leakage, and unintended behavior. The goal of this study is to understand practitioners' decision making process and security considerations in selecting and integrating AI components through an exploratory semi-structured interview study. Toward this goal, we conducted semistructured interviews with 22 software developers, architects, and AI practitioners across diverse organizations about how they integrate AI components into their software.
  Our analysis finds that practitioners' model selection is predominantly driven by functional criteria, including performance, accuracy, cost, and specific features, e.g., tool calling or multimodal support, while security is rarely considered as an evaluation criterion. We observe a consistent lack of security concern throughout the AI component integration process, with established software supply chain lessons overlooked or ignored. The industry is repeating the historically costly mistakes of early software dependency management, prioritizing rapid reuse and availability over security and provenance. We distill our findings into actionable recommendations for AI adopters, model providers, and researchers, advocating for a proactive, security-by-design approach that integrates security evaluation into component selection and sustains it throughout the software development lifecycle.

</details>


### [140] [Specification-Driven Development as the Foundation of AI-Native Enterprise Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16680)
*Mamdouh Alenezi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 文章比较了直觉驱动的vibe coding与规格驱动开发（SDD），提出规格治理参考模型（SGRM），主张企业级AI软件工程应以可验证、可审计的规格治理约束概率性生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型和智能体进入软件开发，纯对话式生成虽提高原型开发速度，却可能导致生产率—可靠性矛盾、架构退化、安全缺陷及技术债务。文章旨在建立适用于企业场景的治理框架，以平衡生成效率、质量保证与工程可控性。

Method: 研究基于经验证的文献语料归纳非治理式生成的失效模式；设计工具无关的SGRM，包括四组件规格契约、以确定性验证约束随机生成、三个严谨度等级，以及生成—验证—治理闭环；随后依据ISO/IEC 25010质量模型，将质量属性映射到对应治理机制，并引用已有实证结果评估效果。

Result: SGRM将规格作为权威事实来源，使需求、架构、约束与验收标准能够共同约束智能体生成过程。文中报告，在约束性规则下安全缺陷降低73%，在规格治理的智能体交付中上市周期缩短50%。vibe coding适合创意探索和快速原型，但难以独立满足企业软件对可追溯性、可靠性与审计性的要求。

Conclusion: 文章的核心结论是，规格治理能够将概率性的AI代码生成纳入确定性验证和闭环控制，从而提升企业软件工程的质量与可治理性。其贡献主要是提出SGRM及质量映射，但摘要中的量化效果更像二手实证证据；仍需明确实验设计、基线、任务类型、样本规模和统计显著性，以进一步验证模型的通用性与因果效应。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and agentic AI are shifting software engineering from manual coding toward intent specification, architecture, and governance. Two paradigms have emerged: vibe coding, an intuition-driven approach accepting AI artifacts via observed behavior, and Specification-Driven Development (SDD), which uses structured specifications as the authoritative source of truth. This article makes three contributions. First, based on a verified literature corpus, it identifies failure modes of ungoverned conversational generation: the productivity-reliability paradox, architectural erosion from limited context, security exposure, and technical debt. Second, it introduces the Specification Governance Reference Model (SGRM). This tool-independent framework defines four-component specification contracts, constrains stochastic generation via deterministic validation, formalizes three rigor levels, and integrates generation, verification, and governance into a closed-loop architecture. Third, it evaluates SGRM against ISO/IEC 25010, mapping quality characteristics to governance mechanisms. Empirical evidence supports this, reporting a 73% reduction in security defects under constitutional constraints and a 50% reduction in time-to-market through specification-governed agentic delivery. The analysis concludes that while vibe coding is valuable for ideation and rapid prototyping, enterprise software requires specification governance to transform probabilistic AI generation into deterministic, auditable engineering. Boundary conditions, threats to validity, and future research directions are discussed.

</details>


### [141] [Portable models as a replacement for industrial heuristics in compiler optimizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17389)
*Fot Nikolai,Vinarsky Alexander*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种可移植的函数内联决策预测框架，用机器学习近似成熟编译器的内联启发式，使轻量级编译器、源到源工具和解释器无需复用完整优化基础设施，也能预测是否应内联某个调用点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成熟编译器的内联器依赖内部中间表示、优化流程、目标平台模型及复杂分析组件，轻量级系统通常难以直接复用。论文旨在将这些编译器已有的内联决策迁移为独立、低依赖、可部署的预测器。

Method: 作者以生产编译器诊断信息生成监督标签，构建独立提取器恢复调用者—被调用者调用点，并生成隔离源码片段。随后将片段规范化为通用AST，必要时降级为轻量结构IR，提取标量特征训练表格模型。特征涵盖源码局部性、显式inline标记、被调用函数规模、副作用、分支与调用结构、函数签名形态及实参形态。最终模型可导出为普通C代码，不依赖编译器运行时。评估数据集包含15个开源C项目的336,938个调用点，其中79,287个为编译器报告的内联事件；采用按项目划分的验证方式，并进行留一项目交叉验证。

Result: 在留一项目验证中，CatBoost表现最佳，ROC-AUC达到0.928，PR-AUC达到0.713。经阈值调优后，F1从0.670提升至0.729，假阳性率从0.192降至0.084。结果表明，基于源码及轻量结构信息的特征能够捕获大量与生产编译器内联决策相关的信号。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了无需复刻完整编译器IR和优化管线，也可以通过可移植特征与监督学习有效预测内联决策。该框架尤其适合基础设施受限的编译、转换或执行系统；但其预测目标本质上是近似既有编译器策略，实际泛化能力仍取决于训练项目、标签编译器及目标环境之间的差异。

Abstract: The paper investigates the possibility of predicting function-inlining decisions in compact compilers, source-to-source tools, and interpreters where the reuse of GCC or LLVM optimization infrastructure is impractical. The relevance of this work is determined by the need to transfer mature inlining heuristics to systems with limited compiler infrastructure, restricted runtime dependencies, and reduced access to target-specific analysis. Existing production compilers already contain strong inliners, but their decisions depend on internal intermediate representations (IRs), pass ordering, target models, and analysis stacks that are difficult to reproduce in lightweight systems. To overcome these constraints, we propose a portable inlining-prediction framework. Production compiler diagnostics serve as supervision; a separate extractor reconstructs caller-callee callsites, prepares sterile source snippets, normalizes them into a universal AST, optionally lowers them to a lightweight structural IR, and exports scalar features for model training. Thus, a trained predictor can be emitted as ordinary C code without a compiler-runtime dependency. To evaluate the proposed framework, we constructed a dataset comprising 336,938 callsites from fifteen open-source C projects, including 79,287 compiler-reported inline events. A comparison of several tabular models is performed using project-aware validation. Under leave-one-project-out validation, CatBoost reaches ROC-AUC 0.928 and PR-AUC 0.713; after threshold tuning, F1 improves from 0.670 to 0.729 and the false-positive rate drops from 0.192 to 0.084. Feature analysis shows that most signal is concentrated in source locality, explicit inline intent, callee size, side effects, branch and call structure, signature shape, and callsite argument shape.

</details>


### [142] [Beyond Visibility and Technical Reuse: Public Application Transformation in Open-Source Model Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16687)
*Duorong Wang,Xiaoting Wei,Bin Liu,Jiannan Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“公共应用转化”作为衡量开源模型影响力的新维度，利用Hugging Face上模型与Spaces之间的结构化链接，分析模型从仓库发布到公开应用或演示系统的转化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅统计模型发布、下载量、点赞数或下游模型复用，无法完整反映模型是否真正进入可见的公共应用。论文旨在区分平台可见性、技术复用与应用导向采用等不同类型的影响力，并扩展开源模型影响力的测量框架。

Method: 研究构建了覆盖平台规模的数据集，包括256万个模型仓库、106万个Spaces、81万个数据集仓库和122万个账户资料，并提取Model-Space链接、Dataset-Space链接以及模型到模型的技术复用链接。随后比较模型进入Spaces的分布特征、与下载和点赞等可见性指标的关系、与下游技术复用的差异，以及应用转化模型的元数据完备度和Space配置类型。

Result: 进入公共应用的模型比例较低，且Model-Space链接高度集中于少数模型，表明应用转化具有明显的选择性和集中性。应用转化与平台可见性相关，但并不等同于技术复用；下载量、点赞数、下游模型复用和应用采用衡量的是不同维度的影响。被转化为应用的模型通常具有更强的元数据准备度，并进入包含数据集、SDK和特定任务类别的异质化Space配置。

Conclusion: 开源模型影响力不应只被理解为模型可获得性或技术层面的下游复用，还应包括其在平台机制支持下被整合进公开应用与演示系统的能力。论文提供了基于平台链接数据的测量路径，将模型影响力研究从静态发布和技术依赖关系扩展到面向应用的转化过程。

Abstract: Open-source model platforms have made it easier to publish AI models, but model release alone does not reveal whether models become visible, technically reused, or incorporated into public applications. This study introduces public application transformation as a platform-visible dimension of model impact and examines it through structured Model-Space links on Hugging Face. We construct a platform-scale dataset of 2.56 million model repositories, 1.06 million Spaces, 810,087 dataset repositories, and 1.22 million account profiles, together with Model-Space, Dataset-Space, and model-to-model technical reuse links. The analysis shows that public application transformation is highly selective and concentrated: only a small share of models are linked to Spaces, and most Model-Space links are concentrated among a limited set of models. More importantly, application transformation is associated with platform visibility but is not equivalent to technical reuse, indicating that downloads, likes, downstream model reuse, and application-facing uptake capture different forms of model impact. Additional analyses show that application-transformed models tend to exhibit stronger metadata-based readiness and enter heterogeneous Space configurations involving datasets, SDKs, and task-specific application categories. By tracing how models move from repositories into public applications and demos, this study extends the measurement of open-source model impact from artifact availability and technical reuse to platform-mediated transformation across AI information objects.

</details>


### [143] [Dependency-Guided Code Generation: Structured Matrix Decomposition and Consistency-Guided Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16692)
*Mingqiao Mo,Yangchen Zeng,Zikai Xiao,Xin Xiao,Wenhua Nie,Zhaolu Kang,Guangyuan Dong,Kai Shu,Hao Zhang,Xiaodong Fan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种依赖感知的自动代码生成框架，通过显式建模代码实体间的多层依赖关系，提高生成代码的语义一致性与结构完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成方法难以充分捕获代码实体之间复杂的显式与隐式依赖，因而生成结果可能存在逻辑缺失、组件难以集成或结构不一致等问题。

Method: 方法将代码依赖图分解为两部分：量化矩阵用于表示强且显式的依赖关系，稀疏低秩分解用于刻画弱且隐式的交互关系。该分解通过交替优化算法学习；在生成阶段，将学习得到的依赖结构作为约束引入生成过程。针对强依赖，论文还使用稀疏三元组表示以降低存储成本并提升计算可扩展性。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法相较现有方法能够更稳定地产生语义对齐程度更高、结构保真度更好的代码，并在依赖表示的存储效率和计算扩展性方面具有优势。

Conclusion: 依赖结构的显式建模及其在生成过程中的约束作用，是改善自动代码生成质量的有效途径。强弱依赖分解结合稀疏表示，能够在保证表达能力的同时兼顾效率与可扩展性。

Abstract: The increasing complexity of modern software systems has made automated code generation a fundamental task in software engineering. However, existing approaches often fail to adequately capture the intricate, multi-level dependencies among code entities, leading to generated code that is logically incomplete or difficult to integrate into real-world systems. To address this limitation, we propose a dependency-aware code generation framework that explicitly models interactions among code entities through a graph-based representation. We decompose dependencies into two complementary components: a quantized matrix that captures strong, explicit relations, and a sparse low-rank factorization that models weaker, implicit interactions. The decomposition is efficiently learned via an alternating optimization procedure. During code generation, the learned dependency structure is incorporated as a constraint, ensuring both semantic coherence and structural consistency of the generated code. Furthermore, we introduce a sparse triplet representation for strong dependencies, significantly improving storage efficiency and computational scalability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach consistently produces code with superior semantic alignment and structural fidelity compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [144] [Model-Driven Discipline for Multi-Agent LLMs: Requirement-to-Verification Generation of Traceable System Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16708)
*Ran Wei,Le Zhu,Haochi Wang,Ruizhe Yang,Jiapeng Guan,Siyuan Ji,Yuchen Hu,Zhe Jiang,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出RADIANT：一种将模型驱动工程与多智能体大语言模型结合的系统开发方法，用于自动生成跨工程阶段的异构模型、可执行追踪链接、变更影响分析及形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型驱动工程通常涉及多个工具和异构模型，导致模型间追踪、维护与变更管理困难。论文旨在通过大语言模型自动化模型生成与关联，并提升复杂系统开发效率与可验证性。

Method: RADIANT以精确定义的需求模型为输入，通过多智能体协作生成概念模型、领域专用建模语言、符合该语言的系统模型及行为模型；建立元素级可执行追踪链接，并据此进行精确的自动化变更影响分析。行为模型被转换为CSP，接受死锁自由性、收敛性等形式化验证；当验证失败时，系统利用反例驱动修复循环迭代改进模型。实验比较了三种大语言模型下的多智能体方案与单智能体基线，并开展用户研究及跨领域迁移测试。

Result: 在三种大语言模型上，多智能体任务分解均能稳定提高生成形式化工件的语法有效性；对于支持代码生成的模型，也提高了工件可执行性。语义准确性的提升则依赖于具体模型。六名参与者的研究表明，该方法可将开发时间降低约10至15倍；无需修改流程即可迁移至第二个领域。

Conclusion: RADIANT表明，多智能体大语言模型能够有效增强模型驱动工程中的自动化建模、形式化验证和变更管理能力。其主要优势体现在语法正确性、可执行性与开发效率，而语义正确性仍受所选大语言模型能力限制，未来需进一步提升语义一致性与生成结果的可靠性。

Abstract: Software complexity is a long-standing challenge for system engineers. Model-Driven Engineering (MDE) addresses it by treating models as first-class artefacts, but a typical MDE process spans many tools and produces heterogeneous models of different system aspects, making traceability, maintenance, and change management difficult.
  We propose RADIANT, an engineering methodology that combines MDE with Multi-Agent Large Language Models (LLMs) for complete model-based system development, with a focus on safety-critical systems. From a carefully specified requirement model, RADIANT automatically generates heterogeneous models across engineering phases -- a concept model, a domain-specific modelling language, a conforming system model, and a behaviour model -- together with executable, element-level traceability links, on top of which it provides exact, automated change-impact analysis. Generated behaviour models are translated into CSP and formally verified (e.g.\ for deadlock freedom and convergence) with a counterexample-driven repair loop. Evaluating RADIANT across three LLMs, we find that the multi-agent decomposition reliably improves the \emph{syntactic validity} of the generated formal artefacts over a single-agent baseline -- and their \emph{executability} where the model's code generation permits -- while gains in semantic accuracy are model-dependent. A six-participant study shows an order-of-magnitude ($10$--$15\times$) reduction in development time, and the unmodified pipeline transfers to a second domain.

</details>


### [145] [Agentic Code Review in the Terminal: A Trajectory-Level Analysis of Behavior, Cost, and Human-Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16740)
*Wachiraphan Charoenwet,Kla Tantithamthavorn,Patanamon Thongtanunam,Hong Yi Lin,Minwoo Jeong,Ming Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出应从轨迹与成本角度评估终端环境中的智能体代码审查，而不仅关注最终性能；结果表明，智能体审查精度较高，但伴随显著的探索与验证开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码审查评测主要衡量结果性能，难以揭示智能体在真实代码仓库中的动态决策过程、失败模式及隐性运行成本。作者希望通过分析执行轨迹，理解智能体审查成功与失败的行为差异。

Method: 研究分析终端式、仓库上下文驱动的智能体代码审查器执行轨迹，考察其探索、规划与后续验证等行为，并将这些行为与审查精度及成功结果关联起来。

Result: 智能体审查器具有较高的审查精度，但通常需要较多仓库探索和验证操作，因而产生较高运行开销。成功的审查轨迹表现出更强的前期规划能力，并且依赖较少的后续验证。

Conclusion: 未来的智能体代码审查评测应纳入轨迹质量与成本敏感指标，联合评估审查结果、规划与探索效率、验证开销，而非仅以最终性能作为标准。

Abstract: Agentic code review in terminal-based environments enables early feedback during local development before pull request creation. However, existing evaluations remain performance-centric and fail to capture the dynamic behaviors of repository-grounded agentic reviewers. Understanding these behaviors is critical for identifying how agentic reviewers succeed, fail, and incur hidden operational costs in practice. Then, we analyze the reviewers' behavior based on their trajectories. Our results show that agentic reviewers achieve higher review precision but incur substantial exploration and validation overhead, while successful reviews are associated with stronger planning and less downstream validation. These findings highlight the potential benefits of trajectory-aware and cost-sensitive evaluation of future agentic code review systems.

</details>


### [146] [A Preliminary Study on the Impact of AI in the Creativity and Collaboration in Software Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16744)
*Danilo Monteiro Ribeiro,Kiev Gama,Victoria Jackson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文基于对4家公司13名软件工程专业人员的半结构化访谈，探讨生成式人工智能如何影响团队中的创造力与协作。研究发现，AI既能扩展开发者的创意与问题解决路径，也可能削弱独立构思、同伴交流及导师制；同时，团队正形成开发者、同事与AI共同推理的新型三方协作模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI开发工具主要面向个人效率优化，但软件开发本质上依赖团队协作、知识共享与共同创造。论文旨在弥补对AI如何改变团队创造力、协作关系和社会互动机制认识不足的问题。

Method: 研究采用定性研究设计，对来自4家公司的13名软件专业人员开展半结构化访谈，每家公司对应一个不同团队。研究者围绕AI工具的使用方式、使用后果以及团队协作体验进行主题分析，并将情绪作为贯穿各主题的交叉分析维度。

Result: 研究归纳出三个核心主题：AI使用、AI的后果、协作与团队动态，以及跨主题的情绪维度。具体而言，AI可帮助开发者获得更多思路、方案和表达方式，拓宽创造性资源；但对AI的依赖可能减少自主探索和独立创意生成。开发者也更倾向于先向AI提问而非咨询同事，这可能弱化非正式知识交流、同伴学习和指导关系。与此同时，部分团队出现了开发者、同事和AI共同讨论、验证与推理的三方协作实践。

Conclusion: 生成式AI在软件团队中并非单纯的效率工具，而是正在成为影响认知、创造力和协作结构的参与者。组织在推广AI时，应不仅关注个体生产率，还应设计机制维护同伴咨询、知识传承和独立思考，例如鼓励团队共同审查AI输出、保留协作式讨论环节，并将AI纳入明确的团队工作规范。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) tools are reshaping software engineering work, increasingly acting as cognitive partners rather than purely instrumental aids. Most AI tooling remains optimized for individual use, yet software development is inherently collaborative and creative, raising open questions about how AI affects team-based work. This paper examines how software engineering professionals perceive and integrate AI tools focusing on creativity and collaboration within teams. We conducted semi-structured interviews with 13 software professionals from four companies, each representing a distinct team. Our analysis identifies three central themes - AI Use, Consequences of AI, and Collaboration and Team Dynamics, alongside Emotions as a cross-cutting dimension. Key findings include: AI broadens developers' creative repertoires but may narrow independent ideation; developers increasingly consult AI instead of colleagues, weakening peer learning and mentoring; and teams are developing emerging triadic collaboration patterns in which developers, colleagues, and AI reason together.

</details>


### [147] [A Non-Intrusive Traffic Analysis Framework for Authorization Risk Detection and Coordinated Response in Web Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16754)
*Siqi Lin,Yuchao Luo,Zihan Zhu,Yiran Wang,Borui Qiu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向有效Web会话中授权违规的非侵入式流量分析框架，通过关联请求—响应事务、提取多源运行时证据并进行可解释风险融合，实现分级处置与协同响应。受控测试集上指标很高，但实验规模、数据分布和规则设计均较理想化，尚不能证明其在真实生产环境中的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效认证会话中的授权违规通常不具备明显的协议层特征，其识别依赖业务对象、用户身份、访问上下文和行为序列等语义信息。传统仅依赖网络协议或单一异常特征的检测方式难以实时识别此类风险，因此需要一种无需修改应用代码、能够从流量中组织异构证据并驱动响应的机制。

Method: 框架对请求与响应事务进行关联，提取访问主体、业务端点、对象标识、认证状态和行为序列等信息；随后将对象访问证据、身份一致性、行为异常、认证上下文、网络环境以及端点—操作风险映射为可解释风险分量。系统通过加权融合与高风险优先约束形成分级风险决策，并映射至放行、告警、拦截和外部策略联动等动作。论文使用确定性、可解释规则对风险分量进行实例化，并通过消融实验验证关键模块的贡献，同时测量原型系统的决策时延。

Result: 在包含2,000个均衡标注样本的受控本地测试环境中，系统在1,000个授权风险事件中识别出998个，在1,000个正常访问中未产生误报，报告准确率为99.90%、精确率为100.00%、召回率为99.80%、F1为99.90%。消融结果显示，移除运行时对象证据后F1降至81.31%，移除高风险优先约束后F1降至73.90%，表明两者是性能关键因素。原型可行性测试中，在100并发、总计1,000个请求条件下，平均风险决策计算时延为0.077毫秒，P99时延为0.137毫秒。

Conclusion: 该研究的主要价值在于构建了一个可解释、非侵入式的授权风险检测与响应闭环，并证明了运行时对象证据和高风险优先机制对框架有效性的重要性。其局限性也较明确：实验数据量较小且类别均衡，规则为人工确定性设计，测试环境受控，缺少跨应用、跨业务场景、真实流量分布、加密流量可观测性、误报处置成本和长期规则维护等验证。因此，该论文能够支持框架级可行性的结论，但不足以支持生产环境普适性或大规模部署有效性的结论。

Abstract: Authorization violations under valid Web sessions are difficult to identify and handle in real time from traffic because they depend strongly on business semantics and exhibit few distinctive protocol-level features. This paper proposes a non-intrusive traffic analysis framework for authorization risk detection and coordinated response. Request-response transactions are correlated to extract the access subject, business endpoint, object identifier, authentication state, and behavioral sequence. Object-access evidence, identity consistency, behavioral anomalies, authentication context, network environment, and endpoint-operation risk are mapped to interpretable risk components. Weighted fusion and high-risk priority constraints produce graded decisions that drive allow, alert, block, and external policy actions. The risk components are instantiated using deterministic and interpretable rules to evaluate multi-source evidence organization, risk fusion, and the coordinated-response loop at the framework level. In a controlled local testbed containing 2,000 balanced labeled samples, the framework classified 998 of 1,000 authorization-risk events as risky and produced no false positives among 1,000 normal accesses, achieving 99.90% accuracy, 100.00% precision, 99.80% recall, and a 99.90% F1 score. Removing runtime object evidence reduced the F1 score to 81.31%, while removing the high-risk priority constraint reduced it to 73.90%. In a prototype feasibility test with 100 concurrent requests and 1,000 total requests, mean risk-decision computation latency was 0.077 ms and P99 latency was 0.137 ms. The results demonstrate the feasibility of organizing heterogeneous runtime evidence and establishing an executable authorization-risk decision and coordinated-response loop without modifying application code, but do not establish general applicability in production environments.

</details>


### [148] [When Post-Anchor Metrics Fail: Stabilization Regimes in AI-Evidence Open-Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16979)
*Hudson Craig,Benjamin Barlog,Chenggang Wang,Longwei Wang,Zedong Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文指出“锚点后稳定化密度”无法可靠衡量架构变更带来的延迟稳定化义务，因为该指标将日常维护活动与真正由架构锚点引起的后续修复混为一谈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码仓库挖掘研究需要识别架构边界引入后是否产生测试、CI、文档和修复等延迟性稳定化工作，但现有的锚点后事件计数缺乏对因果归属和背景维护的区分。

Method: 作者分析338个高可见度GitHub仓库的差异级证据，并开展308个事件的连续时间窗口对照实验，比较锚点事件前后的广义与严格稳定化信号密度，同时设置非锚点对照。另由两名独立标注者对100个分层抽样候选事件进行盲法人工验证，并归纳六类稳定化模式。

Result: 338个仓库中有321个存在真实的变更文件锚点证据，但锚点后的稳定化密度相对锚点前的中位数比值接近1.0，且与非锚点对照无差异。人工验证中，100个候选锚点有45个并非持久架构边界；仅3个事件可归因为延迟稳定化义务。其余事件可由分类错误、锚点当时的即时加固、背景维护或锚点前加固解释。债务归属标注的一致性为κ=0.50。

Conclusion: 单纯使用锚点后稳定化密度会产生误判，不能作为识别架构变更后延迟义务的可靠指标。仓库挖掘应采用含非锚点对照的受控设计，并结合稳定化模式和人工或机制化归因，区分真实延迟义务与普遍维护活动。

Abstract: Repository mining studies increasingly analyze AI-evidence projects, yet it remains unclear how to measure whether architectural changes create deferred stabilization obligations. A natural metric, post-anchor stabilization density, counts tests, CI gates, documentation, and fixes appearing after a durable boundary is introduced. We show that this metric fails. In a diff-level study of 338 high-visibility 2026 GitHub repositories, anchors are common (321 of 338 contain real changed-file anchor evidence), but a controlled 308-event contiguous-window experiment finds no post-anchor uplift: broad and strict stabilization signals both yield median post/pre density ratios near 1.0, and non-anchor controls are equally dense. We introduce stabilization regimes, six recurring patterns that explain why the density metric fails, and use human validation to calibrate them. Two independent coders label 100 stratified candidate-anchor events from blinded packets (kappa = 0.50 on debt attribution). The validation exposes a two-layer trap: many candidate anchors are not durable boundaries (45 of 100), and even among valid anchors in this calibration sample the no-uplift result holds: only 3 of 100 events survive as attributable delayed obligations; the remaining 97 are explained by classifier error, anchor-local hardening, background maintenance, or pre-anchor hardening. Post-anchor density conflates pervasive maintenance with genuine debt; controlled designs with regime-aware attribution are necessary before repository mining can reliably identify stabilization obligations.

</details>


### [149] [Schema-Bound LLM Control of Scientific Instrumentation through Model Context Protocol Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17012)
*Roberto dos Reis,Vinayak P. Dravid*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种通过模型上下文协议（MCP）将本地大语言模型连接至科学仪器的方法，重点在于以类型化、物理约束和可测试的软件接口降低智能体直接操控仪器的风险，并支持长时运行的实验任务与可复用实验流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学仪器通常受限于厂商专有接口、采集主机内进程加载要求及本地部署限制，难以直接接入云端或通用智能体。同时，自然语言生成的工具参数可能违反物理边界，导致不合理甚至不可执行的仪器操作。因此，需要一种本地化、厂商中立、具备参数安全约束且易于验证的智能体—仪器连接机制。

Method: 方法基于MCP构建四层能力：第一，定义模式约束的工具接口，在请求分发至仪器适配器前验证参数类型及物理范围；第二，采用厂商中立的宿主进程适配器模式，将语言模型推理与仪器端执行解耦；第三，为实时处理和长时间实验提供持久化任务生命周期管理；第四，将由类型化工具组成的多步骤实验协议注册为MCP提示词技能。参考实现开源提供30个类型化工具、5类实时任务和6项技能，并通过具有相同协议接口的物理合理模拟器进行验证。

Result: 软件验证部分的120项与硬件无关测试全部以确定性方式通过。15项本地LLM集成测试在多次运行中每次通过12至15项，失败波动主要归因于模型输出的非确定性。针对5个开放权重工具调用模型的初步单次试验显示，小型本地模型能够在无云端依赖下驱动该模式约束接口；但该试验不构成基准评测，未报告置信区间，也不能据此比较模型性能。

Conclusion: 该工作贡献了一个受物理边界限制、可复现、可测试且可扩展的本地智能体仪器控制基础设施。其核心价值不在于证明LLM具有可靠的科学自主性，而在于通过模式验证、适配器隔离和生命周期管理，将模型生成的不确定性限制在可检查的工具调用边界内。后续仍需在真实硬件、故障恢复、权限控制、实验安全和系统性模型评测条件下验证其实用性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can plan tool-mediated scientific work, but scientific instruments remain difficult to connect to such agents: vendor APIs may load only inside acquisition host processes, facilities may prohibit cloud-hosted agents, and natural-language interfaces can emit physically unreasonable arguments. We present a method for connecting local LLMs to scientific instruments through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). It combines: (1) a schema-bound tool surface that validates requests against physical bounds before adapter dispatch; (2) a vendor-neutral, host-process adapter pattern separating language-side reasoning from instrument-side execution; (3) a persistent lifecycle for long-running live-processing jobs; and (4) MCP-prompt-registered skills that compose typed tools into reusable multi-step protocols. Our open-source reference server exposes 30 typed tools, 5 live-job types, and 6 skills through a physics-plausible simulator implementing the same protocol surface. Validation is software-only: all 120 hardware-independent tests pass deterministically, while 15 local-LLM integration tests pass 12-15 of 15 across runs because of model nondeterminism. A preliminary single-run probe across five open tool-calling LLMs indicates that the schema-bound interface can be driven locally by small open-weight models without cloud dependency; it is not a benchmark and has no confidence intervals. The method provides a bounded, testable, and reproducible foundation for closed-loop agentic instrumentation research.

</details>


### [150] [Can LLM Code Explanations Adapt to Diverse Problem-Solvers' Needs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17022)
*Andrew Anderson,David Piorkowski,Justin Weisz,Margaret Burnett,Kush Varshney*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究大语言模型如何根据不同的问题解决风格调整代码解释，并提出了13类语言适配方式及10个对应提示词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码解释模型虽可按用户年龄或专业水平调整输出，但尚未关注用户在解决问题时的认知与行为风格差异。若解释不符合个人的问题解决需求，可能降低工作效率并限制学习效果。

Method: 研究基于一种既有的包容性设计方法，覆盖5类问题解决风格，设计相应提示词；随后使用6个开放权重大语言模型生成1,072份代码解释，并通过自然语言处理方法归纳其中的适配特征。研究还结合既有文献、提示词设计和模型输出，为各类适配提供证据支持，并比较不同模型的适配频率。

Result: 研究形成了包含13种语言适配方式的分类体系，识别出不同模型在采用问题解决风格适配时的频率差异。论文还产出两类可复用成果：描述每种适配方式的陈述性规范，以及10个面向问题解决风格的提示词。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统考察了大语言模型代码解释对问题解决风格的适配，为构建更具包容性和个性化的代码辅助系统提供了设计框架、提示词资源与评估维度。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) code explanations can support people in solving code-related problems, yet prior work has shown that people have diverse problem-solving styles. If explanations fail to meet people's problem-solving needs, they may be less productive in their occupations and miss opportunities to learn and grow. Although some research has examined how LLMs can adapt their outputs to a user's age or expertise, no prior work has examined how LLMs can adapt their code explanations to people's problem-solving styles. To address this gap, we developed prompts from an established inclusive design method that considers 5 types of problem-solving styles, and we generated 1,072 code explanations from six open-weight LLMs. Using natural language processing techniques, we uncovered a taxonomy of 13 linguistic adaptations, with each adaptation supported by evidence from the literature, the prompts, or the LLMs' outputs. They also show which LLMs adapted their code explanations more frequently than others. This paper is the first to investigate problem-solving style adaptations in LLM code explanation, contributing two problem-solving adaptation approaches: declarative statements for each adaptation and 10 problem-solving style prompts.

</details>


### [151] [Where Does Agent Reliability Come From? A Cross-Benchmark Decomposition of Verification Loops, Specialist Models, and Scaffolding in a Production Enterprise Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17044)
*Arunabh Dastidar,the Leni Team*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文评估生产级企业智能体系统 Leni 的验证闭环机制。结果显示，系统相对其前沿基础模型在三个公开基准上显著提升，但主要增益来自脚手架、路由和任务专用模型；验证步骤本身的平均独立增益较小，却能挽救高难度、原本会失败的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步骤企业智能体在单次推理模式下，常因“决策后立即提交”而缺乏中间检查，导致计算错误、前提臆造及长工具链中的级联错误。论文旨在检验“执行—观察—比较—纠正”的验证闭环能否提升可靠性，并分离整体系统改进中验证模块的真实贡献。

Method: 作者在未经修改的生产配置下评测 Leni，并使用 SpreadsheetBench Verified、BullshitBench v2 与 GAIA 验证集分别考察静默计算错误、前提臆造和长链工具调用级联错误。研究通过消融实验拆分脚手架、路由、专用模型及验证步骤的贡献；记录端到端循环行为，估计验证器的捕获率、修复率和误报回归；同时以专用验证器替换实验检验观察者模型的重要性，并通过有效前提控制实验测量过度拒绝。

Result: Leni 在 SpreadsheetBench 上达到 91.25%，高于基础模型的 80.25%，提升 11.0 个百分点（n=400，p<0.001）；在 BullshitBench v2 上由 91% 提升至 98%，增益约 7—10 个百分点；在 GAIA 验证集上获得 75.2% 的 pass@1 和 83.0% 的 best-of-k，约提升 15 个百分点。验证步骤单独带来的平均增益约为 1.5 个百分点，但其作用集中于高分段边缘失败样本。实证混淆矩阵显示，验证器捕获率约 0.20、修复率约 0.75，且未观察到误报警导致的性能回退。将小型训练验证器替换为生成答案的前沿模型后，大多数纠错收益消失；在 100 个专家级有效前提问题中，未出现过度拒绝。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：生产智能体的可靠性提升不能简单归因于验证循环，系统级编排、模型路由和任务专用后训练模型贡献更大；但验证闭环对于修复少量关键失败案例仍具有独特价值。验证效果高度依赖专门训练、职责分离的观察者模型，而非让同一生成模型自行复核。其贡献还在于提供可量化的捕获—修复参数，为多步骤智能体可靠性的累积建模提供了经验依据。

Abstract: Multi-step enterprise agent tasks fail in a characteristic way: single-pass inference has no checkpoint between deciding an answer and committing to it. We study one production system (Leni) whose architecture installs such checkpoints: verification loops (execute, observe, compare, correct) staffed by lightweight task-specialized post-trained models. We evaluate the unmodified production configuration on three public benchmarks stressing distinct failure modes: SpreadsheetBench Verified (silent computation error), BullshitBench v2 (premise confabulation), and the GAIA validation split (cascade error over long tool chains). The full system improves over its frontier base model by +11.0 percentage points on SpreadsheetBench (91.25% vs 80.25%, n=400, p<0.001), +7 to +10 percentage points on BullshitBench (98% vs 91%, n=100), and roughly +15 points on GAIA validation (75.2% pass@1, n=165; 83.0% best-of-k). Our central contribution is a decomposition of that uplift: most of it comes from scaffolding, routing, and specialist models rather than from the verification step itself, whose isolated contribution is small (+1.5 points) but concentrated at the top of the score distribution, where it converts otherwise-failing tasks. We instrument the loop end-to-end, yielding an empirical verifier confusion matrix (catch rate about 0.20, fix rate 0.75, no false-alarm regressions) that grounds a compounding-reliability model. Specialist-swap ablations suggest that the loop's value depends on who observes it: replacing the small trained verifier with the generating frontier model eliminates most rescues. A valid-premise control shows zero over-rejections in 100 expert-level questions.

</details>


### [152] [MechMem-RTL: Reusing Verified Mechanism Memories for LLM-Based RTL Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17053)
*Mingyu Cheng,Junjie Gao,Jinhua Cui,Kuncai Zhong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出MechMem-RTL，一种面向时序RTL自动修复的机制记忆框架。该方法以验证器确认的故障机制和修复记录进行严格匹配，而非依赖任务文本相似度，在48个公开时序RTL任务和6种修复模型上显著提升了修复成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂时序逻辑错误通常涉及周期级执行语义，单纯依据自然语言任务描述进行RAG检索，难以准确表达触发条件、状态演化和约束关系，容易向模型提供不相关甚至误导性的历史经验。因此，需要一种能够复用可靠调试经验、并由形式化验证证据约束的检索与修复机制。

Method: MechMem-RTL将每条历史修复经验存储为经验证器确认的结构化记录，记录包含触发证据、诊断出的失效机制、局部修复动作、需保持的约束以及验证摘要。面对新故障时，系统仅在当前确定性验证器证据与历史记录的触发条件严格兼容时注入该记录；若不存在兼容记录，则只使用当前验证反馈进行修复。该设计避免了基于文本相似性的宽松检索造成的错误经验迁移。

Result: 在48个公开时序RTL任务、6个修复模型构成的288个任务—模型组合上，并限制每个任务最多两次修复尝试时，MechMem-RTL成功修复180个组合。相比之下，标准验证反馈修复成功109个组合，基于任务相似度的RAG成功107个组合。MechMem-RTL分别较两类基线多解决71个和73个组合。

Conclusion: MechMem-RTL表明，针对时序RTL修复，基于验证器证据和失效机制的严格经验复用优于基于自然语言相似度的检索增强方法。其核心贡献在于将历史调试知识编码为可验证、可兼容匹配的机制记录，从而提升复杂时序错误修复的可靠性与成功率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can automatically repair register-transfer-level (RTL) designs. However, fixing complex sequential logic errors requires reusing past debugging experience. Existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) relies on task-text similarity to provide this experience. This text-based approach often misguides the model because natural language poorly reflects cycle-level hardware execution semantics. To address this, we present MechMem-RTL, a repair framework that reuses verifier-confirmed repair records instead of text similarity. Each stored record strictly links trigger evidence, a diagnosed failure mechanism, a local repair action, preservation constraints, and a verification summary. For a new failure, MechMem-RTL injects a past record only when deterministic verifier evidence is strictly compatible with the stored trigger. Otherwise, the system uses only current verifier evidence. We evaluate MechMem-RTL on 48 public sequential RTL tasks across six repair models. With at most two repair attempts per task, MechMem-RTL successfully resolves 180 out of 288 task-model pairs, outperforming standard feedback repair (109 pairs) and task-similarity RAG (107 pairs).

</details>


### [153] [Teach it to stop, not just to click](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17136)
*Barada Sahu,Shivesh Pandey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文指出，计算机操作智能体的强化学习结果若仅报告单次运行，容易被上游随机性误导。跨数据抽样与随机种子的方差分解显示，性能波动主要来自数据抽样和运行非确定性；在困难任务中分布甚至呈双峰，因此均值±标准差不足以描述可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体计算机操作强化学习研究常以单次实验结果宣称改进，但这种做法无法区分真实方法收益与训练数据、随机种子或运行过程带来的偶然波动。论文旨在建立更可靠的多种子评测与报告范式，并检验修复策略是否能够稳定改善实际任务成功率。

Method: 作者在五个可由预言机评分的环境中，对35B计算机操作智能体实施验证器引导修复，并采用交叉的数据抽样×随机种子实验网格及自助法置信区间进行方差组分分解。研究比较固定单 token 修复、空间坐标点击和生成式字段填写等不同约束程度的纠错动作；同时使用Hartigan dip检验分析困难设置下结果分布的双峰性，并通过Fisher精确检验评估帧级修复向端到端任务成功的迁移。

Result: 评测方差接近于零，训练种子效应在各设置中不超过10%；主要变异来自数据抽样与运行非确定性。在最困难设置中，数据抽样贡献达到48%，运行结果呈双峰分布，单次运行约有30%概率落入失败模式。固定 token 修复最稳定，完成检测得分为0.97±0.06；开放式修复效果有限，坐标点击的视觉定位为0.53±0.35，生成式字段填写为0.14±0.04。仅当纠错动作是任务唯一剩余障碍时，帧级修复才显著转化为任务成功率提升。重复多种子实验还推翻了作者关于样本效率和能力边界的两项初始过度结论；压力测试表明，在相似条件下，文献常见规模的单次运行改进约有三分之一概率连方向都判断错误。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：计算机操作智能体强化学习应将多种子、跨数据抽样的重复实验作为常规报告要求，不能依赖单次运行或简单均值±标准差。修复能力具有明显层级性：动作越受约束，修复越可靠；而局部视觉或帧级改善并不必然带来端到端任务成功。作者同时发布了cua_reliability工具库，用于支持常规k种子可靠性报告。

Abstract: Agentic computer-use RL is reported in single runs, and those numbers mislead. Using verifier-guided repair of a 35B computer-use agent (CUA) across five oracle-graded environments, we show a repaired policy's success rate is dominated by upstream variance: a variance-components decomposition across three cells (crossed data-draw $\times$ seed grid, bootstrap CIs) finds evaluation variance negligible ($σ_{\mathrm{eval}} \approx 0$) and the training-seed effect small everywhere ($\leq 10\%$); instead it splits between the data draw and run-to-run nondeterminism, the data draw's share rising to dominant ($48\%$) on the hardest cell. There the run-to-run distribution is bimodal (Hartigan dip $p=0.07$, $k=10$), so a single run has roughly a 30% chance of the failure mode and mean$\pm$std is the wrong summary. On that footing, two findings hold. First, repairability is two-tier in how constrained the corrective action is: a single fixed token installs reliably (done-detection $0.97\pm0.06$), while open-ended corrections are only partial -- spatial-coordinate clicks (grounding $0.53\pm0.35$) and a generative field-fill ($0.14\pm0.04$). Second, the frame-level repair transfers to task success only when the corrective action is the task's sole remaining blocker (LinkedIn 8/20 vs. base 0/15, Fisher $p=0.006$). We caught two of our own over-claims -- a sample-efficiency curve and a 'grounding cannot be bought' boundary -- only by replicating across seeds; a stress test makes the stakes external: a single-run improvement of the size this field publishes would have the wrong sign roughly one-third of the time in a comparable regime. We release a library (cua_reliability) for routine k-seed reporting. The apparatus is, to our knowledge, the first multimodal segment-aggregated on-policy self-distillation (SA-OPSD) update on a real 35B CUA policy.

</details>


### [154] [SynH-Rank: Quality-Aware Code Search via Diverse Data Synthesis and Hierarchical Ranking Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17139)
*Keyu Liang,Haoye Wang,Yanfu Yan,Zhiyuan Wan,Zhongxin Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出SynH-Rank，一种面向代码质量的重排序框架。该方法通过大语言模型合成多样化训练数据，并采用分层排序学习，使模型能够将“高质量相关代码”优先于“低质量相关代码”和“不相关代码”。实验表明，该框架显著提升了代码质量偏好与复杂约束下的检索表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码搜索通常采用“召回—重排序”流程，重排序模型主要关注查询与代码之间的语义相关性，却忽略执行效率、内存开销、可维护性等实际开发中重要的非功能质量。开发者不仅需要相关代码，还期望代码满足资源优化和编码规范等质量要求。该问题的核心困难在于：质量标注数据稀缺，以及传统对比学习仅区分相关与不相关样本，无法表达不同质量层级之间的序关系。

Method: SynH-Rank包含两项关键设计：第一，利用大语言模型进行多样化数据合成，以缓解高质量代码标注样本不足的问题；第二，构建三级标签及分层排序训练目标，明确学习“高质量相关代码＞低质量相关代码＞不相关代码”的排序关系。论文还构建了包含4,209个样本对的新基准，并提出QPA指标衡量模型是否优先返回高质量代码，提出MCA指标衡量模型在多重复杂条件约束下的准确性。

Result: 实验显示，SynH-Rank相较基础重排序模型将质量偏好准确率QPA提升20.15%，相较仅使用相关性对比学习的训练方式提升15.80%。此外，该方法在传统相关性检索指标上也获得提升，并表现出更强的多条件泛化能力，说明质量建模没有牺牲基础语义匹配性能。

Conclusion: 该研究将代码搜索中的优化目标从单一语义相关性扩展为质量感知排序，并证明分层标签与分层排序训练能够有效建模代码质量差异。SynH-Rank为实用代码搜索系统提供了可行路径：通过合成质量数据和显式质量偏好学习，使返回结果更符合开发者对性能、资源使用和可维护性的实际需求。

Abstract: Code search enhances developer productivity by enabling efficient code reuse. Current code search systems often use a retrieve-then-rerank pipeline, where rerankers focus on modeling semantic relevance between queries and code. However, these rerankers overlook critical non-functional qualities like execution speed, memory usage, and maintainability, which are essential for practical software development. Studies reveal developers expect results to maintain high coding standards and satisfy specific needs, such as resource optimization, highlighting the importance of quality-aware code search.
  Achieving quality-aware code search faces two major challenges: the scarcity of quality-annotated datasets for effective training and the limitations of standard contrastive learning objectives, which fail to capture the ordinal relationships among high-quality, low-quality, and irrelevant code. Although contrastive learning excels in distinguishing relevant from irrelevant code, its binary objective does not support nuanced quality distinctions.To address these challenges, we propose SynH-Rank, a quality-aware code reranking framework that combines LLM-driven diverse data synthesis with hierarchical ranking training. SynH-Rank employs a three-level labeling scheme to explicitly model the hierarchy: high-quality relevant > low-quality relevant > irrelevant. Additionally, we introduce a new benchmark with 4,209 pairs and two novel metrics: Quality Preference Accuracy (QPA) for assessing prioritization of high-quality code and Multi-Condition Accuracy (MCA) for evaluating performance under complex constraints.Experimental results show SynH-Rank improves QPA by 20.15\% over backbone models and outperforms standard relevance-only contrastive training by 15.80\%, while simultaneously enhancing traditional relevance metrics and multi-condition generalizability.

</details>


### [155] [Prefactory: Automated Discovery and Application of Library-Adoption Refactorings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17211)
*Islem Bouzenia,Michael Pradel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 Prefactory：一种面向 Python 库采用重构的自动化方法。其核心不是对代码库反复调用大语言模型，而是先让大语言模型合成可执行的搜索启发式规则，再通过扫描、排序、重构生成和测试验证完成规模化重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将手写实现替换为成熟库 API 可减少代码量、提升惯用性并复用经过验证的实现，但这类机会通常仅在行为层面相似，缺乏明显语法匹配，传统静态检查工具只能覆盖少量人工定义模式。直接使用大语言模型虽具泛化能力，却成本高、可复现性弱，也难以系统化应用于大规模代码库。

Method: Prefactory 输入目标项目和目标库名称后，收集库元数据与项目词汇信息，并利用大语言模型生成词法检测器和结构检测器。检测器在扫描阶段定位候选函数；系统再依据启发式规则对候选项排序，对高排名函数调用大语言模型生成库采用重构。最后，Prefactory 结合项目原有测试及新生成的差分测试，对重构结果进行验证。

Result: 在 PrefactoryBench 上评估时，该基准包含来自 61 个开源 Python 项目、涉及 18 个库的 100 个真实库采用重构实例。Prefactory 在文件级检测到 75 个实例、函数级检测到 56 个实例；最强基线 Codex CLI 分别检测到 35 个和 32 个实例。在检测出的 56 个候选函数中，Prefactory 最终生成了 40 个通过测试验证的重构。

Conclusion: Prefactory 表明，将大语言模型用于一次性生成可执行搜索启发式，而非逐段代码重复推理，能够在降低推理成本、改善可复现性和支持批量扫描的同时，提高库采用重构的发现与验证能力。其主要贡献是把大语言模型的语义泛化能力与确定性的程序分析、候选排序和测试验证流程结合起来。

Abstract: Replacing hand-written code with library API calls is a common refactoring that can reduce code size, make code more idiomatic, and reuse well-tested implementations. Yet many library-adoption opportunities are hard to find automatically: the original code often does not mention the target library and may resemble the library API only in behavior, with little syntactic overlap. Existing tools, such as linters and static modernizers, cover only a small set of manually specified patterns. LLMs and LLM-based agents, on the other hand, can generalize to more patterns, but they are costly, difficult to reproduce and to apply systematically at scale. This paper introduces Prefactory, an automated approach for library-adoption refactoring in Python. The key idea is to use an LLM to synthesize executable search heuristics rather than relying on repeated LLM prompting over a codebase. Given a target project and a target library name, Prefactory collects library metadata and project vocabulary, then generates lexical and structural detectors. Prefactory executes the detectors during a scan phase to find candidate functions. It then heuristically ranks the candidate functions, generates refactorings for the highest-ranked ones using an LLM, and validates the results with project tests and newly generated differential tests. We evaluate Prefactory on PrefactoryBench, a benchmark of 100 real-world library-adoption refactorings from 61 open-source Python projects and 18 libraries. Prefactory detects 75 instances at the file level and 56 at the function level, compared with 35 and 32 for the strongest baseline (Codex CLI). From the 56 detected functions, Prefactory produces 40 test-validated refactorings.

</details>


### [156] [Specifying the Delegated-Autonomy Boundary: Requirements Engineering for Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17225)
*Chetan Arora,Andreas Vogelsang,Abbi Sharma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“委托自主性边界”概念，认为智能体系统的授权范围、监督方式与控制回收机制应被视为显式需求，而非隐含在提示词、工具模式或运行时策略中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统需求工程主要面向预测、推荐或确定性软件功能，难以充分处理智能体持续维护状态、规划并在外部环境中行动所带来的授权与责任问题。当前实践缺少对“何时应采用智能体、可委托哪些决策、需要何种监督”的系统化表达。

Method: 论文提出两类互补工件：Agency Justification Record（AJR），用于论证智能体相对于更简单方案的必要性；Agentic Delegation Policy（ADP），用于规定智能体的目的、权限、信息、协作、保障和演化要求。其中，权限被建模为分级的授权结构，以表达不同程度的自主性与人工监督。

Result: 论文通过两个对比性案例说明框架适用性：出院协调智能体与自动化代码审查智能体。案例旨在展示AJR如何支持智能体采用决策，以及ADP如何将授权边界、监督要求和控制回收条件转化为可管理的需求工件。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将智能体授权问题提升为需求工程中的一等对象。AJR与ADP为团队提供了评估智能体必要性、定义渐进式权限及明确监督和控制机制的结构化方法，但摘要未提供实证评估、工件模板细节或与现有需求工程方法的定量比较。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems do not just predict or recommend; they plan, maintain state, and act in external environments with varying degrees of autonomy. This changes the requirements engineering problem in a specific and under-addressed way: it introduces what we call the delegated-autonomy boundary -- the set of decisions about what may be delegated to the system, under what graduated authority, with what oversight, and how control is returned. Current practices bury these decisions inside prompts, tool schemas, and runtime policies, even though they are requirements-level commitments. This paper proposes two complementary artifacts. First, an Agency Justification Record (AJR) helps teams decide when an agent is warranted over simpler alternatives. Second, an Agentic Delegation Policy (ADP) captures what must be specified for safe and effective development: purpose, authority, information, coordination, assurance, and evolution. Crucially, authority in the ADP is modelled as graduated, i.e., a tiered structure. We illustrate the framework with two contrasting examples: a safety-critical hospital discharge coordination agent and an automated code review agent.

</details>


### [157] [A RFID Based Campus Wide Payment System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17233)
*Miraj Uddin Chowdhury,MD Khairul Islam Prime*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于RFID卡、树莓派、集中式数据库和Web界面的校园无现金支付系统，用于食堂消费、学费缴纳和图书馆罚款等场景，目标是提升支付便利性、实时性与管理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统校园支付方式通常存在现金处理不便、人工核对效率低、账户信息更新滞后及管理成本较高等问题。该研究希望利用低成本RFID硬件和网络化后台，实现统一账户管理与快速身份识别，从而改善校园服务支付体验。

Method: 系统采用RFID卡作为用户身份与账户关联载体，以树莓派连接RFID读卡器完成刷卡识别和交易请求处理；后端使用集中式数据库记录账户余额、交易流水及服务项目，并通过Web界面支持管理人员和用户查询相关信息。设计过程涉及面向对象的软件设计、数据库搭建、RFID硬件集成及基础安全机制设计，同时参考了智能停车和考勤等RFID应用场景以识别可改进问题。

Result: 摘要表明，该系统能够实现多类校园服务的统一刷卡支付，并支持交易和余额的实时更新。作者认为该方案相较传统支付方式具有较好的成本效益、操作效率和使用便利性，也具备向其他类似机构推广的潜力。但摘要未提供实验规模、交易成功率、响应时间、安全测试结果、成本量化数据或用户满意度等具体指标，因此其性能与安全性仍缺少可验证的实证支撑。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了RFID与树莓派构建校园支付平台的可行性，核心价值在于以相对低成本实现身份识别、统一账户和实时交易管理。后续工作可重点加强身份认证、卡片丢失与克隆防护、数据库访问控制、网络传输保护、并发与故障恢复测试，并通过量化实验评估系统在真实校园环境中的可用性和扩展性。

Abstract: This work titled "RFID Based Campuswide Payment System" introduces an innovative cashless payment solution for educational institutions. It uses RFID cards and a Raspberry Pi to enable hassle free payments for various campus services, such as cafeteria purchases, tuition fees, and library fines. A centralized database ensures real-time updates on transactions and account balances, accessible through a simple and user-friendly web interface. This work is involved in designing a secure system with object-oriented principles, setting up databases, and integrating hardware like RFID readers with a Raspberry Pi. The systems are proved to be a cost-effective and efficient alternative to traditional payment methods, enhancing convenience and security for students and administrators. The study also explored similar RFID applications, like smart parking and attendance systems, to identify challenges and improvements. Looking ahead, it envisions features like wearable RFID devices, voice-activated payments, and blockchain integration to boost security and usability. Results show that this system simplifies campus payments and has the potential for broader adoption in similar environments.

</details>


### [158] [A Large-Scale Measurement of AI Bill of Materials Completeness in Hugging Face Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17242)
*Md Erfan,Ahmed Ryan,Md Rayhanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文以约9.75万个公开Hugging Face模型仓库生成的AIBOM工件为样本，评估其机器可读AI供应链文档的完整性。结论是：AIBOM的必需结构字段覆盖充分，但模型卡、数据集、局限性、责任使用、环境影响及有意义描述等AI特定信息普遍缺失或不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练模型降低了ML密集型软件开发门槛，但模型仓库常缺少来源、许可证、训练数据、限制条件和外部引用等可机读信息，导致AI供应链透明度、可追溯性与治理能力不足。AIBOM旨在系统记录这些信息，因此有必要实证检验当前生成AIBOM的实际完整度。

Method: 研究以公开Hugging Face模型仓库为案例，对约9.75万个AIBOM工件进行分析。完整性评估包括四方面：必需结构与元数据字段是否存在；模型身份、许可证和外部引用是否被表示；模型卡中的数据集、局限性、安全风险评估及环境信息是否被捕获；以及文档覆盖率是否随任务类型、许可证可用性、数据集声明、模型家族和论文引用等仓库或工件特征而变化。

Result: 生成的AIBOM在规定的结构层面表现良好，必需字段得到完整覆盖。但在AI语义与治理相关文档上覆盖有限：模型卡内容、扩展元数据、责任使用信息、环境信息、模型局限性及具备实质含义的描述字段均较弱或缺失。说明现有自动生成机制更擅长填充格式化结构，而难以补全依赖仓库维护者提供的高质量模型文档。

Conclusion: AIBOM可以满足基础机器可读结构要求，但尚不能保证AI供应链文档在来源、用途边界、风险与可追溯性上的充分性。论文建议改进模型卡编写与维护实践，加强仓库级追踪机制，并引入自动化AIBOM验证，以提升AIBOM生成质量和实际采用价值。

Abstract: Pretrained machine learning (ML) models help developers build ML-intensive software systems without training models from scratch. However, model repositories often provide incomplete machine-readable documentation about model provenance, licenses, datasets, limitations, and external references, creating transparency and governance gaps across the AI supply chain. Artificial Intelligence Bills of Materials (AIBOMs) address these gaps by documenting AI artifacts, including models, metadata, licenses, datasets, model-card information, and external references. Taking public Hugging Face (HF) model repositories as a case study, this paper empirically investigates AIBOM completeness, defined as the extent to which repositories provide AIBOM-relevant information for machine-readable AI supply-chain documentation. We examine approximately 97.5K AIBOM artifacts to assess the extent to which generated AIBOMs: (i) contain required structural and metadata fields, (ii) represent model identity, license, and external-reference information, (iii) capture model-card documentation such as datasets, limitations, safety-risk assessment, and environmental information, and (iv) vary in documentation coverage across repository and artifact characteristics such as task, license availability, dataset declaration, model family, and paper reference. Results indicate that generated AIBOMs provide complete coverage of required AIBOM structure but limited AI-specific documentation completeness. Required fields are fully represented, but model-card, metadata, responsible-use, environmental, limitation, and meaningful-description fields remain weakly represented or missing across generated artifacts. Our findings motivate improved model-card practices, repository-level traceability, and automated AIBOM validation to advance the generation and adoption of more complete AIBOMs.

</details>


### [159] [IssueExec: A Test-Driven Approach for Localizing Software Engineering Issues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17286)
*Jiawei Liu,Yun Lin,Chenyan Liu,Yu Qian,Yiming Liu,Jiaxin Chang,Weinan Zhang,Linpeng Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出IssueExec：利用测试用例作为需求的可执行代理，通过“问题描述→测试→执行轨迹→代码”的两跳路径进行问题定位，在SWE-bench Lite上显著优于现有方法，并能提升自动修复流水线的实际解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统问题定位方法直接将问题描述与代码元素匹配，但自然语言需求与具体实现之间存在较大抽象鸿沟，导致语义对齐困难。论文认为测试套件同时连接需求行为与代码执行，是更合适的中间信息源；理论分析表明，测试可平均降低7.73比特的定位不确定性。

Method: 作者先在18个仓库上进行实证研究，验证测试对真实修改文件的覆盖率及两跳语义关联的有效性。随后提出IssueExec：其一，使用领域知识增强的测试表示，缓解问题描述与测试标识符之间的语义差距；其二，采用分层执行轨迹分析，过滤框架、基础设施等无关调用产生的噪声。最终依据相关测试及其净化后的执行轨迹，对候选函数进行排序定位。

Result: 实证结果显示，现有测试覆盖96.98%的真实修改文件；在82.4%的案例中，经由测试的两跳路径比问题描述与代码的直接匹配具有更强语义连通性。在SWE-bench Lite上，IssueExec取得最优性能，相比最强基线将函数级Recall@1提升41.57%。将其集成到Agentless自动修复流水线后，成功解决的问题数量提升17.72%。

Conclusion: 测试套件不仅适合验证修复结果，也可作为连接问题描述与实现代码的重要定位媒介。IssueExec通过改进测试语义表示和抑制执行轨迹噪声，有效将这一信息源转化为高质量函数级定位结果，并证明了其对下游自动程序修复任务具有实际价值。

Abstract: Issue localization, which identifies code locations requiring modification from issue descriptions, is a critical step in automated software maintenance. Existing approaches predominantly attempt to directly align issue descriptions with code elements, yet often struggle due to the inherent abstraction gap between the issue description and code implementation. Seeking alternative signals, our theoretical analysis suggests that test suites can serve as executable proxies for requirements, reducing localization uncertainty by 7.73 bits of entropy on average. A large-scale empirical study on 18 repositories validates this premise: existing tests cover 96.98\% of ground-truth files, and the two-hop pathway yields stronger semantic connectivity than direct matching in 82.4\% of cases. Despite their potential, leveraging tests for localization faces two key challenges: the semantic gap separating issue descriptions from test identifiers, and the substantial noise in execution traces from infrastructure code. To address these, we propose IssueExec, which bridges the semantic gap through domain-knowledge-enhanced test representations and filters noise via hierarchical trace analysis. Experiments on SWE-bench Lite show that IssueExec achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving function-level Recall@1 by 41.57\% over the strongest baseline. When integrated into the Agentless pipeline, IssueExec resolves 17.72\% more issues, demonstrating practical downstream benefits.

</details>


### [160] [Combining model checking with simulation-based techniques for protocol verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17453)
*Takanori Ishibashi,Kazuhiro Ogata*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出将模型检测与仿真关系结合，以缓解通信协议验证中的状态空间爆炸问题。核心思路是在较小、较抽象的协议模型上执行模型检测，再通过仿真关系将验证结果迁移到更具体、更大规模的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABP和SWP等协议的可达状态数量会随信道容量、窗口大小等参数增长而急剧增加，使直接穷举式模型检测不可行。需要一种能够保留形式化验证严谨性、同时避免在大型状态空间上直接搜索的方法。

Method: 作者将SCP、ABP和SWP建模为状态机，并按抽象程度由高到低排列：SCP、ABP、SWP。首先直接对状态空间较小的SCP进行模型检测以验证不变式；随后构造ABP到SCP的仿真关系，将SCP上的验证结论推广至ABP；最后组合SWP到ABP、ABP到SCP的仿真关系，从而将SCP的不变式验证结果进一步推广至SWP。

Result: SCP可通过直接模型检测完成验证。对于参数增大后直接验证不可行的ABP，论文利用其到SCP的仿真关系证明其满足不变式。对于状态空间更大的SWP，论文通过两段仿真关系的复合证明其满足相应不变式，避免了直接遍历SWP的大规模可达状态空间。

Conclusion: 该方法以小型抽象模型上的模型检测为基础，并借助仿真关系及其可复合性实现验证结果向具体模型传递。它能够有效降低大型协议形式化验证的状态空间负担，尤其适用于存在明确抽象层次和可建立仿真映射的参数化通信协议。

Abstract: Model checking is a powerful technique for verifying properties of systems or protocols, but it often suffers from the state space explosion problem. For protocols such as the Alternating Bit Protocol (ABP) and the Sliding Window Protocol (SWP), increasing parameters such as channel capacities or window sizes leads to a reachable state space that is infeasible for exhaustive model checking. To address this, we propose a technique that combines model checking with simulation relations. We focus on three protocols: the Simple Communication Protocol (SCP), ABP, and SWP. Formalized as state machines, these protocols are presented in decreasing order of abstraction: SCP, ABP, and then SWP, which results in a corresponding increase in reachable states. While SCP is verifiable through direct model checking without state space explosion, the direct application of model checking to ABP and SWP becomes infeasible as their parameter values increase. We demonstrate that ABP satisfies its invariant property by combining model checking on SCP with a simulation relation from ABP to SCP. Furthermore, we demonstrate that SWP satisfies its invariant property through the composition of simulation relations from SWP to ABP and from ABP to SCP, in conjunction with model checking on SCP. These approaches enable the formal verification of protocols with large reachable state spaces by conducting model checking on a significantly smaller state machine.

</details>


### [161] [PSASpotter: A Tool to Detect the Usage of Platform-Specific APIs in Python](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17462)
*Ricardo Job,Andre Hora*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出PSASpotter工具，用于检测Python系统中平台特定API的使用，并识别其是否受到跨平台防御性代码保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台特定API通常仅适用于目标操作系统或运行环境，在其他平台可能失效。自动发现这类调用能够帮助维护者识别可移植性风险，并为跨平台开发、测试和维护提供支持。

Method: PSASpotter对Python代码进行分析，定位平台特定API调用；同时检测调用是否位于防御性结构中，例如try/except异常处理块，或根据当前平台进行条件判断的if语句。工具还可用于收集生态系统层面的API使用数据，并从防御性代码中挖掘不可用API的替代实现线索。

Result: 摘要未报告具体的实验数据、准确率、召回率或大规模实证发现。其主要产出是可公开获取的PSASpotter工具，以及用于展示工具功能的视频资源。

Conclusion: PSASpotter为Python项目中的平台特定API识别和防御性使用分析提供了自动化支持，可帮助发现跨平台兼容性隐患，并为后续关于Python生态系统可移植性、替代方案和测试策略的实证研究提供基础。

Abstract: A platform-specific API is implemented for a particular platform (e.g., operating system), thus, it may not work on other platforms than the target one. Detecting the usage of such APIs is important for supporting software maintenance, as it allows maintainers to be alerted about APIs that could pose potential risks. This paper proposes PSASpotter, a tool to detect the usage of platform-specific APIs in Python systems. PSASpotter also identifies whether the platform-specific APIs are used within a defensive code, such as try/except blocks or if blocks that check the current platform. PSASpotter can support the development of novel empirical studies about the usage of platform-specific APIs in the Python ecosystem. Moreover, the defensive code detected by PSASpotter may contain alternative solutions for unavailable APIs, which can provide insights for software development and testing across multiple platforms. PSASpotter is available at: https://github.com/ricardojob/PSASpotter. Tool video: https://youtu.be/d3WyozTAKS8.

</details>


### [162] [CommitLLM: A Fine-Tuned Pipeline for Git Commit Message Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17532)
*Md Rafid Haque,Poojan Narendrabhai Patel,Meetkumar Vijaybhai Raychura*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出CommitLLM：一个基于微调小型语言模型、受限解码与确定性后处理的三阶段系统，用于从代码差异自动生成简洁且符合Conventional Commits规范的Git提交信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低信息量提交信息会削弱版本历史在代码审查、调试和新成员熟悉项目时的作用。直接使用通用大模型往往产生冗长、对话式或格式不合规的输出，因此需要面向结构化提交信息生成的轻量化方案。

Method: 系统首先使用CommitPackFT数据集对Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.2进行QLoRA微调；随后通过受限解码限制生成长度；最后采用确定性后处理移除对话残留并校验、修正Conventional Commits格式。该方案可在配备16GB显存的NVIDIA T4单卡上运行。

Result: 在50个样本的评测中，CommitLLM的格式合规率达到98%，显著高于原始Mistral的22%；平均输出长度由154.8个字符降至37.9个字符；LLM-as-a-Judge评分从5分制的1.97提升至3.68。作者进一步发现，确定性后处理带来的质量提升超过单纯微调模型的贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，对于提交信息等格式要求明确的结构化文本生成任务，将语言模型嵌入“微调+解码约束+规则后处理”的确定性流水线，可能比仅优化模型本身更有效、更可靠。结论受限于仅50个样本及以LLM裁判为主的评估，仍需在更大规模、多项目数据及人工评测中验证泛化性。

Abstract: Developers frequently write uninformative git commit messages such as "fix" or "update stuff", degrading the value of version-control history for code review, debugging, and onboarding. We present CommitLLM, a three-stage pipeline that generates concise, Conventional Commits-compliant messages from code diffs using a fine-tuned small language model. The system combines (1) QLoRA fine-tuning of Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.2 on the CommitPackFT dataset, (2) constrained decoding to enforce brevity, and (3) deterministic post-processing to strip conversational artifacts and enforce format. On a 50-sample evaluation, CommitLLM achieves 98% format compliance (vs. 22% for vanilla Mistral), reduces average output length from 154.8 to 37.9 characters, and improves LLM-as-a-Judge scores from 1.97 to 3.68 out of 5. Notably, the post-processing layers contribute more to quality improvement than the fine-tuning itself, suggesting that for structured-output tasks, treating the LLM as a component in a deterministic pipeline is more effective than optimizing the model alone. The entire system runs on a single consumer GPU (NVIDIA T4, 16 GB VRAM).

</details>


### [163] [BTOR2-Based C Program Verification via Hardware Model Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17622)
*Xinyu Zhang,Runxuan Fang,Ziqun Bao,Yechuan Xia,Jianwen Li,Geguang Pu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出C2Btor，将基于断言的C程序验证任务编码为BTOR2转换系统，使其能够复用硬件模型检查后端进行反例搜索与归纳安全证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序验证工具通常依赖特定中间表示和分析后端，导致验证算法与模型检查器难以跨框架复用。BTOR2已在硬件模型检查领域形成成熟生态，因此需要建立从C程序到BTOR2的有效转换路径。

Method: C2Btor以程序计数器表示控制流转移，以位向量和数组表示数据状态及内存对象，并将程序中的假设映射为BTOR2约束、将断言映射为坏状态属性，从而构造可由硬件模型检查工具直接处理的状态迁移系统。

Result: 在SV-COMP C ReachSafety基准和整理后的断言类别基准上，C2Btor正确解决263个任务，比采用有界模型检查配置的CBMC多解决101个任务。在位向量类基准中，C2Btor解决率达到75.5%，且未出现错误判定。

Conclusion: 将C程序验证转换至BTOR2是可行且有效的路线。该方法可引入硬件模型检查后端在字级状态迁移系统推理、反例搜索和归纳安全证明方面的能力，扩展C程序验证的工具与算法选择。

Abstract: Program verification tools often rely on specific intermediate representations and analysis backends, limiting the reuse of verification algorithms and model checkers across frameworks. In contrast, hardware model checking has developed a mature backend ecosystem, where standard formats such as BTOR2 support reusable algorithms for counterexample search and inductive safety proving. Applying these capabilities to C requires translating assertion-based programs into transition systems that hardware model checkers can directly process.
  We present C2Btor, a method for encoding such verification tasks into BTOR2 models. C2Btor uses a program counter to capture control transfers, represents data states and memory objects with bit-vectors and arrays, and maps assumptions and assertion checks into BTOR2 constraints and bad-state properties.
  We evaluate C2Btor on SV-COMP C ReachSafety benchmarks and a curated assertion-category benchmark suite, comparing it with representative program verification tools. C2Btor correctly solves 263 tasks, 101 more than CBMC configured with bounded model checking, and is especially effective on bit-vector benchmarks, where it solves 75.5% of the tasks with no wrong verdicts. These results show that the BTOR2 route allows C program verification to benefit from advances in hardware model-checking backends, expanding the available capability for counterexample search, inductive safety proving, and word-level transition-system reasoning.

</details>


### [164] [Integrating High-Level Requirements to Low-Level Tests with Machine-Readable V&V Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17686)
*Mansur Arief,Nur Ahmad Khatim,Ali Akarma,Ahmad Alfan Alfian Irfan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出开源框架 VNVSpec，将高层验证与确认（V&V）需求转化为机器可读、可执行的规范，并把需求、模块级指标、测试结果和审计证据连接起来，以支持可追溯的软件质量验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 pytest、JUnit、Jest 等工具擅长低层单元测试，但高层用户需求、工程要求与实际测试之间通常依赖人工维护，缺乏系统化可追溯性。对于 AI 系统和信息物理系统，仅有原始测试结果难以满足合规审计所需的“需求已被验证”的结构化证据。

Method: VNVSpec允许用户直接编写高层需求，或从基于公开标准构建的需求目录导入需求；框架随后检查需求质量，将需求分解为带有明确度量和验收准则的模块级需求，并通过可追溯性图将这些需求关联到测试结果。最后，框架汇总验证证据，生成判定结果和面向审计的报告。论文还讨论了其对黑盒 AI 模型及 AI 编程代理测试的扩展方式。

Result: 作者采用自应用方式持续评估框架：在持续集成环境中，VNVSpec依据自身的36项需求运行449个测试。实验表明，处理时间受限且随需求数量线性增长，作者据此认为该框架可扩展至约10,000项需求。代码、测试套件、需求目录和基准脚本已开源。

Conclusion: VNVSpec试图弥合系统工程中的高层V&V规范与软件工程中的自动化测试之间的断层。其核心价值在于建立从需求到测试证据、再到审计报告的可执行追溯链，为需要可验证合规证据的软件、AI和信息物理系统提供基础设施。

Abstract: Modern software teams have mature tools for low-level testing, such as pytest, JUnit, and Jest, which make it inexpensive to write unit tests and run them on every commit. Systems engineering, in parallel, has developed rigorous principles for design verification and validation (V&V), which has worked very well across engineering discipline to align user expecations and requirements with developers' deliverables. In practice, however, the two rarely connect, and the link between users' high-level requirements and the low-level tests that machines actually run is maintained by hand, if at all. This gap is increasingly costly for AI-enabled and cyber-physical systems, for which regulators now ask for traceable evidence that high-level requirements are met, while raw test results provide little of the structure such evidence requires. We introduce VNVSpec, an open-source framework that makes V&V specifications machine-readable and executable. With this framework, users state high-level requirements directly or import them from catalogs derived from published standards. Then, the framework checks requirement quality, supports decomposition into module-level requirements with explicit metrics and acceptance criteria, links these requirements to test results through a traceability graph, and compiles the collected evidence into verdicts and audit-ready reports. We evaluate the framework by self-application, in which it is continuously assessed in CI against its own specification of 36 requirements verified by 449 tests, completed within limited time which scales linearly and thus can handle up to 10,000 requirements. We also discuss how the framework extends to testing black-box AI models and AI coding agents. The framework, its full test suite, the catalogs, and the benchmark scripts are available at https://github.com/ai-vnv/vnvspec.

</details>


### [165] [KernelDiag: Agent-Based Root Cause Diagnosis for Kernel Crashes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17722)
*Weijing Wang,Zan Wang,Dong Wang,Haichi Wang,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出KernelDiag，一个面向Linux内核模糊测试崩溃的智能体式根因诊断框架。该方法通过统一对齐系统调用、日志、崩溃报告与源代码，并构建证据图进行结构化因果推理，实现故障方法定位及可解释诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内核模糊测试可持续发现大量崩溃，但根因分析仍主要依赖人工。现有基于大语言模型的根因分析方法通常针对分布式系统设计，难以适应内核场景中的低层证据稀疏、诊断信息异构、错误传播路径非线性以及需要细粒度方法级语义推理等问题。

Method: KernelDiag首先执行日志到代码映射，将系统调用、运行日志、崩溃报告等异构诊断工件与相关源代码关联。随后，框架利用面向不同工件类型的专门智能体，结合源代码语义与崩溃相关内核配置进行迭代推理。推理过程中发现的因果依赖被逐步组织为证据图，以支持故障方法定位，并生成具有因果链条的诊断解释。

Result: 在真实世界KGYM基准上的实验表明，KernelDiag在文件级和方法级定位任务中均优于现有先进方法。在缺少显式提示的困难设置下，其Top@k定位准确率最高可获得约4倍和2倍提升。人工评测与大语言模型辅助评测进一步表明，该框架生成的解释较为准确、连贯且具有可操作性。

Conclusion: KernelDiag通过将低层内核诊断证据映射至源代码层面的结构化因果推理，有效改善了内核崩溃的自动根因诊断能力。其证据图与多智能体协作机制不仅提升定位精度，也增强了诊断结果的可解释性，为自动化内核调试提供了具有实践价值的技术基础。

Abstract: The Linux kernel is one of the most complex software systems, where automated fuzzing continuously exposes thousands of crashes, yet root-cause diagnosis remains a manual and time-consuming bottleneck. Existing LLM-based root cause analysis (RCA) techniques, effective for distributed systems, do not readily generalize to kernel debugging due to sparse low-level artifacts, heterogeneous diagnostic evidence (e.g., syscalls, logs, and crash reports), and complex non-linear fault propagation that demands fine-grained method-level reasoning. To address these challenges, we propose KernelDiag, an agent-based framework for kernel root-cause diagnosis via structured causal reasoning. KernelDiag first aligns heterogeneous diagnostic artifacts through log-to-code mapping, and then employs artifact-specialized agents to iteratively reason over source-level program semantics and crash-specific kernel configurations. The inferred causal dependencies are incrementally organized into structured Evidence Graphs, enabling accurate faulty-method localization and causal explanations. We evaluate KernelDiag on the real-world KGYM benchmark. KernelDiag consistently outperforms state-of-the-art localization approaches at both file and method levels, achieving significant improvements in Top@k accuracy, including up to 4x and 2x gains in challenging settings without explicit hints. Furthermore, both human and LLM-assisted evaluations show that KernelDiag generates accurate, coherent, and actionable diagnostic explanations. Overall, this work lays the foundation for automated kernel root-cause diagnosis by bridging low-level diagnostic evidence with source-level causal reasoning.

</details>


### [166] [Persona-as-Configuration: Generative Stakeholder Reporting for Agricultural Floods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17774)
*Oliver Aleksander Larsen,Tiziano Santilli,Francesco Daghero,Mahyar T. Moghaddam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种将大语言模型用于确定性边缘推理系统报告生成的架构模式：生成层仅只读消费边缘决策日志，且利益相关者适配通过可版本化的人设提示模板实现，以保持底层系统的可重放性、可审计性与关注点分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性边缘系统会产生结构化决策日志，不同使用者需要不同形式的解释与报告。引入具有非确定性的LLM虽可提升报告的适配性，但可能破坏边缘决策平面的审计性、复现性和系统边界，因此需要一种明确隔离生成能力与确定性推理能力的集成架构。

Method: 作者定义两项架构不变量：其一是“单向消费”，即生成层作为确定性边缘平面的严格只读消费者，不得向其回写数据或控制信息；其二是“人设即配置”，即面向不同利益相关者的表达差异由版本化提示模板承载，而非运行时自由调整。该模式被实现为一个上下文感知仪表板层，读取既有基于边缘设备的农田积水检测系统所生成的JSON决策日志。论文进一步将生成可靠性缓解机制定位为配置层或中间件层的可扩展点，并采用结构化专家评审进行质量分析。

Result: 专家评审依据ISO/IEC 25010相关的五项质量维度对该模式给出了总体积极评价，其中“关注点分离”获得最强一致认可。结果表明，所提边界设计能够在不影响确定性边缘平面行为的前提下，为LLM报告生成提供可控的集成位置，并支持将可靠性治理机制模块化部署。

Conclusion: 该工作强调，LLM不应介入或反向影响确定性边缘决策链路，而应在只读、可配置、可版本管理的外围解释层中运行。该模式有助于兼顾面向不同使用者的自然语言报告需求与关键决策系统的审计、复现和治理要求。当前证据主要来自专家评审；面向农业领域实际使用者的终端用户评估仍是后续工作。

Abstract: Cyber-physical systems built on deterministic edge inference, such as on-vehicle flood detection for agricultural fields, produce structured decision logs that must be interpreted differently by heterogeneous stakeholders. Pairing such systems with large language models (LLMs) to generate stakeholder-specific reports introduces a tension: the generative layer is non-deterministic, while the edge plane must remain replayable and auditable. We propose an architectural pattern resting on two invariants: unidirectional consumption, in which the generative layer is a strict read-only consumer of the deterministic plane and never writes back, and persona-as-configuration, in which stakeholder adaptation is a versioned prompt-template artifact rather than runtime improvisation. We instantiate the pattern as a context-aware dashboard layer over the JSON decision logs of a previously published edge-based standing-water detection system, and analyse how the integration boundary admits standard generative-reliability mitigations as configuration- or middleware-level extension points. A structured expert review rated the pattern favourably across five ISO/IEC 25010-aligned quality dimensions, with strongest agreement on separation of concerns. End-user evaluation with agricultural stakeholders is planned for future work.

</details>


### [167] [(Over)Reliance on Test Agents in AI-Assisted Software Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17927)
*Eduard Paul Enoiu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文讨论软件测试中对AI测试代理的过度依赖风险，并提出分析框架，以在提升测试效率的同时保持工程师的判断力与测试证据可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI测试代理可缩短持续开发中的反馈周期，并提升测试工作的可扩展性与可维护性；但其输出不能仅因来自高能力系统而被默认视为正确、有效或可靠。工程师仍需能够审查生成的测试设计、结果及其证据基础。

Method: 论文通过三个理论视角展开论证：将软件测试视为认知性问题求解过程；将测试代理视为具有适应性自主性的实体；将测试设计论证视为使生成测试可审查的方法。在此基础上，提出用于收集测试代理工作流中过度依赖数据的框架，并归纳具体的过度依赖模式。

Result: 论文将过度依赖界定为两类相互关联的问题：一是自主性问题，即工程师可能将测试设计决策中的认知控制权让渡给代理；二是保障问题，即测试工件可能未经充分审查便被当作可信证据接受。论文给出了识别和研究这些风险的概念框架，而非报告实证实验结果。

Conclusion: AI测试代理应被视为需要审查与论证的协作工具，而非可直接替代工程判断的权威来源。通过记录和识别过度依赖模式、增强测试设计及证据的可审查性，可在加速测试的同时维持测试活动的判断质量和保障价值。

Abstract: AI-based test agents promise to accelerate software testing by shortening feedback loops in continuous development and improving scalability and maintainability. To realize these benefits, engineers must still be able to assess if agent outputs are useful, valid, and reliable, rather than treating them as credible because they come from a capable system. This paper argues that overreliance on AI in testing is both an agency problem, in which engineers may cede cognitive control over test design decisions, and an assurance problem, in which testing artifacts may be accepted as evidence without sufficient scrutiny. We develop this argument through three theoretical lenses: software testing as cognitive problem-solving, test agents as adaptively autonomous entities, and test design argumentation as a means of making generated tests reviewable. We propose a framework for collecting data on overreliance in test agent workflows and identify specific modes of overdependence. The goal is to support accelerated testing without weakening judgment or the assurance value of testing evidence.

</details>


### [168] [How Agent Skills Fail under Long Contexts: A White-Box Study in Code Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17937)
*Yue Xue*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文通过白盒代码审计案例检验长上下文中智能体技能要求的持续执行能力，发现上下文变长会显著降低任务通过率，但现有证据不足以支持普适的长度阈值；外部细化检查表比通用自检更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能包虽能向通用智能体提供操作流程和约束，但在长时间、多工具调用轨迹中，部分要求可能被遗忘、执行偏移或未被正确检查。作者希望定位这类失败最早出现的位置，并评估上下文规模和检查机制的影响。

Method: 在固定任务及24项产物检查的白盒代码审计流程中，改变周边上下文条件：短且干净的上下文、长且相关的上下文、等长但不相关的上下文。作者将首次可见失败归类为需求丢失、编辑漂移、检查失败，以及非智能体的评估器或运行时失败；同时比较通用自检与详细外部检查表，并使用双侧Fisher精确检验评估通过率差异。

Result: Codex与gpt-5.4-mini在10,991字符的干净上下文中10次运行通过8次，而在299,140字符的相关长上下文和等长无关长上下文中均仅通过3次，差异为50个百分点，但统计上仅呈趋势性（p=0.0698）。两种长上下文中的需求覆盖率仍超过92%，说明少量遗漏即可导致整体产物失效。另一项任务在短、长上下文下均全部通过，因而不能推导统一的上下文长度阈值。详细外部检查表通过率为10/10，显著高于通用自检的5/10（p=0.0325）。

Conclusion: 研究的贡献是针对白盒代码审计提出有限的失败分类，并给出实证案例，而非提出新的通用监控方法。长上下文可能降低技能约束的可靠执行，但影响具有任务依赖性；更具体、外置的检查清单可明显改善可靠性。编码智能体脚手架可通过缩小工作集提供帮助，但无法完全消除失败。

Abstract: Agent Skills package procedural instructions and checks for use by general-purpose agents, but loading a skill does not guarantee that every requirement remains active throughout a long tool-using trajectory. We study this problem in a production-derived, white-box code-audit workflow. Holding the task and 24 artifact checks fixed, we vary the surrounding context and classify where failures first become visible: lost requirements, editing drift, failed checking, or non-agent evaluator/runtime failures. Codex with gpt-5.4-mini passes 8/10 runs in a 10,991-character clean context but only 3/10 in both a 299,140-character relevant context and an equal-length irrelevant context. This 50-percentage-point difference is large but remains trend-level under two-sided Fisher tests (p = 0.0698). Requirement coverage nevertheless stays above 92% in both long conditions, showing that a few omissions can invalidate an otherwise complete artifact. A second task passes all clean and long runs, so the evidence does not support a universal context-length threshold. A detailed external checklist passes 10/10 runs, compared with 5/10 for a generic self-check (p = 0.0325). Coding-agent scaffolds may help by selecting a smaller working set, but they do not eliminate failures. We do not introduce context rot or a new general monitoring method; we provide a bounded failure classification and empirical case study for white-box code auditing.

</details>


### [169] [DepRepair: LLM-Based Source-Code Repair for Dependency Breaking Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17957)
*Shenghao Yang,Bo Lu,Yaochen Liu,Yu Kang,Qiongfang Zhang,Chetan Bansal,Saravan Rajmohan,Minghua Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出DepBench与DepRepair，聚焦第三方依赖升级导致的跨仓库兼容性修复问题。DepRepair通过筛选上游变更证据、定位消费者代码使用点，并按破坏性变更子类提供修复指导，在真实基准上取得较高的可执行测试通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖库升级常引入破坏性变更，消费者项目的适配通常依赖人工分析，成本高且易出错。现有方法要么仅识别依赖变更而不生成经验证的消费者补丁，要么假设故障证据与修复上下文都位于目标仓库中。实际依赖升级场景中，关键证据存在于上游发布说明和API差异中，且消费者测试未必能定位受影响代码，因此需要面向跨仓库证据的自动化修复方法。

Method: 作者构建DepBench基准，收录4个软件生态中的95个真实依赖更新实例，并为每个实例提供基于Docker的可执行判定器，使用消费者项目自身测试验证补丁。随后提出单次调用大语言模型的DepRepair：首先利用证据过滤器从上游发布说明与API差异中提炼相关变更；其次通过使用点定位器识别消费者仓库中受影响的调用或引用位置；最后利用面向破坏性变更子类别的修复指南生成针对性补丁。
