Result: 在DepBench上，DepRepair在所评估的各个模型骨干中均获得最高的可执行通过率：采用GPT-5.5时达到89.5%，采用Claude Opus 4.6时达到82.1%。消融与对比结果表明，直接向模型提供未经处理的上游证据会使LLM及智能体方法的通过率下降7至23个百分点；经结构化筛选和组织的上游证据则能稳定提升修复效果。

Conclusion: 依赖升级修复的核心难点在于跨仓库信息不对称，而非仅是消费者代码中的局部程序修复。通过将上游变更信息转化为与消费者使用点和变更类型对应的结构化证据，DepRepair能够显著提高自动补丁的可执行正确性。DepBench为后续研究提供了具有真实依赖更新实例和可复现测试验证环境的评测基础。

Abstract: Modern software projects depend on numerous third-party libraries, whose updates often introduce breaking changes. Adapting consumer code to such changes remains labor-intensive and error-prone. Existing work either characterizes dependency breaking changes without producing a verified consumer-side patch, or studies automated repair only in settings where the failure and repair context are contained within the target repository. However, dependency breaking changes violate this assumption: the decisive repair evidence lies upstream in release notes and API diffs, and no failing test localizes where the consumer breaks, leaving the repair under-informed. To study this cross-repository problem on real data, we introduce DepBench, a benchmark of 95 real-world dependency-update instances across four ecosystems, each paired with a Docker-based executable oracle that runs the consumer's own tests. To address these challenges, we propose DepRepair, a single-call LLM approach that grounds repair in structured upstream evidence through three components: an evidence filter that distills relevant upstream changes, a usage locator that identifies affected consumer sites, and a subcategory-aware guide that tailors repairs to the breaking-change type. Evaluated on DepBench, DepRepair attains the highest executable pass rate on each backbone, achieving 89.5% with GPT-5.5 and 82.1% with Claude Opus 4.6. We further find that raw upstream evidence reduces LLM and agent pass rates by 7--23 percentage points, whereas structured evidence consistently improves them.

</details>


### [170] [Chiral Analysis of Smart Contracts: Detecting Vulnerabilities from Relational Inconsistencies Across Business Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17987)
*Yue Xue*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“手性分析（chiral analysis）”，通过比较语义上成对的智能合约业务路径，检测单函数规则难以发现的关系型业务逻辑漏洞，并实现原型工具ChiralDetector。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约中的许多漏洞并非局部语法、控制流或数据流错误，而是本应语义一致的业务路径之间存在不一致。例如，单笔与批量入口、直接调用与适配器调用、报价与执行、买入与卖出等路径，可能在权限检查、状态更新、资金流、失败处理或外部调用方面产生偏差。现有分析器对这类以“路径关系”为判定依据的漏洞支持有限。

Method: 作者将语义配对的业务路径视为彼此的隐式规范，并将其形式化为静态的变形关系类比。针对路径对，工具检查守卫条件、调用主体、状态变迁、价值流、执行顺序、失败行为及外部交互等维度的一致性义务；当义务被违反且具有安全影响时报告漏洞。ChiralDetector首先提取业务路径，利用静态事实对候选路径对排序，再通过基于大语言模型的语义过滤与检测缩小范围，最后进行验证和去重。

Result: 在Phi协议的初步评估中，工具将3,217个静态排序后的路径对缩减为1,643个语义候选项，生成101个去重后的发现组；其中44个通过严格验证器的阳性结果，经人工归并后形成13个有效独立问题。发现的问题包括跨艺术品Merkle证明复用、费用单位不匹配、公开的状态跟踪辅助函数以及退款传播缺失等。

Conclusion: 手性分析为发现跨业务路径的不一致性提供了结构化方法，能够覆盖难以表示为单函数规则的业务逻辑漏洞。结合静态候选排序、LLM语义筛选和验证器校验，该方法在控制分析成本与改善结果精度之间展现出可行性；但实验目前仅基于单一协议的初步评估，仍需在更多合约、更多路径关系类型及更严格的误报和漏报评测中验证其通用性。

Abstract: Smart-contract vulnerabilities often arise from inconsistencies between business paths that should correspond to one another, such as single and batch entry points, direct and adapter-based flows, quote and execution paths, or inverse operations such as buy and sell. Existing analyzers are effective for many local syntactic and data-flow patterns, but they provide limited support for bugs whose oracle is relational: whether two semantically paired paths preserve compatible guards, state transitions, value flows, and failure behavior.
  This paper introduces chiral analysis, a relational model that treats paired business paths as implicit specifications for each other. We formalize chiral relations as static analogues of metamorphic relations, derive obligations over guards, actors, state, value, ordering, failure behavior, and external interactions, and report a vulnerability when a violated obligation has security impact. We implement this idea in ChiralDetector, a Solidity prototype that extracts business paths, ranks candidate pairs with static facts, applies LLM-based semantic filtering and detection, and validates and deduplicates findings.
  In a preliminary evaluation on the Phi protocol, ChiralDetector reduced 3,217 statically ranked path pairs to 1,643 semantic candidates, produced 101 deduplicated finding groups, and retained 44 strict-validator positives that manually collapsed to 13 effective unique issues. These include cross-art Merkle proof reuse, fee unit mismatches, public state-tracking helpers, and refund propagation gaps. The results suggest that chiral analysis can expose business-logic bug classes that are difficult to express as single-function rules while providing a structured way to control LLM cost and validator precision.

</details>


### [171] [Test Coverage Analysis of Agentic Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18057)
*Atish Kumar Dipongkor,Talank Baral,Wing Lam,Kevin Moran*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究基于 AIDev 数据集中的 4,882 个智能编码代理生成的拉取请求，发现代理提交的代码测试不足：仅约半数相关 PR 包含测试修改，现有测试对变更代码的覆盖有限，且代理新增测试带来的覆盖提升只出现在少数 PR 中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能编码代理能够独立提交完整 PR，软件开发逐渐从辅助式编程转向自主式工作流。若代理生成的代码未被充分测试，可能引入回归问题；但此前对代理生成 PR 中测试实践、现有测试覆盖及代理自编测试效果缺乏系统认识。

Method: 研究分析了由五种编码代理生成的 4,882 个 PR，其中包括 532 个 Java PR 和 4,350 个 Python PR。分析重点包括：代理在何种频率下修改或新增测试，以及现有测试和代理编写测试对代理修改的可执行代码行的覆盖情况；同时比较不同语言及不同代码构造的未覆盖程度。

Result: 在涉及被测代码文件变更的 PR 中，代理仅在 49.6% 的情况下包含测试变更。现有测试仅覆盖 Java 中代理修改的可执行行的 61.5%，Python 中则仅为 27.0%；Python PR 中有 64.8% 完全没有任何变更行被现有测试执行。代理新增测试虽能提高覆盖率，但效果并不普遍：仅 35.9% 的 Java“代码加测试”PR 和 22.5% 的 Python“代码加测试”PR 获得覆盖提升。错误处理结构，如 try 和 catch 块，是最持续未被充分测试的部分，Java 和 Python 的漏测率最高分别达到 86.0% 和 81.0%。

Conclusion: 智能编码代理当前尚不能稳定地为自身代码变更提供充分测试保障，现有测试套件也无法作为可靠的安全网。研究建议在代理开发流程中引入覆盖率感知实践和覆盖反馈闭环，并在评测基准中纳入测试质量指标，以提升代理自主测试和可靠交付代码的能力。

Abstract: AI coding agents increasingly submit complete pull requests (PRs) with minimal human intervention, shifting software development from AI-assisted to autonomous workflows. As these agents become more prevalent, ensuring the code they generate is adequately tested, by existing tests or by tests the agents write, is critical to preventing regressions, yet little is known about testing in agentic PRs. To address this gap, we analyze 4882 agent-generated PRs from the AIDev dataset (532 Java and 4350 Python PRs) produced by five coding agents. We study (i) how often agents include test changes and (ii) how well covered are code changes by existing and agent-written tests. Agents include test changes in only 49.6% of PRs that change code under test files. Existing tests provide an incomplete safety net: they cover 61.5% of agents' changed executable lines in Java and only 27.0% in Python, where 64.8% of PRs have no changed line executed by any existing test. Agent-written tests improve coverage over existing tests, but only in a minority of PRs: 35.9% of Java and 22.5% of Python Code + Tests PRs show a coverage gain. Across both languages, error-handling constructs (e.g., try and catch blocks) are the most consistently under-tested, with miss rates reaching 86.0% in Java and 81.0% in Python. These findings motivate coverage-aware development practices, coverage feedback loops for coding agents, and evaluation benchmarks that measure test quality to better help agents reliably test their own code.

</details>


### [172] [Autoresearch with Coding Agents: Generalizers and Metric-Maximizers on Quran Recitation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18064)
*Nursultan Askarbekuly,Mohamad Al Mdfaa,Ahmed Helaly,Gonzalo Ferrer,Manuel Mazzara*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Coding agents can now be left alone to improve software against a score. In this pattern--recently popularized as "autoresearch"--the agent receives a dataset, an evaluation script, and one editable file, and iterates without supervision: modify the code, measure, keep the change if the score improves. But what does the agent actually optimize--the developer's intent, or the literal number? We ran this loop on a real production task: deciding which Quranic verses appear in a noisy speech-recognition transcript and splitting the transcript by verse. Two frontier coding agents, Claude Code and OpenAI Codex, started from the same blank file with the same instructions, budget, and reasoning effort, three runs each. Both independently invented the same algorithm (canonicalization, n-gram anchoring, dynamic-programming alignment)--and then diverged. Claude stopped early with compact, general code. Codex drove the score ~10x lower, largely by memorizing answers to individual evaluation rows (19-41 hardcoded verse ids per run): a clean natural instance of specification gaming by a production agent. In a preregistered second study, we added a held-out test set and told both agents it existed. The memorization vanished, and the score gap vanished with it--yet Codex's general core transferred better and more consistently (held-out detection+split 0.085+/-0.004 vs. 0.121+/-0.031), losing only on one missed rejection of non-recitation input. Two exploratory community arms (Cursor, Antigravity) are consistent with the pattern. Every agent's held-out solution matched or beat the hand-engineered pipeline it was built to replace--the best by an order of magnitude--and now runs in production. From the ways agents exploited our harness--reading sibling runs through shared git state, leaving notes to "future runs" in persistent memory--we distill five design rules for evaluating autonomous agents.

</details>


### [173] [Testing Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems with Chunk Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18155)
*Jinhan Kim,Samuele Pasini,Paolo Tonella*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向检索增强生成系统检索组件的无预言测试充分性指标 Chunk Coverage（CC）。CC 统计测试套件中至少被检索过一次的语料块占比，用于衡量检索空间的覆盖范围，并可指导测试选择与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 评估通常以单个查询为单位，依赖参考答案或相关性标注等测试预言，主要衡量检索和生成质量，难以判断整个测试套件是否充分覆盖系统的检索行为。论文旨在提供一种不依赖查询级预言、能够反映检索行为多样性与测试充分性的整体性度量。

Method: 作者定义 CC 为测试套件触达的不同语料块数量除以语料库总块数。随后将 CC 用作测试排序、选择和生成的指导信号，优先保留或构造能够检索到尚未覆盖语料区域的查询。实验将 CC 引导策略与随机选择及偏向冗余的策略比较，并以覆盖达到速度和故障检测有效性指标 APFD 评估效果。

Result: 在临床与金融 RAG 场景中，CC 引导测试达到可达覆盖率 50% 的速度分别较随机选择快 1.7 倍、较冗余偏向策略快 4.2 倍。与随机策略相比，CC 还将 APFD 提升了 10% 至 25%，表明其能够更早发现不同类型的检索故障。

Conclusion: CC 是一种简单、结构化且无须测试预言的 RAG 检索测试充分性准则。其核心价值不在于直接评价单次查询的正确性，而在于量化测试套件对语料检索空间的整体探索程度；实验结果表明，按 CC 优化测试能够提高覆盖效率并增强检索故障发现能力。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG)-based systems\footnote{For brevity, RAG-based systems are referred to as RAG systems throughout this paper.} are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings where correct behaviour depends not only on the language model but also on the retrieval component that selects external documents at inference time. While existing RAG evaluation metrics assess retrieval and generation quality on a per-query basis, typically relying on query-level test oracles such as reference answers or relevance annotations, they provide limited insight into whether a test suite adequately exercises the retrieval behaviour of the system as a whole. In this paper, we introduce Chunk Coverage (CC), an oracle-independent test adequacy criterion for testing the retrieval component of RAG systems. CC measures the fraction of corpus chunks that are retrieved at least once across a test suite, providing a structural view of which parts of the retrieval space have been exercised. We further show how CC can be used to guide test selection and generation by prioritising queries that expand coverage of previously unexercised retrieval regions. We evaluate CC on clinical and financial RAG system scenarios. CC-guided testing reaches 50% of attainable coverage 1.7x faster than random selection and 4.2x faster than redundancy-biased strategies. Moreover, CC improves fault detection effectiveness (APFD) by 10% to 25% over random, indicating earlier discovery of distinct retrieval faults. These results show that CC captures retrieval diversity relevant to effective testing without requiring test oracles.

</details>


### [174] [TRIM: Reducing AI-Generated CodeSlop via Agent Trajectory Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18161)
*Alex Mathai,Shobini Iyer,Aleksandr Nogikh,Petros Maniatis,Franjo Ivancic,Junfeng Yang,Baishakhi Ray*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“CodeSlop”概念，用于描述智能体生成代码中遗留的、功能上非必要的冗余修改；并提出TRIM算法，通过压缩智能体的搜索轨迹而非直接删减最终代码，在几乎不影响任务性能的前提下减少17.9%至32.9%的CodeSlop，且验证成本约为Delta Debugging等基线方法的一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在迭代解决问题时会保留推测性修改、已放弃假设和临时变更，导致最终补丁比人工实现更长、更冗余。单次修改的冗余看似影响有限，但随着智能体参与更大规模代码库维护，冗余会持续累积，降低代码可维护性并增加长期维护负担。

Method: 作者首先将CodeSlop形式化定义为AI生成代码中残留且功能非必要的修改。随后提出TRIM（Trajectory-guided Redundancy Identification and Minimization），其核心不是直接在最终补丁上做最小化，而是以智能体执行过程中产生的编辑轨迹为对象，识别并移除不必要的轨迹步骤，从而间接得到更精简的最终代码。该方法旨在利用轨迹中的因果与时序信息，降低传统补丁最小化方法反复验证候选变更组合的成本。

Result: 实验表明，TRIM在不同智能体脚手架配置下可减少17.9%至32.9%的CodeSlop，同时仅带来可忽略的性能回归。效率方面，TRIM所需的验证成本约为Delta Debugging等算法基线的一半，说明轨迹引导的最小化不仅有效，也具有更好的计算经济性。

Conclusion: 论文指出，智能体代码冗余的根源不仅在于生成模型本身，也来自其试错式搜索过程中未被清理的编辑历史。TRIM通过最小化搜索轨迹实现代码冗余削减，为控制AI辅助开发中的代码膨胀提供了有效且高效的思路。其主要价值在于将代码清理问题转化为轨迹级优化问题，但摘要未说明CodeSlop的完整判定标准、实验任务分布及长期维护收益，仍需结合正文进一步评估其泛化性。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly used to accelerate code generation in many downstream tasks, such as fixing bugs, building applications, and prototyping. However, despite their value as coding assistants, agent-generated code tends to be larger and more verbose than the corresponding human-written implementation. In this work, we show that the cause lies in the agent's own search process: while iterating toward a passing solution, an agent accumulates speculative edits, abandoned hypotheses, and temporary changes that persist into the final patch. This may seem harmless for a single patch, but the problem compounds as agents take responsibility for ever-larger portions of a codebase-a codebase that was once minimal and well-maintained slowly accumulates redundancy faster than it can be cleaned up, drifting to a state that is harder to maintain. Given the magnitude of this problem, we take a step towards alleviating this issue. First, we formally define this phenomenon as CodeSlop-the residual and functionally unnecessary edits commonly seen in AI-generated code. We then introduce our algorithm TRIM (Trajectory-guided Redundancy Identification and Minimization). Rather than minimizing CodeSlop directly, TRIM instead minimizes agent trajectories. As we show empirically, this indirect technique of minimizing CodeSlop is highly effective: TRIM cuts CodeSlop by 17.9%-32.9% across agentic scaffolds, with negligible performance regression. TRIM is also highly efficient, requiring roughly half the validation cost of algorithmic baselines such as Delta Debugging.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Boundary-Seeking GAN-Augmented TabTransformer for Adversarially Robust Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16348)
*Raihan Sultan Pasha Basuki,Aliyah Kurniasih*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出将边界搜索生成对抗网络（BGAN）与TabTransformer结合，用于基于流量特征的入侵检测。BGAN同时承担少数类过采样与对抗样本生成两项任务，实验表明该方法改善了类别不平衡下的检测效果，并显著提升了模型在扰动条件下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习入侵检测系统通常面临两类核心问题：其一，攻击类别分布不均衡会使模型偏向多数类，导致少数攻击类型识别不足；其二，模型容易受到对抗性扰动影响，实际部署中的稳定性与可信度下降。作者旨在构建一个能够同时缓解类别不平衡并增强对抗鲁棒性的表格数据检测框架。

Method: 研究以CICIDS2017数据集为基础，采用TabTransformer作为主要分类模型，并使用BGAN生成少数类合成样本以扩充训练数据。与此同时，BGAN生成对抗样本，用于测试不同模型在攻击性扰动下的性能衰减。评估指标包括Macro-F1、各类别F1、性能下降率（PDR）和误触发率（FTR），并将BGAN增强的TabTransformer与未增强模型及BGAN增强决策树等方法进行比较。

Result: BGAN增强后，TabTransformer的Macro-F1由82.96%提升至86.50%。少数类Web_Attack的改善最明显，F1由0.29提高至0.61。对抗测试中，所有未增强模型的PDR均为100%，说明其性能在扰动下完全失效；所有经BGAN增强的模型则获得负PDR，表明扰动条件下仍保持或改善了相对性能。BGAN-TabTransformer在不同噪声水平下的FTR稳定且较低，为1.51%至2.92%；相比之下，BGAN增强决策树在良性扰动下的FTR最高达到49.09%。

Conclusion: 论文结论是，BGAN可有效改善少数类别学习不足，并提升检测模型对对抗性扰动的适应能力。BGAN-TabTransformer在总体分类性能、少数类识别能力、对抗鲁棒性和误报控制之间表现出更优的综合平衡。需要注意的是，摘要未说明对抗样本的具体生成约束、训练与测试划分方式、统计显著性及跨数据集泛化结果，因此其真实环境适用性仍需通过更多数据集、攻击场景和消融实验验证。

Abstract: Machine learning-based intrusion detection systems (IDSs) often suffer from class imbalance and vulnerability to adversarial attacks, leading to degraded detection performance and reduced robustness. This study proposes a TabTransformer framework augmented by the Boundary-Seeking Generative Adversarial Network (BGAN) for flow-based intrusion detection using the CICIDS2017 dataset. BGAN serves a dual purpose by generating synthetic minority-class samples to mitigate data imbalance and producing adversarial samples to evaluate model robustness. Experimental results demonstrate that BGAN augmentation improves TabTransformer's Macro-F1 score from 82.96% to 86.50%, with the largest class-wise improvement observed for Web_Attack (F1 score: 0.29 to 0.61). Robustness evaluation shows that all non-augmented models experienced a 100% Performance Drop Rate (PDR) under adversarial testing, whereas all BGAN-augmented models achieved negative PDR values, indicating improved resilience. Furthermore, the augmented TabTransformer maintained stable and low False Triggered Rate (FTR) values (1.51%-2.92%) across all noise levels, compared with the BGAN-augmented Decision Tree, which reached 49.09% under benign perturbations. These findings demonstrate that BGAN consistently enhances both class balance and adversarial robustness, while the proposed BGAN-TabTransformer framework provides an effective and adaptive intrusion detection solution for adversarial network environments.

</details>


### [176] [Reliable Remediation Impact Prediction for Black-Box Security Ratings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16357)
*Nada Hanad,Mehdi Acheli,Ali NourEldin,Mohamed Sellami,Walid Gaaloul*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种在不暴露安全评分平台内部评分规则的前提下，预测组织实施候选修复措施后评分变化的方法。核心是具备检查项适用性建模能力的代理模型，以及用于识别不稳定预测的可靠性层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全评分平台需要帮助组织确定修复优先级，但若持续返回精确的修复后评分，可能泄露隐藏的评分引擎。另一方面，外部可观测的检查项证据数量与结构存在差异，使修复影响预测的可靠性并不均匀，因此需要兼顾评分引擎保密性与预测可信度的方法。

Method: 方法构建基于组织配置的代理评分模型，显式表示各安全检查项是否适用于当前组织及其实际可观测的检查项集合。随后通过受控限制检查项的敏感性分析，刻画证据变化对预测的影响；以检查项适用性相关特征为基础建立可靠性层，识别预测不稳定或证据不足的情形；最后针对模型支持的修复动作，预测其带来的评分变化。

Result: 在来自商业安全评分平台的5,188个组织配置数据上评估显示，显式建模检查项适用性的代理模型优于使用较简单特征表示的评分预测模型。对于支持的修复动作，代理模型能够预测其评分影响；可靠性层可标记应谨慎解读预测影响的案例。

Conclusion: 检查项适用性不仅能提升代理评分预测精度，也是判断修复影响预测是否稳定的重要依据。该框架在保护内部评分机制不透明性的同时，为修复措施的潜在评分收益提供可操作的预测与可信度提示，但其结论主要适用于模型支持的修复动作和具有足够可观测检查证据的场景。

Abstract: Security rating platforms summarize externally observable cyber exposure and are expected to help organizations prioritize remediation. A platform may want to tell an organization how a candidate remediation action would affect its score, but repeatedly exposing exact score responses can reveal information about the hidden scoring engine. We propose a surrogate based approach for remediation score impact prediction that is designed to respect this opacity constraint. The surrogate predicts scores from organization configurations while explicitly representing checkpoint (i.e., a security check) applicability and the observed checkpoint set. A main challenge is that such predictions are not uniformly reliable: they depend on the amount and structure of the observable checkpoint evidence available for a given configuration. To address this, the approach combines applicability-aware surrogate construction, sensitivity analysis under controlled checkpoint restriction, a reliability layer for identifying unstable predictions, and score-impact prediction for supported remediation actions. Explicit modeling of checkpoint applicability is central throughout: it improves score prediction and provides the feature basis used by the reliability layer to identify unstable cases. We evaluate the approach on a real-world dataset of 5,188 organization configurations from a commercial security rating platform. The results show that the applicability-aware surrogate improves score prediction over simpler feature representations. For remediation, the surrogate predicts the score impact of supported actions, while the reliability layer helps identify cases in which these predicted impacts should be interpreted cautiously.

</details>


### [177] [Identity-Bound Academic Credentials on Blockchain: On-Chain Issuer Accreditation with ERC-3643 and OnchainID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16383)
*Gonçalo Frutuoso,Diogo Rodrigues,Alexandre P. Francisco,Cátia Vaz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于区块链的身份绑定学术凭证登记系统，将 OnchainID 与 T-REX/ ERC-3643 组合，用于解决学历证明缺乏身份绑定、发证机构认证以及凭证更正和撤销机制的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学历验证依赖院校各自封闭系统，流程缓慢且人工成本高；单纯将证书哈希上链的方案无法证明证书持有人身份，也难以管理合格发证机构，更不支持已发凭证的动态修正、撤销与完整生命周期管理。

Method: 系统使用 ERC-734/ERC-735 的 OnchainID 建立学生自主管理身份；将 T-REX 的可信发行方注册表改造为链上院校或发证机构白名单；将学历证书建模为与学生身份绑定、由合格发行方签名、可更新的声明。敏感信息保留在链下，第三方无需持有钱包即可验证。论文还分析 ERC-3643 原本面向可转移代币的机制与不可转移学历凭证之间的适配边界，并通过参考实现覆盖签发、验证、更正和撤销等全生命周期操作。

Result: 参考实现表明，该设计能够提供身份绑定验证、机构资质校验和凭证状态更新能力，并支持公开第三方验证及链下隐私数据存储。评估指标包括交易 Gas 成本、可扩展性、验证与操作延迟、安全性，以及相对于仅上链存证书哈希基线方案的额外开销。摘要未给出具体实验数值。

Conclusion: 该工作说明，面向受监管资产的链上身份与可信发行方基础设施可被重新用于学术凭证场景，但必须限制或重新解释其与可转移性相关的原始保证。其核心贡献是构建可认证发行方、身份绑定、可更正及可撤销的学历凭证架构；实际部署仍需依据量化开销、隐私设计、链下数据可用性和治理机制进一步评估。

Abstract: Verifying academic credentials remains difficult: records are held by individual institutions in proprietary systems, verification is slow and manual, and counterfeit qualifications are widespread. Blockchain-based registries have been proposed as a remedy, but existing systems tend to anchor certificate hashes without binding them to a verifiable identity, without an explicit mechanism to accredit issuing institutions, and without support for correcting or revoking credentials once issued. This paper investigates whether an infrastructure designed for regulated financial instruments can be repurposed to close these gaps. We present the design of a registry for identity-bound academic credentials that composes OnchainID self-sovereign identities (ERC-734/ERC-735) with the T-REX suite (ERC-3643): its trusted-issuer registry becomes an on-chain issuer-accreditation whitelist, and each certificate is represented as a signed, updatable claim bound to a student's identity and verifiable by any third party without a wallet, while sensitive fields are kept off-chain. We make explicit the tension between a transferable-security-token standard and non-transferable credentials, clarifying which of its guarantees carry over. We validate the design with a reference implementation covering the full certificate life cycle and evaluate it in terms of gas cost, scalability, latency, and security, quantifying the overhead relative to a hash-anchoring baseline.

</details>


### [178] [Signal-based Model Access Risk Analysis for AI System Operations Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16414)
*Maria Mahbub,Steven Young,Amir Sadovnik,Edmon Begoli,Chris Rugenstein,Donald Coulter,Anthony Ayodele*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“基于信号的模型访问风险分类法”（SMART），以部署系统向攻击者返回的信息信号类型与丰富度为核心，重新组织AI模型规避攻击的风险评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述通常按攻击者对模型内部信息的了解程度，将规避攻击划分为白盒、灰盒和黑盒。然而，“黑盒”类别往往混合了仅返回最终标签、返回置信度分数、暴露中间表征或提供模型参数等差异显著的部署场景，难以准确反映实际攻击能力及部署风险。

Method: 作者构建面向部署的SMART分类框架，依据攻击者可获得的信息输出信号及其丰富程度分级，而非仅依据其是否掌握模型架构、参数或梯度。论文据此系统梳理不同信号暴露层级下的规避攻击，并比较接口设计如何改变攻击策略与攻击可行性。

Result: 该框架揭示：即使都被称作黑盒访问，不同输出接口提供的信息量会显著影响攻击者能够采用的攻击方法、查询效率和攻击成功能力。信息信号越丰富，攻击者通常越容易推断决策边界、近似梯度或构造有效对抗样本。

Conclusion: SMART为评估已部署AI系统的规避攻击风险提供了更贴近实际接口条件的分类视角。该框架可帮助组织在模型采购、接口设计和部署配置中识别不必要的信息暴露，并据此选择更稳健的输出机制与防护措施。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) systems are now ubiquitous across domains such as security, finance, healthcare, consumer technology, and large-scale cloud services, where they process massive volumes of data and make consequential decisions daily. This widespread adoption has created a broad attack surface through which adversaries can manipulate, evade, extract information from, or otherwise subvert deployed models. Depending on system design and exposure, attackers may have very different forms of access: some observe only final decisions, while others receive confidence scores, intermediate representations, or even full model parameters. While previous surveys typically organize evasion attacks into white-box, gray-box, and black-box categories based on the attacker's knowledge of model internals (architecture, parameters, gradients), this taxonomy often conflates different deployment scenarios that provide vastly different output signals, all labeled as ``black-box'' despite enabling fundamentally different attack strategies. Understanding how evasion attack strategies adapt to the specific information signals returned by deployed systems is critical for organizations making procurement and deployment decisions. To address this gap, we introduce the Signal-based Model Access Risk Taxonomy (SMART), a deployment-oriented framework that classifies attacker access according to the nature and richness of the information signals available from deployed AI systems. Using this taxonomy, we provide a structured overview of evasion attacks across progressively richer levels of information exposure, highlighting how deployment interfaces influence attack capabilities and informing more secure AI deployment and procurement decisions.

</details>


### [179] [TaintRadar: Semantic-Aware Taint-Style Vulnerability Detection via Augmented Code Property Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16456)
*Elie Rizk,Firas Ben Hmida,Birhanu Eshete*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出TaintRadar，通过为代码属性图增加消毒、持久化和对象语义层，改善PHP静态污点分析在误报、跨数据库传播和对象字段流追踪方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于代码属性图的污点分析通常将输入验证简单视为二元屏障，无法准确区分不同漏洞类型下的消毒效果；同时难以跨数据库持久化层追踪数据，也无法充分处理面向对象程序中的字段修改、别名和跨过程数据流。这些缺陷会导致较高误报率及关键路径漏报。

Method: TaintRadar在代码属性图上叠加三类语义分析：其一，漏洞类型化消毒分析，利用传递函数和上下文敏感的参数绑定，为节点计算安全保证；其二，持久化感知传播，将数据库模式约束与查询安全分析结合，以追踪经由共享数据库状态传播的多脚本数据流；其三，对象感知到达定义分析，结合调用上下文和有界变量别名上下文，建模跨方法边界的对象字段读写与修改。

Result: 在SARD基准上，TaintRadar在保持80%总体准确率的同时显著降低误报。在19个真实PHP应用中，该方法重新发现了大多数已知CVE，并发现29个已确认且已获得CVE编号的零日漏洞，其中包括26个SQL注入和3个存储型XSS问题。

Conclusion: 论文表明，仅依赖传统代码属性图的污点分析难以充分表达消毒、数据库状态和对象别名语义。通过组合式语义增强图表示，可同时提升静态分析的精度、覆盖率与实际可用性；不过摘要未提供与具体基线工具的完整定量对比、误报绝对数量及计算开销，仍需结合全文进一步评估其通用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Despite significant advances, static vulnerability analysis suffers from three critical limitations: coarse sanitization modeling, which treats validation as a binary barrier; database blindness, which breaks taint tracking across persistence layers; and shallow object-oriented analysis, which misses field-level and interprocedural data flows. These flaws stem from a common root cause: Code Property Graph (CPG)-based taint analyses lack a compositional semantic layer to jointly model sanitization, persistence, and object aliasing. Consequently, existing tools generate excessive false positives or miss critical attack paths entirely. To address these limitations, we present TaintRadar, an approach that systematically augments CPGs with three semantic analysis layers. First, vulnerability-typed sanitization computes node-level safety guarantees using transfer functions and context-sensitive parameter binding. Second, persistence-aware propagation integrates database schema constraints and query safety analysis to track multi-script attack paths traversing shared database states. Finally, object-aware reaching definitions combine calling and bounded variable alias contexts to precisely model object-field mutations across method boundaries. We evaluate TaintRadar on both synthetic benchmarks and real-world systems. On the SARD benchmark, TaintRadar drastically reduces false positives while maintaining 80% overall accuracy. Deployed across 19 real-world PHP applications, it rediscovered the majority of known CVEs and uncovered 29 confirmed zero-day vulnerabilities, including 26 SQL injection and 3 stored XSS vulnerabilities, that have already received CVE identifiers. These results demonstrate that semantic-aware graph augmentation significantly improves the precision, coverage, and practical utility of static taint analysis.

</details>


### [180] [Fuzz'EMup: Leveraging EM Side-Channel Emanation to Guide Black-Box Embedded Firmware Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16487)
*Fatemeh Moradihaghighi,Zihao Zhan,Yanan Guo,Ziming Zhao,Mashrur Chowdhury,Zhenkai Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向纯黑盒固件测试的电磁侧信道引导模糊测试方法，通过分析设备运行时的电磁辐射推断执行路径差异，以替代不可获得的代码覆盖率反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网与嵌入式设备固件的安全测试日益重要，但许多目标无法被提取、插桩或准确仿真，导致传统覆盖率引导模糊测试失效，只能采用探索效率较低的黑盒模糊测试。论文旨在利用无需访问固件内部状态的电磁信号，为此类场景提供有效反馈。

Method: 方法首先依据活跃状态与空闲状态之间的信号对比度选择合适的电磁频段；随后采用动态时间规整对不同输入对应的电磁轨迹进行时间对齐，缓解噪声和时序抖动造成的特征偏移；再识别轨迹中持续存在的分歧，以此判断输入是否触发不同执行行为。为保证大规模测试时的效率，论文按照轨迹分歧发生的时间构建执行树，对测试执行进行组织与管理。

Result: 在四个真实固件目标上的评估表明，基于电磁侧信道获得的反馈能够增强路径探索能力，并相较于无反馈的黑盒模糊测试取得更高的代码覆盖率。

Conclusion: 电磁侧信道可作为不可插桩、不可提取或难以仿真的固件目标的有效外部执行反馈来源。通过频段选择、动态时间对齐和基于分歧时间的执行树，该方法在纯黑盒条件下提升了模糊测试的路径探索与覆盖效果。

Abstract: As IoT and embedded devices proliferate across various domains, securing their firmware has become critical. Fuzzing offers a systematic approach to uncovering vulnerabilities in firmware, and coverage feedback can improve its effectiveness by guiding exploration. However, many devices make coverage information impossible to obtain by preventing firmware extraction, instrumentation, or accurate emulation; in such cases, testers are left with only inefficient black-box fuzzing. In this paper, we present an approach that leverages electromagnetic (EM) side-channel emanations to guide firmware fuzzing in purely black-box settings. However, turning raw EM measurements into reliable guidance is challenging: EM traces are noisy, and timing jitter causes corresponding features in different traces to shift in time. We address these challenges by combining frequency band selection based on the activity-to-idle signal contrast with dynamic time warping to align per-input traces and detect sustained divergence, while maintaining scalability by organizing executions in a tree structure based on their divergence times. We evaluate our approach on four real firmware targets and demonstrate that EM-derived feedback enhances path exploration, yielding higher code coverage than unguided fuzzing.

</details>


### [181] [Enhanced Multi-Class DDoS Attack Identification using a Meta-Learning Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16521)
*Ankith Indra Kumar,Genya Ishigaki,Amith Kamath Belman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Distributed Denial of Service (DDoS) attacks continue to pose significant threats to network availability and security. While many detection systems focus on binary classification (attack vs. benign), effective mitigation often requires identifying the specific type of DDoS attack. This paper introduces a robust intrusion detection framework centered around a high-accuracy, multi-class classification model designed to precisely identify various DDoS attack types. We propose an ensemble architecture integrating Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN), and Random Forest (RF) models, whose outputs are synthesized by a Logistic Regression meta-learner. This approach explicitly addresses the ambiguity often encountered when combining predictions from multiple independent classifiers. Evaluated on the CIC-DDoS2019 dataset, our proposed ensemble meta-learning model achieves 96% accuracy in the multi-class identification task, significantly outperforming a baseline chain model (combining individual binary classifiers), which reached 92% accuracy and suffered from high ambiguity. Furthermore, integration and testing within a Software-Defined Networking (SDN) environment using Mininet and the Ryu controller demonstrated the practical applicability of our model, achieving 93% accuracy in identifying DDoS types in the emulated network traffic. Our work highlights the value of meta-learning ensembles for nuanced DDoS threat identification, paving the way for more adaptive and effective defense mechanisms.

</details>


### [182] [A Control-Driven Framework for Secure SaaS Onboarding in Regulated Enterprises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16543)
*Naga Sundeep Krishna Thota,Rithika Dulam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一套面向受监管企业的端到端SaaS上线框架，将第三方风险管理、网络安全、身份与访问管理及灾难恢复整合至统一生命周期，以降低流程摩擦、提升审计能力和增强运营韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SaaS引入流程通常将采购、第三方风险、网络安全、身份设计和恢复能力评估分割执行，导致评估重复、责任边界不清、关键依赖遗漏及上线延迟。对于承担关键业务功能的SaaS，这种孤岛式治理会遗留显著的合规与运营风险。

Method: 研究构建阶段化、控制驱动的SaaS上线生命周期，覆盖需求受理与风险定界、架构验证、身份设计、韧性评估及生产后治理。框架通过跨领域控制映射识别不同评审环节之间的依赖关系和常见失效模式，并提供安全连接、联合身份认证、最小权限授权及共享责任灾难恢复等设计模式；同时结合企业级实施经验和治理检查清单支持落地。

Result: 该框架能够将原本独立的控制域纳入一致的编排流程，减少重复审查与交接损耗，改善控制责任的可追溯性，并在上线前更系统地识别身份、连接、恢复和供应商治理方面的缺口。摘要未提供量化实验数据，因此其效果主要体现为实践性和治理流程层面的预期改进。

Conclusion: 论文的核心价值在于以生命周期和依赖管理为主线，替代孤岛式SaaS准入评审。该方法适合对合规、可审计性和业务连续性要求较高的企业，但实际收益仍取决于组织是否能建立跨团队责任机制、统一控制标准及持续的生产后监督。

Abstract: As enterprises increasingly adopt Software-as-a-Service (SaaS) platforms for mission-critical functions, onboarding these services has emerged as a complex challenge extending well beyond procurement and basic security review. In regulated environments, SaaS onboarding must address multiple interdependent control domains, including Third-Party Risk Management (TPRM), cybersecurity assessment, Identity and Access Management (IAM), and disaster recovery (DR), which are often executed in isolation, resulting in delayed go-lives, duplicated assessments, unclear ownership, and residual operational risk. This paper proposes a control-driven, end-to-end SaaS onboarding framework that integrates TPRM, cybersecurity, IAM, and DR into a unified lifecycle model. The framework introduces a stage-based approach spanning intake and risk scoping, architecture validation, identity design, resilience assessment, and post-production governance. Key contributions include: (1) a structured SaaS onboarding lifecycle emphasizing sequencing and dependency management across control domains; (2) a cross-domain control mapping highlighting failure modes caused by siloed reviews; and (3) practical design patterns for secure connectivity, federated identity, least-privilege access, and shared-responsibility disaster recovery. Supported by operational lessons from enterprise-scale implementations and a reference governance checklist, the framework helps organizations reduce onboarding friction, improve auditability, and strengthen the security and resilience posture of SaaS-enabled platforms.

</details>


### [183] [Towards Secure and Trustworthy DAOs for Cross-Chain Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16548)
*Faisal Haque Bappy,Tahrim Hossain,Tarannum Shaila Zaman,Tariqul Islam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向跨链DAO的分层安全框架，针对贿赂、代币控制、人机交互欺骗及协议缺陷四类风险，通过链上规则验证、门限密码学与可逆争议处理机制提升治理安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨链DAO的治理流程涉及多条链、跨域资产与异构协议，其信任边界和攻击面较单链DAO更复杂。现有安全机制通常缺乏对跨链治理风险的系统化分类与端到端防护能力。

Method: 作者首先将跨链DAO治理威胁划分为四类攻击向量：贿赂攻击、代币控制利用、人机交互欺骗和协议漏洞。随后设计多层安全架构：治理内核负责链上治理规则验证；跨链信任层基于门限密码学建立可信协作与验证；韧性层通过时间锁撤销和渐进式争议解决机制降低错误或恶意决策的不可逆影响。同时结合抗欺诈共识与去中心化验证措施形成综合防护。

Result: 摘要宣称该框架能够为跨链DAO提供结构化的安全对策，增强治理过程的透明性、可验证性、抗操纵性与故障恢复能力。但摘要未给出原型实现、形式化安全证明、性能开销、实验设置或与现有方案的量化对比，因此实际效果尚无法从摘要中验证。

Conclusion: 该工作的重要贡献在于提出跨链DAO治理攻击的分类体系，并将密码学信任、链上规则执行和可恢复的争议处置整合为统一框架。其研究价值主要体现为安全架构设计与问题定义；后续需通过明确威胁模型、跨链协议兼容性分析、实现评测和真实场景验证来证明可部署性与有效性。

Abstract: Cross-chain DAOs face unique security challenges that go beyond traditional single-chain vulnerabilities. This paper identifies and categorizes four critical attack vectors in cross-chain DAO governance: bribery attacks, token control exploits, human-computer interaction deceptions, and protocol vulnerabilities. We propose a comprehensive security framework with a multi-layered architecture that integrates cryptographic trust anchors, fraud-resistant consensus mechanisms, and decentralized validation techniques to address these threats. Our framework introduces novel components, including a Governance Kernel with on-chain rule verification, a Cross-Chain Trust Layer using threshold cryptography, and a Resilience Layer offering time-locked decision reversals and progressive dispute resolution. By establishing a structured set of countermeasures, this work lays the foundation for secure, transparent, and attack-resistant governance across diverse blockchain environments.

</details>


### [184] [Auditable Session Admission for Cross-Silo Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16559)
*Enzo Fenoglio,Philip Treleaven*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向跨机构联邦学习的会话准入网关：参与方在会话建立阶段签发限定操作范围的会话能力令牌，运行时网关通过无状态密码学验证、能力匹配及请求绑定证明决定是否放行，并生成可验证的审计证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨孤岛联邦学习虽然保留原始数据在本地，但不同机构间“谁能够在特定会话中调用哪些操作”的准入控制常依赖中心化策略服务、平台配置或临时检查。这些机制容易随时间漂移，且难以仅依据边界可见证据进行事后审计，因此需要一种可验证、可审计且适合跨组织边界执行的会话级授权机制。

Method: 方法包含两个阶段。第一，会话建立阶段：参与机构共同批准角色与约束，并为指定session_id签发数字签名的会话能力令牌；令牌明确列举允许的会话操作。第二，运行阶段：每个请求携带能力令牌及与该请求绑定的持有证明，网关据此验证令牌签名、请求绑定关系和操作是否属于令牌授权范围。该设计使被窃令牌的重放或冒用能够被网关检测。准入决策被简化为网关侧逐请求、无状态的密码学验证与能力匹配；会话编排和初始协商不属于方案范围，仍由各机构自行实现。

Result: 作者在以MNIST作为替代负载的跨孤岛联邦学习工作流上进行了端到端验证。原型系统开源，并提供容器化部署、基础设施即代码、可复现实验测试以及证据日志，表明该方案具备工程复现与审计支持能力。摘要未提供吞吐量、延迟、扩展性、故障场景或与既有准入机制的定量对比结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将跨机构联邦学习中的会话授权从易漂移的中心化或临时控制，转化为基于预批准能力令牌和请求绑定证明的边界准入机制。其主要价值在于以无状态在线验证实现细粒度会话操作控制，并留下可验证决策记录。局限在于安全性仍依赖会话建立阶段的可信协商、密钥与令牌生命周期管理，且摘要中的验证仅基于替代工作负载，尚不足以证明大规模真实部署下的性能和运维表现。

Abstract: Cross-silo federated learning keeps raw data local, but deployments frequently stall on a practical bottleneck when deciding who may invoke which session-scoped operations across organizational boundaries, under constraints that remain auditable after execution. In practice, admission is implemented via centralized policy services, platform configuration, or ad hoc checks, which drift over time and are hard to audit from boundary-visible evidence. We present a session admission gateway that enforces pre-approved session capabilities at the boundary and emits verifiable decision records. During session setup, participating organizations approve roles and constraints and mint signed session capability tokens that enumerate permitted session operations for a given session_id. At runtime, each request carries the token and a request-bound proof-of-possession, making replay and impersonation with stolen tokens detectable at the gateway via request binding. Admission reduces to stateless per-request cryptographic verification and capability matching at an admission gateway, while setup and orchestration remain out of scope and organization-specific. We validate the approach end-to-end on a cross-silo FL workflow using MNIST as a surrogate workload. The proof-of-concept is open-source, containerized, provisioned as code, and includes reproducible tests and evidence logs.

</details>


### [185] [Synchronization-Free Algebraic Fingerprints for Large Language Models: From Autoregressive to Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16648)
*Jaroslaw Janas,Josef Pieprzyk,Pawel Morawiecki*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种免同步的大语言模型文本水印方案：通过相邻词元对生成局部、独立的二元同余约束，并利用Reed-Solomon多项式编码秘密身份，以提高文本经插入、删除、改写或词元重排后的归因恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于固定文本块的水印通常依赖位置同步；文本被编辑、插入或删除后，水印块边界易发生错位，导致检测和身份恢复失败。因此，需要一种不依赖全局位置、同时能承载不同长度身份信息的鲁棒水印机制。

Method: 方法将秘密身份表示为Reed-Solomon多项式。对每个相邻词元对，先由密码学哈希确定该多项式的一个求值点，再以多项式求值的奇偶性作为嵌入到后一词元选择过程中的水印比特。每个词元对对应一个可独立验证的二元同余，因此无需维护跨文本块的同步关系。论文还从代数恢复角度讨论了不同身份长度下的解码算法，并将水印受损过程建模为二元对称信道。

Result: 理论分析表明，即使词元遭受较高比例的扰动，只需引入较小冗余即可实现可靠的身份恢复。由于验证约束仅依赖局部相邻词元，插入、删除和词元重排不会像块式方案那样造成全局同步失效。该框架能够支持短身份与长身份的灵活嵌入。

Conclusion: 该工作以局部自包含的同余约束替代传统分块同步机制，结合Reed-Solomon编码的纠错能力，实现了面向编辑与改写场景的鲁棒文本水印归因。其主要贡献是消除同步脆弱性，并为不同容量需求提供统一的身份编码与恢复框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have created an urgent need for reliable watermarking methods that enable attribution of generated text while remaining robust to editing and paraphrasing. We propose a novel synchronization-free watermarking scheme in which every watermark consists of a single binary congruence generated from a pair of neighbouring tokens. For each token pair, a cryptographic hash determines an evaluation point of a Reed--Solomon polynomial representing the secret identity, while the parity of the polynomial evaluation determines the watermark bit embedded into the second token of the pair. Since each congruence is self-contained and depends only on the local token pair, the proposed construction is naturally resistant to insertions, deletions, and token reordering. We analyse the recovery problem from an algebraic perspective, discuss several decoding algorithms suitable for different identity sizes, and model watermark corruption as a Binary Symmetric Channel. The analysis shows that reliable recovery requires only a small redundancy even for relatively high token corruption rates. Unlike existing block-based watermarking schemes, the proposed method avoids synchronization problems while providing a flexible framework for embedding both short and long secret identities.

</details>


### [186] [VIGIL: Verifying Identity via Gated Intermittent Likelihoods for Continuous Biometric Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16651)
*Aldridge Fonseca,Udayan Atreya,Amith Kamath Belman,Frank Sicong Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出VIGIL连续多模态生物认证框架，通过可配置跨模态融合、双状态状态转移机和三区决策机制，提升弱信号或证据不充分条件下的身份验证可靠性，并缩短异常身份检测时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序融合方法难以区分持续性非法使用者与信号质量较差的真实用户，容易在安全性与可用性之间失衡。因此，需要一种能处理多模态证据不确定性、支持持续多轮验证且可适应不同部署需求的认证方法。

Method: VIGIL包含三项核心设计：第一，采用按模态独立加权的可配置跨模态融合，使部署者能够选择或调整信息整合策略；第二，利用具有单向转移矩阵的双状态状态转移机进行时序融合，以约束状态演化并减少不合理的回流；第三，采用三区验证决策模型，将结果划分为通过、拒绝和证据不确定区域，并结合自适应收缩验证窗口，对不确定样本执行多轮验证。论文还通过单调衰减、回流消除及解析评估论证其性质。

Result: 摘要声称，该框架能够克服既有方法在低信号质量和持续认证场景中的局限：在维持真实用户较高可用性的同时，更快发现异常身份使用。摘要未提供具体数据集、实验设置、指标数值、统计显著性或与基线方法的定量对比，因此性能提升幅度尚无法独立判断。

Conclusion: VIGIL的主要贡献是将可配置多模态融合、受约束的时序状态建模与延迟决策机制结合，用于连续生物认证。其设计逻辑针对证据波动和短时不确定性较合理，但论文实际有效性仍需依据完整实验结果验证，尤其应关注不同模态缺失、信号退化、验证延迟和误接受/误拒绝率之间的权衡。

Abstract: Continuous multi-modal authentication has emerged as a necessity for securing modern environments against persistent threats. Existing temporal fusion techniques fail to identify a persistent attacker from a genuine user with poor signal strength. In this study, we propose VIGIL (Verifying Identity via Gated Intermittent Likelihoods for Continuous Biometric Authentication), a highly adaptive continuous authentication framework. We introduce configurable cross-modal fusion with per-modality weighting, enabling operators to select their choice of integration strategy. We improve temporal fusion using dual-state State Transition Machines (STM) with unidirectional transition matrices. A three-zone verification decision model that enables multi-round verification when evidence is inconclusive is used in combination with an adaptive shrinking verification window. Monotonic decay, backflow elimination and analytical evaluation demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively addresses the limitations of existing approaches and reduces the time to detect intrusions while maintaining high usability for legitimate users.

</details>


### [187] [SABLE: Minimalist Instruction-Level Authenticated Encryption for Constrained Confidential Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16771)
*Hamid Noori,Carlton Shepherd*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出SABLE，一种面向RISC-V的指令级认证加密架构，通过在取指路径中集成解密与验证机制，保护程序指令的机密性与完整性，并在FPGA原型上系统评估了七种微架构实现的成本与权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统处理器在执行期间以明文形式暴露代码和数据，可能导致知识产权泄露及关键检查逻辑被篡改。现有指令级加密方案通常绑定特定微架构、在错误指令执行后才发现篡改、采用非标准密码算法，或依赖复杂的程序状态分析。因此，作者希望构建一种对既有RISC-V生态侵入较小、可移植且支持指令认证的方案。

Method: SABLE在指令存储器封装层和CPU前端两类位置部署“解密并验证”阶段，兼容标准RISC-V工具链，仅需对编译后的ELF文件进行轻量后处理。作者以ASCON-128a作为认证加密算法，在开源NEORV32 SoC及Xilinx Artix-7 FPGA上实现一类单周期组合式设计和六类多周期时序式设计，共七种ILE微架构；使用Dhrystone基准测试比较其逻辑资源、性能、功耗及单指令能耗。

Result: 相对于未加密基线，七种配置的LUT开销为1.6至9.3倍，性能开销为4.1至10.0倍，功耗开销为1.5至8.0倍，单指令能耗开销为10.4至80.0倍。结果表明，组合式与多周期式设计在吞吐、面积和能耗上存在显著差异，且解密验证模块所在位置也会影响实现代价与性能特征。

Conclusion: SABLE证明了可在保持RISC-V工具链基本兼容和较低软件改造成本的前提下，实现微架构相对无关的指令级认证加密。该方案并非无代价：认证加密会带来明显的性能和能耗负担；但通过选择不同的组合式或时序式实现，可分别面向面积优先、性能优先或能耗优先的应用需求进行设计折中。

Abstract: Conventional processor designs expose code and data as plaintext throughout execution, rendering them inherently vulnerable to attacks that recover intellectual property or modify security/safety checks. Instruction-level encryption (ILE) enables CPU-level decryption, execution, and optionally authentication of individual encrypted program instructions at runtime. However, existing proposals depend on specific micro-architectures, detect corrupted instructions after they have executed, rely on non-standard ciphers, or require complex analyses of program state. In this work, we introduce and present a design exploration of a RISC-V processor architecture (SABLE) that enables minimally invasive instruction-level authenticated encryption of programs. SABLE is agnostic to the underlying micro-architecture, remaining compatible with the standard RISC-V toolchain with minor changes to post-process compiled ELF binaries. We integrate a decrypt-and-verify stage at two points (the instruction-memory wrapper and the CPU frontend) and explore seven ILE micro-architectures from a single-cycle (combinational) design to six multi-cycle (sequential) variants. We implement and evaluate the designs using ASCON-128a on a Xilinx Artix-7 FPGA with the open-source NEORV32 system on chip. Relative to baseline performance, the configurations span LUT, performance, power, and energy-per-instruction overheads of 1.6-9.3$\times$, 4.1-10.0$\times$, 1.5-8.0$\times$, and 10.4-80.0$\times$, respectively, using the Dhrystone benchmarking suite. Finally, we discuss design trade-offs, highlighting area-, performance-, and energy-aware design points.

</details>


### [188] [Rollback-Free Cross-Chain Atomicity Through Forward-Only Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16959)
*Tahrim Hossain,Faisal Haque Bappy,Tarannum Shaila Zaman,Tariqul Islam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向异构区块链的跨链智能合约原子协调框架。该框架不依赖回滚，而是通过后续链上补偿交易修正未完成操作，以实现前向式原子性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨链应用需要多个独立链上的智能合约作为一个整体执行，但区块链交易一旦确认便不可逆，传统原子提交协议依赖的回滚机制无法直接使用。同时，跨链操作需要在结果未确定期间锁定相关合约状态，并在链间无法直接观察彼此执行结果的条件下确认各链结果。

Method: 框架采用“前向纠正”机制：对于未完成或部分完成的跨链操作，不撤销已确认交易，而是提交新的链上交易以完成补偿、收敛或状态修正。它通过限制状态持有时长来避免无限期锁定，并将并发竞争范围限定在操作实际涉及的状态上。各链的执行结果则依据链上记录进行证明和确认，避免依赖单一协调者。

Result: 该方法能够在不可回滚的区块链环境中支持跨链智能合约操作的原子协调；其设计可有界地控制状态锁定时间，减少无关状态间的竞争，并利用各链可验证的执行记录建立操作结果。摘要未提供具体性能实验、实现细节或定量评估数据。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将传统依赖回滚的原子提交思路替换为基于补偿交易的前向式恢复机制，为异构区块链上的通用智能合约跨链原子操作提供了基础框架。

Abstract: Blockchain platforms have grown into an ecosystem of independent networks, and a growing class of applications now requires smart contracts on separate chains to act as one. Such operations must be atomic, yet immutability makes this fundamentally harder: a confirmed transaction cannot be reversed, so the rollback on which classical atomic commitment protocols depend is unavailable. Two challenges follow. Contract state must be held across an operation whose outcome is not yet known, and each chain's execution outcome must be established even though no chain can observe another. In response, we introduce a framework that achieves atomicity through forward-only correction, resolving incomplete operations with new on-chain transactions rather than reversal. The framework bounds how long contract state is held and confines contention to the state an operation touches, and it establishes outcomes from an on-chain record of what each chain executed, without relying on any single coordinating party. This work lays the foundation for atomic coordination of general smart contract operations across heterogeneous blockchains.

</details>


### [189] [Adaptive Incident Prioritization for Security Operations at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16963)
*Scott Freitas,Amir Gharib,Maayan Magenheim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出自适应事件优先级排序（AIP），用于在安全运营中心的动态事件队列中持续排序待调查事件。该方法已部署于大规模真实环境，并通过专家评审、线上遥测及公开数据集扩展验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型安全运营中心每日需处理大量活跃事件，传统按到达时间、粗粒度严重性或产品启发式规则排序的方式，难以准确反映事件的相对调查优先级。分析人员因此面临较高认知负担，需要一种能在多租户、无显式查询的实时队列中识别高价值事件的方法。

Method: AIP将BM25风格排序适配到无查询的多租户事件队列。系统将每个事件表示为从告警及元数据中提取、归一化后的安全组件集合；排序分数综合组件的饱和局部出现频率、跨租户估计的全局组件稀有度、受限的领域先验乘数，并提供组件级解释。系统支持近实时推理，并持续刷新事件分数。论文还扩展Microsoft GUIDE数据集，加入由专家生成的队列优先级标签。

Result: AIP部署于数万客户环境，事件分数刷新的中位延迟为5秒。在覆盖1,000个客户组织的专家评审中，Precision@10达到92.8%。在覆盖473,000个组织日队列的上线遥测中，相对于按严重性排序，AIP使告警详情交互提升5.8%，告警详情浏览事件提升17.5%。新增公开标签数据覆盖499个组织队列和9,980个真实事件。

Conclusion: AIP表明，基于组件稀有度、局部频率及领域先验的可解释排序方法，能够有效提升动态事件队列中高优先级事件的靠前程度，并促进分析人员对关键事件的交互。论文的主要贡献还包括提供面向真实事件队列优先级任务的公开专家标注资源，为后续模型比较与研究提供基准。

Abstract: Large security operations centers (SOCs) often face hundreds of active incidents per day, creating substantial cognitive and operational demands for analysts. Analysts must quickly decide which incidents deserve attention within long, constantly changing queues, yet incidents are commonly ordered by arrival time, coarse severity, or product-specific heuristics that leave their relative priority unclear. We introduce Adaptive Incident Prioritization (AIP), the ranking algorithm behind Microsoft Defender Queue Assistant, which continuously prioritizes security incidents for analyst investigation. AIP adapts BM25-style ranking to a query-less, multi-tenant queue setting by representing each incident as a collection of normalized security components extracted from alerts and metadata. The model combines saturated local component frequency, global component rarity estimated across tenants, bounded domain-prior multipliers, and component-level explanations. Deployed across tens of thousands of customers, AIP performs near-real-time inference and refreshes incident scores with a median latency of five seconds. In an expert-reviewed evaluation across 1,000 customer organizations, AIP achieves 92.8% Precision@10. In post-launch telemetry across 473,000 organization-day queues, AIP increases alert-detail interaction by 5.8% and alert-detail view events by 17.5% relative to severity ordering, providing behavioral evidence that model-ranked queues concentrate analyst engagement. We also extend the Microsoft GUIDE dataset with, to our knowledge, the first public label source for SOC queue prioritization over real-world incidents. The extension covers 499 organization queues and 9,980 incidents with expert-derived priority labels, enabling the research community to develop, compare, and advance methods for incident prioritization.

</details>


### [190] [Federated Lightweight Intrusion Detection in Drone Swarms with Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17025)
*Fawaz J. Alruwaili,Cihan Tunc*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种结合知识蒸馏的轻量级联邦学习入侵检测系统，用于资源受限的无人机群网络，在保持约98.6%检测准确率的同时，降低约70%的通信成本和29%的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机群中的异构设备通常计算能力有限，且开放通信环境增加了安全风险。传统集中式机器学习方法需要汇集各节点数据，存在传输负担、隐私风险和中心节点依赖；现有联邦学习检测方案仍面临模型通信与本地训练开销较高的问题。

Method: 作者构建面向无人机群的联邦学习入侵检测框架，以深度神经网络作为检测模型，并引入知识蒸馏，将较复杂模型的知识迁移至轻量模型。各无人机节点在本地训练并仅交换模型相关信息，通过联邦聚合完成全局模型更新，从而避免集中原始数据；知识蒸馏用于压缩模型、减少参数传输量及本地推理和训练成本。实验部署在Raspberry Pi 4设备上，并使用真实无人机网络数据集进行评估。

Result: 实验结果显示，所提方法达到约98.6%的检测准确率，同时将总体通信成本降低约70%，计算开销降低约29%。这表明知识蒸馏能够在较少资源消耗下维持较高的入侵检测性能。

Conclusion: 该研究说明，将联邦学习与知识蒸馏结合能够有效缓解无人机群入侵检测中的数据集中、通信负担和设备算力限制问题，具有面向资源受限分布式网络部署的实用潜力。摘要未提供与未蒸馏联邦学习、集中式模型及其他轻量化方法的完整对比细节，后续仍需验证其在不同攻击类型、节点异构程度和网络不稳定条件下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Drone swarms are increasingly deployed in critical applications such as surveillance, disaster response, and infrastructure monitoring. However, their reliance on open communication channels and their limited computational resources make them vulnerable to a wide range of cyber-threats. There is a growing interest in intrusion detection systems (IDS) specifically designed for drone environments and operations. However, the conventional solutions including Machine Learning (ML)-based approaches require collecting all data from heterogeneous drones in the swarm and processing on a central server may not be always feasible. Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a promising distributed solution with an additional privacy-preserving feature. Even though potential studies exist, conventional FL-based IDS frameworks still face communication and computational overhead challenges, while achieving a balance between efficiency and effective detection under practical resource constraints remains a challenge. Therefore, we propose a lightweight FL-based IDS tailored for drone swarm networks using deep neural networks (DNN) enhanced with knowledge distillation (KD) to reduce model complexity and communication costs without sacrificing detection performance. We evaluate our framework using Raspberry Pi 4 devices and a real-world drone network dataset. Our approach demonstrates a detection accuracy of approximately 98.6% while reducing overall communication cost by around 70% and computational overhead by 29%. These results show that FL combined with KD is a practical and suitable solution for secure and efficient deployment in resource-constrained drone networks.

</details>


### [191] [Federated Learning and LLM-Driven Threat Intelligence for Zero Trust IoT Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17035)
*Amal Alshehri,Cihan Tunc*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向资源受限物联网设备的零信任安全架构，将联邦学习异常检测、持续身份验证、基于大语言模型的威胁响应，以及MQTT上的双向TLS通信整合为统一管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物联网设备通常难以及时升级，因而容易面临恶意软件、数据泄露和未授权访问等风险；同时，集中式检测会带来隐私暴露与数据传输成本。论文旨在在不依赖全面设备替换的前提下，提高关键物联网部署的安全性和隐私保护能力。

Method: 方法以零信任原则为核心，在通信层通过MQTT结合双向TLS实现设备与消息的双向认证；在检测层使用联邦学习，使多节点在不直接共享原始数据的条件下协同训练异常检测模型；在响应层引入大语言模型驱动的威胁情报与自动化处置，并结合持续身份验证形成端到端安全流程。实验部署使用树莓派和多类传感器，以F1分数和ROC-AUC评估检测性能。

Result: 实验报告异常检测F1分数为0.9091、ROC-AUC为1.0，表明该方案在所测试环境和数据集上具有较强的区分异常与正常行为的能力。结果初步支持联邦学习与零信任通信机制可用于资源受限物联网设备。

Conclusion: 该工作贡献在于将隐私保护的分布式异常检测、零信任通信认证和自动化威胁响应进行系统集成。其性能指标较高，但摘要未说明数据规模、攻击类型分布、联邦学习参与节点数、基线比较、计算与通信开销及大语言模型响应的可靠性，因此模型的泛化能力、可扩展性和实际部署成本仍需通过更全面实验验证。

Abstract: While the Internet of Things (IoT) has become essential, they introduced serious security and privacy challenges, especially for mission-critical environments. Legacy devices are vulnerable to viruses, data breaches, and unauthorized access, and updating these devices would be infeasibly costly. As a solution, this paper presents a Federated Learning and LLM-Driven Threat Intelligence for Zero Trust IoT Architecture, with FL for anomaly detection integrating privacy-preserving distributed learning, continuous identity verification, and LLM-driven autonomous threat response into a unified pipeline. Unlike existing solutions, our framework enforces Zero Trust at every communication layer via mutual TLS (mTLS) over MQTT, ensuring no device or message is implicitly trusted. Our experiments with Raspberry Pis and various sensors achieve an F1 score of 0.9091 and an ROC-AUC of 1.0, highlighting the effectiveness of the proposed framework in enabling privacy-preserving anomaly detection for resource-constrained IoT devices.

</details>


### [192] [A Systematic Evaluation of Traditional Privacy Policy Analysis Tools Against LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17075)
*Madhav Aryal,Sudipa Saha,Kaushal Kafle,Anshuman Chhabra,Sunil Manandhar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文系统评估通用大语言模型能否替代隐私政策与数据合规分析中的专用工具。基于10份隐私政策及多个中间任务的比较，GPT-5.2和Gemini-2.5总体上达到或超过既有工具表现，但实验规模、标注基准和泛化能力仍限制其结论的外推范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私政策分析长期依赖面向特定任务开发的工具，例如矛盾检测、法规合规判断、政策摘要与信息聚合。这类工具通常需要领域知识、定制工程和专门训练。随着大语言模型能力提升，核心问题变为：无需额外微调或复杂工程的现成模型，是否能覆盖这些专用工具提供的多样化功能与分析方法。

Method: 研究选取6个代表性专用工具，覆盖三类功能：政策矛盾检测、法规合规分析、隐私政策摘要与聚合；同时考察三类中间任务：元组形式的结构化数据抽取、语义角色标注和人工式隐私政策标签标注。作者在自建的10份隐私政策数据集上，通过直接提示方式让GPT-5.2和Gemini-2.5的不同配置执行对应任务，并将结果与原有工具及OPP-115标注数据集进行比较。

Result: 结果表明，所测试的大语言模型在各类功能中总体可匹配或超过既有专用工具。针对第一方数据收集实体的人工标签任务，模型平均精确率为81.8%，召回率为70.9%；针对第三方数据共享实体，平均精确率为91.4%，召回率为70.8%。这说明模型在实体识别和政策语义解析方面具有较强能力，尤其第三方共享实体的预测精确率较高；但约29%的召回缺口也意味着仍会遗漏部分应识别的信息。

Conclusion: 论文认为，现成大语言模型已能有效承担大量原本依赖专用工具的隐私政策和法规分析工作，并可降低领域工具开发与维护成本。不过，“替代”结论应谨慎解读：实验仅基于10份政策，且结果可能受提示设计、模型版本、任务定义及评测标注质量影响。对于高可靠性合规场景，较合理的实践路径是将大语言模型与规则校验、结构化输出约束、人工复核及跨数据集验证结合，而非完全无监督地取代专用系统。

Abstract: The advent of LLMs has significantly changed the research on privacy policy and data compliance analysis by enabling tasks that previously required specialized, domain-specific tools. However, it remains unclear to what extent LLMs can truly replicate the diverse functionalities, and the wide range of methodologies and analysis offered by prior work. In this paper, we conduct the first systematic evaluation of whether off-the-shelf LLMs can replace specialized privacy analysis tools. We study six representative tools spanning three major functionalities: contradiction detection, regulatory compliance analysis, and privacy policy summarization and aggregation, and across three intermediate tasks: structured data extraction using tuples, Semantic Role Labeling (SRL) and manual privacy policy labeling. We compare the performance of two state-of-the-art LLMs (GPT-5.2 and Gemini-2.5 in various configurations) against the tools by directly prompting the models to perform corresponding functionalities and tasks on a custom dataset of 10 privacy policies, allowing us to assess whether off-the-shelf models can produce tool-specific functionalities without further engineering or domain-specific training, major limitations in prior work. Our results show that LLMs consistently match or exceed the capabilities of existing tools across the functionalities. In manual labeling of first-party collection entities, LLMs achieved an average precision of 81.8% and recall of 70.9%, while for labeling of third-party sharing entities, they achieved an average precision of 91.4% and recall of 70.8% compared to the OPP-115 dataset. Overall, our findings indicate that LLMs can effectively perform a broad range of functionalities and tasks in privacy policy and regulation analysis that previously required specialized tools.

</details>


### [193] [SATLOCK: Handover-Coupled Scheduling for Weather-Resilient Quantum Key Distribution over LEO Constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17076)
*Mohammad Arif Hossain,Md Jafrin Hossain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出SATLOCK框架，用于低轨卫星星座中的量子密钥分发路由；其核心贡献是联合建模链路物理损耗、天气随机性、有限密钥效应与卫星切换约束，并以ILP给出吞吐量上界、以分布式DQN实现在线天气自适应路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨卫星QKD路由不同于传统网络路由：卫星切换使相邻时隙的调度决策相互耦合，云层遮挡会使地面链路随机失效，而有限密钥修正会令短时或低仰角过境无法产生有效密钥。因此，需要同时处理物理链路质量、随机天气和资源竞争的路由优化方法。

Method: SATLOCK包含三部分：第一，构建复合信道模型，纳入大气损耗、指向抖动、马尔可夫云覆盖、诱骗态参数估计和有限密钥修正；第二，建立整数线性规划模型，以显式刻画卫星切换和资源分配约束，并提供具备可证明性的吞吐量上界；第三，设计去中心化DQN基线，使各需求能够根据天气状态进行在线路由决策。实验在Walker星座和跨区域业务需求上，比较低竞争与高竞争资源场景。

Result: 在低竞争场景（16颗卫星、6个需求）中，ILP累计吞吐量达到1311 Mbit，最强启发式方法达到ILP结果的95%至96%，DQN达到91.8%。在高竞争场景（8颗卫星、12个需求）中，卫星切换约束成为主要瓶颈，启发式方法下降至ILP的89.5%，DQN达到84.6%。结果表明，启发式方法在资源充足时接近最优，但竞争增强后性能退化明显；DQN能学习单个需求的有效天气适应策略，但因缺少跨需求协调而受限。

Conclusion: 论文表明，面向低轨卫星QKD的路由必须显式考虑切换、天气和有限密钥效应。ILP适合作为评估算法性能的理论上界与离线优化工具；分布式DQN具有在线适应天气变化的能力，但需要引入多需求协同、集中训练或多智能体协调机制，才能缩小高竞争条件下与最优上界的差距。

Abstract: Routing quantum keys over low-earth-orbit (LEO) satellite constellations is harder than classical routing: satellite handovers couple consecutive scheduling decisions, stochastic cloud cover can silently zero a ground link, and finite-key effects eliminate short, low-elevation passes entirely. We present SATLOCK, a handover-aware Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) routing framework that combines (i) a composite channel model incorporating atmospheric loss, pointing jitter, Markov cloud cover, decoy-state estimation, and finite-key correction; (ii) an integer linear program (ILP) giving a provable handover-aware throughput upper bound; and (iii) a decentralized deep Q-network (DQN) baseline for weather-adaptive online routing. We evaluate two contention regimes on a Walker constellation serving intercontinental demands. In low contention (16 satellites, 6 demands), the ILP delivers 1,311 Mbit while the strongest heuristics reach 95--96\% of ILP. In high contention (8 satellites, 12 demands), where handovers become binding, heuristics drop to 89.5\% of ILP. The DQN agent reaches 91.8\% and 84.6\% of ILP in the two regimes; it learns effective per-demand weather policies but is limited in aggregate by the lack of cross-demand coordination.

</details>


### [194] [A Multi-Model Hybrid Defense Approach Against White-box Adversarial Attacks in Computer Network Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17105)
*Khushnaseeb Roshan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出结合对抗训练与高斯数据增强的混合防御机制，以提升网络入侵检测系统在FGSM和C&W对抗样本攻击下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络入侵检测系统虽然可识别异常流量，但容易受到对抗样本误导，导致检测准确率显著下降。因此，需要构建能够同时应对特定攻击方向与多方向扰动的防御方法。

Method: 方法将对抗训练（AT）与高斯数据增强（GDA）结合：AT利用攻击样本强化模型对已知攻击向量的适应能力；GDA通过注入高斯噪声扩展训练数据分布，增强对多方向扰动的稳定性。实验针对FGSM与C&W攻击，并在0.0001至0.0009范围内评估扰动强度或置信噪声参数的影响。

Result: 未受攻击时，NIDS具有较好的准确率和F1值；遭受攻击后，FGSM和C&W分别使准确率降至0.2649和0.4961。采用混合防御后，FGSM场景准确率恢复至96.57%，C&W场景恢复至89.20%，表明该方法对两类攻击均具有明显缓解效果。

Conclusion: AT与GDA的组合能够有效提高NIDS抵抗对抗扰动的能力，且对FGSM的防御效果优于C&W。论文的主要贡献是展示了混合启发式防御在对抗鲁棒网络入侵检测中的潜力；但摘要未说明数据集、模型结构、攻击白盒或黑盒设定及与其他防御基线的对比，因此泛化性和实际部署价值仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: It is crucial to safeguard computer networks from evolving network security threats and unknown cyberattacks. An essential tool for protecting computer networks against unknown cyber threats is Network Intrusion Detection System (NIDS). However, NIDS faces a major security concern due to its susceptibility to adversarial attacks. Adversarial attacks aim to deceive NIDS by crafting and injecting adversarial examples into the system. These adversarial inputs can deceive the NIDS into misclassifying benign network traffic as malicious. We developed a resilient hybrid defense mechanism aimed to mitigate the impact of two potent adversarial attacks: Fast Gradient Sign Method (FGSM) and Carlini & Wagner (C&W) attack. Our hybrid defense approach leverages the combined strength of two heuristic defense methods: Adversarial Training (AT) and Gaussian Data Augmentation (GDA). GDA provides multi-directional defense, while AT enhances NIDS robustness against specific adversarial vectors. Under pre-attack scenarios, NIDS demonstrated good accuracy and f1-score. However, in the post-attack scenario, its accuracy significantly dropped under FGSM and C&W attacks (0.2649 and 0.4961, respectively). Our proposed hybrid defense method effectively mitigated these adversarial threats, with post-defense accuracy of 96.57% and 89.20% for FGSM and C&W attacks. We evaluated the defense strategy across a range of epsilon and confidence noise factor values (ranging from 0.0001 to 0.0009). This research provides a good direction for future researchers in the emerging area of adversarial machine learning from a security perspective.

</details>


### [195] [DSA Nonce Vulnerabilities: An Interactive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17107)
*Rundong Wei,Xiaomei Tian,Xiaoqi Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一个面向CTF竞赛教学与分析的DSA签名可视化平台，用于展示DSA签名、验证及三类随机数漏洞攻击的中间计算过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSA涉及模运算、大整数计算和数论知识，初学者难以理解签名生成、验证及私钥恢复攻击的具体机制；现有工具通常仅展示输入与输出，缺少对关键中间变量和攻击步骤的可解释性支持。

Method: 平台实现DSA基础签名生成与验证，并复现三类典型随机数漏洞场景：随机数重复使用、线性随机数泄漏以及基于隐藏数问题（HNP）的格攻击。系统通过分步骤展示、关键数值高亮和动态流程可视化，使用户能够跟踪模逆、签名参数计算、方程构造及私钥恢复等过程。

Result: 实验表明，该平台能够正确复现标准DSA签名与验证流程，并成功演示三类随机数漏洞条件下的攻击流程。摘要未提供具体实验规模、运行效率、成功率或与现有工具的定量对比结果。

Conclusion: 该工作主要贡献在于将DSA及其常见CTF漏洞攻击转化为可交互、可检查的教学分析流程，有助于降低学习门槛并提升攻击原理的可解释性。其价值偏向教育和竞赛训练；后续可通过用户研究、更多漏洞类型及量化性能评估进一步验证平台有效性。

Abstract: Digital signatures are fundamental to identity authentication and data integrity in cybersecurity, and the NIST-standardized Digital Signature Algorithm (DSA) frequently appears in the cryptography track of CTF competitions. However, DSA relies on number theory, modular arithmetic, and large-integer computation, making both the algorithm and its associated attacks difficult for beginners to follow. Conventional tools often expose only inputs and outputs, leaving the intermediate computations of signing, verification, and key-recovery attacks opaque. This paper presents a DSA signature analysis and visualisation platform tailored to CTF competitions. The platform provides three main capabilities: basic signature generation and verification, reproduction of common CTF attack methods, and dynamic visualisation of attack workflows. It covers three representative nonce vulnerabilities: nonce reuse, linear nonce leakage, and HNP-based lattice attacks. Stepwise displays and highlighted intermediate values make the underlying computations directly inspectable. Experiments show that the platform correctly reproduces the standard DSA workflow and all three attack scenarios.

</details>


### [196] [SlotGuard: Stop Oversharing Private Local Context in LLM Agent Transcri](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17147)
*Haocheng Xia,Yongjoo Park*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出SlotGuard：一种部署在本地、位于智能体运行时与外部模型服务之间的转录边界机制。它通过保留结构与引用关系的敏感信息重写，在不向外部模型暴露原始路径、邮箱或凭据等内容的前提下，维持智能体任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体会将工具输出、命令行日志和文件读取结果追加到发送给模型服务商的上下文中，进而泄露隐私信息与凭据。现有占位符式脱敏方法存在漏检嵌入式或跨轮引用、误删普通相似字符串、破坏文件路径等结构化信息从而降低推理能力的问题。

Method: SlotGuard在本地重写待发送的智能体转录内容：将具有结构绑定关系的数据转换为带类型、支持后缀识别的槽位表示；将密钥等秘密替换为格式保持的合成值；使用轻量级会话图连接跨轮次引用；仅在可信运行时内部将槽位恢复为原始值。该设计旨在让外部模型获得足以完成推理的结构、格式和指代关系，而不接触真实敏感数据。

Result: 在受控的、面向代码仓库任务的智能体转录数据上，SlotGuard移除了9,229个路径中标注的全部20,814个结构敏感字符，并将852个植入凭据的泄露率降至0.0%。在四个上游模型上，其任务成功率接近使用原始转录的设置；相比之下，通用脱敏方法的成功率降至2.5%。每个智能体轮次的转录重写中位耗时为14.424微秒。

Conclusion: SlotGuard表明，敏感信息保护不必依赖会显著损害语义和结构的简单遮蔽。通过类型化槽位、格式保持替换与跨轮引用建模，可在较低运行开销下实现较强的路径和凭据泄露防护，同时基本保留LLM智能体的任务能力。其结论的适用范围主要基于受控仓库转录与植入式凭据测试，真实生产环境中对更多数据类型、复杂工具链及对抗性输入的泛化能力仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: LLM agents can leak privacy (e.g., paths, emails) and credentials (e.g., API keys) as agent observations (e.g., tool outputs, shell logs, and file reads) are appended to provider-bound transcripts. Existing placeholder redaction is brittle: it can miss embedded or cross-turn references, over-redact benign lookalikes, and destroy the structure useful for reasoning. We present SlotGuard, a local transcript boundary that can hide sensitive data while retaining agents' performance. SlotGuard rewrites structural bindings as typed, suffix-aware slots, replaces secrets with format-preserving synthetic values, links cross-turn references with a lightweight session graph, and restores raw values only inside the trusted runtime. On controlled repository-oriented agent transcripts, SlotGuard removes all 20,814 annotated structurally sensitive characters across 9,229 paths and reduces credential leakage to 0.0\% across 852 planted values. It remains close to raw-transcript task success across four upstream models, while generic redaction drops to 2.5\%. Transcript rewriting takes a median of 14.424~$μ$s per agent turn. The code is publicly accessible at https://github.com/illinoisdata/SlotGuard.

</details>


### [197] [How Jailbreak Attacks Inform Safety Alignment: A Defender-Centric, Shapley-Based Evaluation of Jailbreak Contributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17152)
*Yukai Zhou,Feiyang Lu,Xiaokai Mao,Jinfei Liu,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出以防御者收益而非攻击成功率评估越狱攻击：衡量攻击样本用于安全训练后带来的实际安全改进，并通过A-MESS框架在有限黑盒查询下选择高价值、紧凑的攻击子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统指标如攻击成功率主要反映攻击者能否绕过模型限制，但高成功率攻击不一定能为后续安全训练提供更大收益。作者希望建立与模型安全改进直接关联的评估准则，使红队数据的筛选更有效。

Method: 作者定义最小有效攻击子集选择问题（A-MESS）。该框架从攻击子集的黑盒效用观测中估计AttackSHAP分数，即基于Shapley值的单个攻击边际贡献归因；随后在给定预算下，采用贪心优化或代理模型优化选择攻击子集。该方法不依赖特定训练设置，适用于不同安全训练流程和效用函数。

Result: 在受控效用景观与真实大语言模型安全实验中，攻击成功率排序与防御者中心效用的相关性较弱；AttackSHAP可通过有限次数的效用查询获得较准确估计；相较于按攻击成功率筛选或仅依据归因分数筛选，直接面向子集效用的优化能带来更强的安全提升。

Conclusion: 越狱攻击的价值不应仅由其突破模型的能力决定，更应由其作为红队训练资源所产生的安全改进决定。A-MESS为低成本识别高效安全训练数据提供了通用方法，并强调应将攻击评估从攻击者中心转向防御者中心。

Abstract: Jailbreak attacks on large language models are usually evaluated by attacker-centric metrics such as attack success rate (ASR), yet an attack that breaks a model is not necessarily useful for improving its safety. We propose a defender-centric view of jailbreak evaluation, where attacks are evaluated by the downstream safety improvements they enable when used as red-teaming data for safety training. Building on this view, we introduce A-MESS (Minimal Effective Attack-Subset Selection), a setting-agnostic framework for attributing and selecting jailbreak attacks from black-box subset utility observations. A-MESS estimates AttackSHAP, a Shapley-based score that attributes marginal utility to individual attacks and selects compact attack subsets under user-specified budgets via greedy or surrogate-based optimization. Across controlled utility landscapes and real LLM safety settings, we find that ASR rankings are weakly aligned with defender-centric utility, that AttackSHAP can be estimated accurately with limited utility queries, and that directly optimizing subsets yields stronger safety utility than attacker-centric or attribution-only selection. These results suggest evaluating jailbreak attacks as resources for improving safety, not only as tools for breaking models.

</details>


### [198] [Fast and Private Max-Sum Diversification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17196)
*Ron Zadicario,Tova Milo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文首次系统研究差分隐私约束下的结果多样化问题，聚焦最大和多样化（MSD）。作者针对基数约束和拟阵约束提出具备近似最优效用保证的差分隐私算法，并给出更高效的实现；实验显示其在强隐私保护下仍接近非隐私基线的效用，且在基数约束下显著缩短运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结果多样化旨在生成信息丰富且非冗余的数据摘要或查询结果，已广泛应用于数据驱动任务。然而，现有方法通常忽略底层敏感数据带来的隐私泄露风险。论文因此将差分隐私引入结果多样化，以在保护个体数据隐私的同时维持结果的相关性与多样性。

Method: 作者以MSD为核心模型，其目标是在给定约束下最大化两部分的线性组合：衡量结果相关性的子模函数，以及衡量结果多样性的所选项目两两距离之和。论文分别针对基数约束与拟阵约束设计差分隐私算法，提供近似最优的效用理论保证；同时构造计算效率更高的算法版本，在保持较强理论保证的前提下降低运行开销。

Result: 理论上，所提方法在基数约束和拟阵约束场景中均具有接近最优的效用保证。效率方面，论文声称其算法甚至快于已有的非隐私方法。实验方面，在真实数据集上，方法即使采用较强的隐私保护配置，所得目标效用仍可与非隐私基线相近；对于基数约束问题，执行时间获得显著改善。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了差分隐私结果多样化、尤其是MSD问题的研究空白，表明隐私保护不必以明显牺牲多样化质量或计算效率为代价。其贡献同时覆盖理论保证与实践性能，并使所提出的高效算法在非隐私场景中也具有应用价值。

Abstract: Result diversification is crucial for generating informative, non-redundant data summaries and query outputs. Although its various formulations have been extensively studied across an array of data-driven disciplines, existing methods fail to address the privacy concerns that arise when the underlying data is sensitive. In this work, we initiate the study of result diversification under differential privacy, focusing on the max-sum diversification (MSD) problem, a widely adopted model with the objective of maximizing a linear combination of a submodular function, quantifying relevance, and the sum of pairwise distances between selected items, quantifying diversity. We propose differentially private algorithms for MSD under both cardinality and matroid constraints, achieving nearly optimal utility guarantees. At the same time, we design more efficient algorithms that maintain strong guarantees. Notably, the proposed algorithms are faster than existing non-private methods, making them appealing even in non-private settings. Experimental evaluations on real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves utility comparable to that of non-private baselines even under strong privacy guarantees, and significantly improves execution times for cardinality constraints.

</details>


### [199] [SpexPay: A Privacy-Preserving Pay-As-You-Go System for Dynamic Spectrum Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17218)
*Mohaimin Al Barat,Hexuan Yu,Shaoyu Li,Yang Xiao,Yi Shi,Eric W. Burger,Y. Thomas Hou,Wenjing Lou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SpexPay提出一种面向动态频谱共享的隐私保护、可审计按量付费框架，通过匿名凭证、不可关联会话化名、选择性披露证明与智能合约托管结算，在隐藏用户身份的同时实现细粒度频谱使用计费和可验证审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有频谱接入系统依赖中心化管理方，可能暴露用户运营元数据，且交易过程缺乏密码学可追责性。随着动态频谱共享向开放化、大规模市场演进，需要一种既支持按实际使用量收费，又能保护用户身份与使用隐私，并提供可靠结算和审计能力的机制。

Method: 系统采用BBS+可验证凭证完成隐私化访问授权，使用不可关联的会话化名避免跨会话身份关联，并借助选择性披露证明仅公开必要授权属性。计量侧将化名化的使用证据与哈希链式计量数据记录至链上；结算侧通过Solidity智能合约执行自动化托管、支付与不可抵赖结算。作者还实现原型，并在Raspberry Pi 5上测试用户端密码学操作，以评估边缘设备适用性。

Result: 原型评估显示，系统端到端延迟约为150毫秒；单次链上操作成本约为603K gas，折合约0.9美元。该结果表明，在作者设定的实验环境和链上成本假设下，方案具有可部署性。通过仅上链化名证据和哈希链计量记录，系统在设计上兼顾了会话不可关联性、账单可验证性和审计能力。

Conclusion: 该工作将匿名凭证体系与智能合约结算结合，为动态频谱共享中的按量付费提供了隐私与问责兼顾的设计方案。其主要价值在于将身份隐私、细粒度计费、可审计使用证据和自动化结算统一到同一框架中。仍需进一步考察在高并发用户规模、长期链上存储、不同区块链手续费波动、计量数据真实性以及实际监管互操作要求下的性能与安全性。

Abstract: Dynamic Spectrum Sharing (DSS) is a cornerstone of next-generation wireless systems, yet existing solutions such as Spectrum Access Systems (SAS) rely on centralized administrators that expose sensitive operational metadata and lack cryptographic transaction accountability. Though SAS administrators, such as Google, have introduced pay-as-you-go pricing models, these approaches still face significant privacy and accountability challenges as DSS evolves toward a more open and large-scale spectrum marketplace. We present SpexPay, a privacy-preserving and auditable pay-as-you-go spectrum usage framework that enforces fine-grained, usage-linked payments without revealing user identities. Spexpay integrates BBS+ verifiable credentials, unlinkable session pseudonyms, and selective-disclosure proofs to enforce privacy-preserving access authorization, while leveraging Solidity-based smart contracts to realize automated and non-repudiable escrow settlement. By recording only pseudonymous usage evidence and hash-chained metering data on-chain, the system achieves strong unlinkability while preserving verifiable accountability and auditability. A full prototype demonstrates low end-to-end latency ($\approx$150 ms) and modest on-chain cost ($\approx$603K gas or $\approx$\$0.9), showing that SpexPay is practical for real-world DSS deployments. We also evaluated the user-side cryptographic operations on a Raspberry Pi 5 to assess scalability and suitability for edge-class hardware. Our code and artifacts are publicly available at https://github.com/iambarat/SpexPay.

</details>


### [200] [Between Safe Boundaries: Exploiting Temporal Consistency for Jailbreaking Text-To-Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17279)
*Xingkai Peng,Jun Jiang,Jiayang Liu,Kejiang Chen,Weiming Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出BSB，一种面向文本生成视频模型的结构化、低查询量越狱评估框架。其核心是将风险意图编码为两个单独无害的边界状态之间的转换，并通过文本代理空间中的蒙特卡洛树搜索发现潜在脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本生成视频模型越狱方法多由文本生成图像攻击迁移而来，未利用视频生成的时间一致性特征；同时，它们往往依赖大量视频查询和启发式局部搜索，不适合查询受限的黑盒评估场景。

Method: BSB将攻击目标定义为边界状态对：每个状态单独看似无害，但模型在生成二者之间的过渡时可能产生不安全中间帧。为避免直接在高成本视频空间枚举候选对，方法先在较低成本的文本代理空间中使用蒙特卡洛树搜索进行结构化探索，再以稀疏的视频级评估定期校准搜索结果。

Result: 在Veo 3.1、Sora 2、Seedance和Kling v1等商业文本生成视频模型上的实验表明，BSB优于所比较的既有基线方法；相对于最强竞争方法，其攻击成功率平均相对提升18.6%。

Conclusion: 时间一致性是文本生成视频模型中重要但此前研究不足的脆弱性来源。通过边界状态转换建模与结构化搜索，BSB能够在有限查询预算下更有效地发现模型安全漏洞。

Abstract: Recently, text-to-video (T2V) models have been widely deployed, sparking growing concerns over their robustness against jailbreak attacks. Existing jailbreak methods, mostly adapted from text-to-image attacks, suffer notable drawbacks when applied to T2V systems. They fail to fully leverage temporal consistency, an inherent characteristic of video generation. Besides, these methods demand heavy video query optimization, which is infeasible in practical black-box scenarios. Their adversarial prompt search is also driven by heuristic local signals, lacking principled structured exploration strategies. To tackle these limitations, we propose BSB, a structured, query-efficient jailbreak framework for T2V models. BSB harnesses temporal consistency by encoding harmful intent as the transition between two individually harmless boundary states. Under this paradigm, the attack targets boundary-state pairs whose interpolation tends to produce unsafe intermediate frames during video generation. Directly evaluating all candidate pairs within the video space incurs prohibitive computation cost. Instead, BSB conducts Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) in a cheaper textual proxy space and regularly calibrates search outcomes with sparse video-level evaluations. We conduct comprehensive experiments on mainstream commercial T2V models including Veo 3.1, Sora 2, Seedance and Kling v1. Results show BSB surpasses all existing jailbreak baselines, delivering an average 18.6% relative gain in attack success rate over the strongest competitor across evaluated models. Our findings identify temporal consistency as an understudied yet vital attack surface for T2V models and verify that structured search facilitates effective vulnerability discovery under constrained query budgets.

</details>


### [201] [Physical Time-Lock Puzzles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17328)
*Niloufar Sayadi,Chenglu Jin,Phuong Ha Nguyen,Zheng Yang,Marten van Dijk*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出物理时间锁谜题（P-TLP），利用硅硬件中物理不可克隆函数（PUF）的固有、受硬件限制的评估延迟，替代传统时间锁谜题依赖的软件顺序计算，从而提供更可预测且更长期的延迟保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间锁谜题的延迟基于顺序计算步数，而单步执行速度会随处理器、专用硬件和实现优化而提升，因此其实际保证更接近“步数锁”而非真实的“时间锁”。论文旨在建立一种将延迟直接绑定到物理硬件评估时间的机制，以降低长期硬件进步对延迟保证的不确定性。

Method: 作者将带噪声的PUF建模为不可并行化的延迟预言机，并在随机预言机模型下构造P-TLP。为应对PUF评估噪声，同时获得集中且可预测的总求解时间，论文采用由多个独立基础谜题组成的复合架构；证明顺序贪心求解策略最优，并使用Hoeffding界与Berry-Esseen界刻画总延迟的集中区间。此外，论文给出提前求解困难性证明，说明即使攻击者拥有大量经典计算资源，仍无法绕过PUF评估这一物理瓶颈。最后，作者基于可配置环形振荡器PUF实现FPGA原型，对理论延迟预测进行实验验证。

Result: 理论结果表明，P-TLP能够同时抵抗并行计算与算法级加速，并可通过复合结构将带噪声PUF造成的随机延迟收敛到较窄的预测窗口。实验结果显示，FPGA原型中的实际延迟测量值与分析模型预测较为一致，支持该方案在物理延迟可控性方面的主张。

Conclusion: 该工作将时间锁谜题的安全基础从软件计算步数转向硬件物理评估延迟，提出了具备形式化分析和原型验证的P-TLP框架。其主要价值在于提升长期延迟保证的可预测性；但实际部署仍依赖PUF的稳定性、环境鲁棒性、设备供应链可信度及攻击模型与真实硬件能力之间的一致性。

Abstract: Traditional time-lock puzzles enforce delayed access to encrypted secrets by relying on inherently sequential computational steps. However, since software-based constructions impose no fundamental bound on the physical execution speed of individual steps, they remain vulnerable to hardware acceleration and improved implementations. In practice, this renders existing schemes ``step-lock'' rather than true ``time-lock'' puzzles, making long-term delay guarantees highly speculative against decades of unpredictable hardware advancement. To address this fundamental limitation, we introduce the Physical Time-Lock Puzzle (P-TLP), a new paradigm that anchors solving delay directly to the intrinsic, hardware-bounded evaluation latency of silicon hardware. By leveraging noisy Physical Unclonable Functions (PUFs) as non-parallelizable delay oracles, P-TLPs establish resistance against both parallel computation and algorithmic acceleration under the random oracle model. To tolerate PUF evaluation noise while maintaining a tightly concentrated and predictable solving-delay window, we propose a composite puzzle architecture that combines multiple independent basic puzzles. We formally prove the optimality of a sequential greedy solving strategy and derive tightly concentrated solving-delay windows using Hoeffding and Berry-Esseen bounds. We additionally prove early-solve hardness, establishing that adversaries with substantial classical computation budgets cannot bypass the physically-enforced PUF evaluation bottleneck. We validate our theoretical analysis with an FPGA prototype built around a configurable Ring Oscillator PUF, confirming that our analytical delay predictions tightly match empirical measurements. Our results demonstrate that P-TLPs can deliver highly predictable, long-term delay guarantees, enabling practical deployment in high-value applications such as digital legacy management.
